######## 5. DemFunctionalGroupIdentifier

- **UUID**: `f12b2a29-843a-4a25-a60f-3377d9fb73d8`
- **参数名**: `DemFunctionalGroupIdentifier`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `51`
- **取值范围**: [51..51]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The identifier of the corresponding functional system group. 
Currently only support the identifier 0x33 for emissions-system group.


If OBD II is supported (DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport == true and DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation == DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_OBDII), 
this parameter is automatically derived from Dem/DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemObdDTC and shall not be configured manually.

If WWHOBD is supported (DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport == true and DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation == DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_WWHOBD), 
this parameter is automatically derived from Dem/DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemWWHOBDDTCClass and the Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemOBDFreezeFrameClassRef of the referenced event and shall not be configured manually.

If OBDonUDS is supported (DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport == true and DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation == DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_OBD_ON_UDS), 
this parameter is not derived and must be configured manually.

If ZEVonUDS is supported (DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport == true and DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation == DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_ZEV_ON_UDS), 
this parameter is not derived and must be configured manually.

######## 6. DemObdDTC3Byte

- **UUID**: `eeeec298-93dd-4a7d-9ea6-b91c74771f3d`
- **参数名**: `DemObdDTC3Byte`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [256..16777214]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter is only relevant for OBDonUDS.

3 Byte OBD DTC value that is used for the UDS Diagnostic Trouble Code (DTC). The value is defining a DTC based on SAE J2012.

This parameter is only considered when UDS and OBD DTC handling is separated (DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport == true, DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemSupportedObdUdsDtcSeparation == true and DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation == DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_OBD_ON_UDS).

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemImmediateNvStorage

- **UUID**: `d94dde56-7dba-46c0-9b50-2e5d26b7ceb8`
- **参数名**: `DemImmediateNvStorage`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Select the write strategy for non-volatile storage of DTC related data.

If enabled, environmental data for this DTC will be written to NV backing storage when it is stored or updated.
If disabled, environmental data for this DTC will be persisted during shutdown.

See also description of related parameters Dem/DemGeneral/DemImmediateNvStorageSupport and Dem/DemGeneral/DemImmediateNvStorageLimit.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemDTCSeverity

- **UUID**: `54869fee-446c-487f-a898-4f7dba9198b4`
- **参数名**: `DemDTCSeverity`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_DTC_SEV_NO_SEVERITY`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_DTC_SEV_CHECK_AT_NEXT_HALT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_DTC_SEV_IMMEDIATELY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_DTC_SEV_MAINTENANCE_ONLY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_DTC_SEV_NO_SEVERITY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: DTC severity
A missing configuration value (or parameter) is interpreted as NO_SEVERITY.

The DTCSeverity byte according to ISO 14229-1 and J1979-2 consists of DTC severity and DTC class information (refer to DemWWHOBDDTCClass or DemOBDONUDSDTCClass) and is reported by UDS service $19 09 and can serve as a filter parameter for UDS services $19 07, $19 08, $19 42.

This parameter has no further influence on the function of the Dem.

######## 2. DemWWHOBDDTCClass

- **UUID**: `e53d11cf-51d4-429c-bf41-6446647168ec`
- **参数名**: `DemWWHOBDDTCClass`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_DTC_WWHOBD_CLASS_A` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_DTC_WWHOBD_CLASS_B1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_DTC_WWHOBD_CLASS_B2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_DTC_WWHOBD_CLASS_C` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_DTC_WWHOBD_CLASS_NOCLASS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: CLASS_A: If a DTC with this class occur, it is assumed that the OBD threshold limits (OTL) are exceeded.
CLASS_B1: A DTC with this class has the potential to lead to emissions above the OBD threshold limits.
CLASS_B2: The malfunction is assumed to influence the emissions but not in a level exceeding the OBD threshold limits.
CLASS_C: The DTC is assumed to influence the emissions but not in a level above the regulated emission limits.
CLASS_NOCLASS: The DTC does not influence the emissions.

Together with DemDTCSeverity, the DTC class forms the DTC severity byte according to ISO 14229-1.

######## 3. DemOBDONUDSDTCClass

- **UUID**: `c9746d53-bba8-4bf0-a322-00773a09fcb4`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDONUDSDTCClass`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_DTC_OBDONUDS_CLASS_0` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_DTC_OBDONUDS_CLASS_1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_DTC_OBDONUDS_CLASS_2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_DTC_OBDONUDS_CLASS_3` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_DTC_OBDONUDS_CLASS_4` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter is only relevant for OBDonUDS and ZEVonUDS.
Together with DemDTCSeverity, the DTC class forms the DTC severity byte according to ISO 14229-1, J1979-2 and J1979-3.

OBDonUDS:
DTC Class to classify the emission severity of a DTC according to SAE J1979-2.


A DTC with Class 0 does not influence the emissions.

ZEVonUDS: 
DTCClass_1 (ZEV Propulsion related DTC) according to SAE J1979-3.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

####### 1. DemCallbackInitMForF

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `a506acbf-f3da-459d-83fc-9ef486570afa`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemCallbackInitMForF`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

The presence of this container sets up an initialization function (InitMonitorForFunction) for this DTC.
The Dem will call this function each time the related DTC is cleared, or the operation (monitoring) cycle of the related monitor restarts.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackInitMForFFnc'.

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Function name of an InitMonitorForFunction callout.

If this parameter exists, it must give a valid C-Function which is called instead of using an RTE port interface.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. DemDtrCompuDenominator0

- **UUID**: `68cfacf2-964a-43b2-8b7e-becaeddef042`
- **参数名**: `DemDtrCompuDenominator0`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..3.4028235E+38]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Part of the conversion between the binary representation and the physical meaning analogous to the SW-C Template conversion CompuRationalCoeffs with 2 numerator coefficients and 1 denominator coefficient in the direction compuInternalToPhys.

The value must be greater than 0.

In detail, the conversion of x (= old Value) to y (= new value) is given by

   y = (A*x + B)/C,

where
A = DemDtrCumpuNumerator1,
B = DemDtrCompuNumerator0,
C = DemDtrCompuDenominator0 (= this parameter).

########## 2. DemDtrCompuNumerator0

- **UUID**: `f1e06f88-1896-4f2b-8c8c-ce3aea392746`

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Part of the conversion between the binary representation and the physical meaning analogous to the SW-C Template conversion CompuRationalCoeffs with 2 numerator coefficients and 1 denominator coefficient in the direction compuInternalToPhys.

In detail, the conversion of x (= old Value) to y (= new value) is given by

   y = (A*x + B)/C,

where
A = DemDtrCumpuNumerator1,
B = DemDtrCompuNumerator0 (= this parameter),
C = DemDtrCompuDenominator0.

########## 3. DemDtrCompuNumerator1

- **参数名**: `DemDtrCompuNumerator1`

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. DemDtrUpdateKind

- **UUID**: `ca06d41c-c6b6-4605-bb22-3a94b3347334`
- **参数名**: `DemDtrUpdateKind`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_DTR_UPDATE_ALWAYS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_DTR_UPDATE_STEADY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Update conditions applied by the Dem to reports of DTR values.

DEM_DTR_UPDATE_ALWAYS: The reported DTR values are always processed.

DEM_DTR_UPDATE_STEADY: The reported DTR values are only processed, if the enable and storage conditions of the event referenced by DemDtrEventRef are fulfilled and the DTR values are consistent with the current event status of this event. This value can therefore only take effect if an event is referenced. If no event is referenced, the Dem behaves as if DemDtrUpdateKind is configured to DEM_DTR_UPDATE_ALWAYS.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. DemDtrCompuDenominator0

- **UUID**: `68cfacf2-964a-43b2-8b7e-becaeddef042`
- **参数名**: `DemDtrCompuDenominator0`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..3.4028235E+38]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Part of the conversion between the binary representation and the physical meaning analogous to the SW-C Template conversion CompuRationalCoeffs with 2 numerator coefficients and 1 denominator coefficient in the direction compuInternalToPhys.

The value must be greater than 0.

In detail, the conversion of x (= old Value) to y (= new value) is given by

   y = (A*x + B)/C,

where
A = DemDtrCumpuNumerator1,
B = DemDtrCompuNumerator0,
C = DemDtrCompuDenominator0 (= this parameter).

######## 2. DemDtrCompuNumerator0

- **UUID**: `f1e06f88-1896-4f2b-8c8c-ce3aea392746`

- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Part of the conversion between the binary representation and the physical meaning analogous to the SW-C Template conversion CompuRationalCoeffs with 2 numerator coefficients and 1 denominator coefficient in the direction compuInternalToPhys.

In detail, the conversion of x (= old Value) to y (= new value) is given by

   y = (A*x + B)/C,

where
A = DemDtrCumpuNumerator1,
B = DemDtrCompuNumerator0 (= this parameter),
C = DemDtrCompuDenominator0.

######## 3. DemDtrCompuNumerator1

- **UUID**: `03ab7c6a-3727-4909-aaae-a8e674ec4f8f`
- **参数名**: `DemDtrCompuNumerator1`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **取值范围**: [0..3.4028235E+38]

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Part of the conversion between the binary representation and the physical meaning analogous to the SW-C Template conversion CompuRationalCoeffs with 2 numerator coefficients and 1 denominator coefficient in the direction compuInternalToPhys.

The value must be greater than 0.

In detail, the conversion of x (= old Value) to y (= new value) is given by

where

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemDtrUpdateKind

- **UUID**: `ca06d41c-c6b6-4605-bb22-3a94b3347334`
- **参数名**: `DemDtrUpdateKind`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_DTR_UPDATE_ALWAYS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_DTR_UPDATE_STEADY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Update conditions applied by the Dem to reports of DTR values.

DEM_DTR_UPDATE_ALWAYS: The reported DTR values are always processed.

DEM_DTR_UPDATE_STEADY: The reported DTR values are only processed, if the enable and storage conditions of the event referenced by DemDtrEventRef are fulfilled and the DTR values are consistent with the current event status of this event. This value can therefore only take effect if an event is referenced. If no event is referenced, the Dem behaves as if DemDtrUpdateKind is configured to DEM_DTR_UPDATE_ALWAYS.

##### 6. DemEventParameter

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `45c6d99d-206d-4451-9fac-9dcbda5b9ab5`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemEventParameter`
- **多重性**: [1..65534]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the configuration of diagnostic events. These represent monitors and can be mapped onto a DTC.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemEventId

- **UUID**: `dc13b87d-9c8f-48d0-b82d-8b8d95f895be`
- **参数名**: `DemEventId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `65535`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Internal identifier of a diagnostic event.

This number is calculated internally and shall not be configured manually. This parameter is only available to publish the result of this calculation.
Applications using Dem APIs must not rely on the value of this parameter. Instead, they must use the symbolic constant (SymbolicNameValue) DemConf_DemEventParameter_{SHORT-NAME}.

######## 2. DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords

- **UUID**: `c8c2b479-91d5-4d0c-93a0-6759f382b3c6`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the number of snapshot records which can be stored for this event.

The parameter will be mandatory, if the snapshot numbers are CALCULATED (refer to DemTypeOfFreezeFrameRecordNumeration of DemPrimaryMemory or DemUserDefinedMemory referenced by the event) and a FreezeFrame is configured for this DemEventParameter (see DemFreezeFrameClassRef).

For configured record numbers this value has no meaning and should be removed.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemEventAvailable

- **UUID**: `019a11d9-ce3c-4552-84b8-9d7ad62167b8`
- **参数名**: `DemEventAvailable`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter has only an effect when /Dem/DemGeneral/DemPostbuild/DemEventAvailableDefaultSupport is enabled. 
If this parameter is set to true, the event will be considered as available during initialization. 
If this parameter is set to false, the event will be considered as unavailable during initialization

######## 2. DemEventAvailableInVariant

- **UUID**: `8178f211-fb1f-4744-8b91-927c678589a5`
- **参数名**: `DemEventAvailableInVariant`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter configures the event's availability in the variant. 
If this parameter is set to false for a specific variant, this event is not available at all in this variant. 
If this parameter is set to true, the event is available for the current variant.

With Postbuild-Selectable setting not supported, this parameter will be set to true and shall not be changed manually. 
If the parameter is changed to false, the event will not be available at all, like it is not configured.

If this parameter set to false, even though the switch DEM_CFG_SUPPORT_EVENTAVAILABLE (/Dem/DemGeneral/DemAvailabilitySupport) is enabled, changing the availability of events at runtime is not possible.

######## 3. DemEventLatchTestFailed

- **UUID**: `d08a5af9-a1f6-4ed1-8bdf-5e0dc6c9a68a`
- **参数名**: `DemEventLatchTestFailed`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Switch to block TestPassed results for a qualified event.

See DemGeneral/DemLatchTestFailedBehavior for the effect of this switch.

######## 4. DemEventCreateInfoPort

- **UUID**: `7e5e6a3c-02ac-4d2d-8338-b9e5bd8035ff`
- **参数名**: `DemEventCreateInfoPort`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Activate/deactivate the creation of the DiagnosticInfo port for the event.

If this parameter is enabled, the interface DiagnosticInfo is provided for the event.

######## 5. DemEventRecoverableInSameOperationCycle

- **UUID**: `adf3371a-f84a-4499-a488-2cb5480f3fc3`
- **参数名**: `DemEventRecoverableInSameOperationCycle`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..0]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Not supported. 
Please use the following parameters to configure the desired behavior

DemEventRecoverableInSameOperationCycle = TRUE:
DemEventLatchTestFailed = FALSE

DemEventRecoverableInSameOperationCycle = FALSE:
DemEventLatchTestFailed = TRUE
AND
DemGeneral/DemLatchTestFailedBehavior = DEM_LATCH_ONLY_THIS_CYCLE

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemEventKind

- **UUID**: `f84fdb62-b1ca-45b7-add8-3833a89e5481`
- **参数名**: `DemEventKind`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_EVENT_KIND_SWC`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_EVENT_KIND_BSW` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_EVENT_KIND_SWC` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter is used to distinguish between SW-C and BSW events.

SW-C events are reported through the RTE port interface DiagnosticMonitor, operation SetEventStatus.
BSW events are reported by C-API Dem_SetEventStatus.

####### 1. DemCallbackClearEventAllowed

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `8d0b8d02-d7dc-46db-8c41-c8d5630ef168`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemCallbackClearEventAllowed`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

The presence of this container sets up a callout function 'ClearEventAllowed' for this DTC. The Dem will call this function each time the related DTC is about to be cleared by an external tool. (API Dem_ClearDTC, usually only called by Dcm).

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackClearEventAllowedFnc'.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_NO_STATUS_BYTE_CHANGE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_ONLY_THIS_CYCLE_AND_READINESS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the resulting UDS status byte for the related event, which must not be cleared according to the ClearEventAllowed callback.
(not supported)

####### 2. DemCallbackEventDataChanged

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `4ce929e0-bbe7-482c-a839-8ef6789fcc9d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemCallbackEventDataChanged`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

The presence of this container sets up a callout function 'EventDataChanged' for this DTC. The Dem will call this function each time the stored data (extended record, snapshot record) has changed.

Please note, data elements can be mapped to internal data. This callback is NOT called if such data changes, only if the stored data record is modified due to a new event report.
Example: If an extended record is mapped to the fault detection counter, this API is not called each time the FDC changes even though a diagnostic service $19 06 would indeed return different data.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackEventDataChangedFnc'.
The presence of this container indicates that the Dem has access to an "EventDataChanged" callback.

This container is ignored, if parameter DemGeneral/DemTriggerEventDataChangedCallback exists and has value False.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Function name of a 'EventDataChanged' callout.

If this parameter exists, it must give a valid C-Function which is called instead of using an RTE port interface.
See DemGeneral/DemHeaderFileInclusion to specify a header file which declares this function.

####### 3. DemCallbackEventStatusChanged

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `8384ec0e-bb4c-4a7c-9b94-62407681b15e`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemCallbackEventStatusChanged`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

The presence of this container sets up a callout function 'EventStatusChanged' for this DTC. The Dem will call this function each time the event status changes.

Please note, the event status is independent of the DTC status availability mask (DemGeneral/DemDtcStatusAvailabilityMask). All transitions of status bits will be reported.
Attention: Due to preemption it is possible that event status changes are reported in the wrong order. This is no issue if the Dem_Mainfunction is mapped to a non-preemptive task.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackEventStatusChangedFnc'.

This container is ignored, if parameter DemGeneral/DemTriggerEventStatusChangedCallback exists and has value False.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

**容器描述**:

This container contains further configuration parameters for the event.

######## 参数配置

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold

- **UUID**: `e185a2d4-9d71-4434-b9d9-3a4835237bfa`
- **参数名**: `DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Number of aging cycles needed to unlearn/delete the event.

A value of 0 means, the event is aged immediately with a passed event status.
A value of 1 means the event is aged at the end of the current aging cycle (if the event is passive at that moment).
Larger values represent the n-th cycle e.g. 2 is the next, 3 the cycle after the next etc.

If this parameter is missing:
- If aging allowed is set to true (refer to DemAgingAllowed), this value defaults to 0.
- If aging allowed is set to false, this value defaults to 255.

If aging allowed is set to false the value is still used as upper limit for the aging counter, given that the aging counter behavior (refer to Dem/DemGeneral/DemAgingCounterBehavior) is set to DEM_AGING_COUNT_ALWAYS. In this case the value is also used as initial value for the inverted aging counter.
If the aging counter behavior is set to DEM_AGING_COUNT_ONLY_AGEABLE, this value is reported for the inverted aging counter, but has no effect for the incrementing aging counter (with aging allowed set to false).

########## 2. DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold

- **UUID**: `449ab15b-9d1b-4abc-a8e9-aa44aa78a214`
- **参数名**: `DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..254]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Number of operation cycles needed to confirm the event.

When the parameter is missing or has the value 0, use the standard UDS behavior of confirming the event (ConfirmedDTC status bit becomes set) with the first qualified FAILED result.
A value of 1 means the event becomes confirmed if it fails again in the next operation cycle.
Larger values represent confirmation in the n-th cycle e.g. 2 is confirmation in cycle after the next cycle, etc.

All DTCs, that are in the same MIL Group, must have the same threshold value (refer to parameter /Dem/DemGeneral/DemMILGroup).

########## 3. DemEventMemoryEntryFdcThresholdStorageValue

- **UUID**: `7b0f1c3f-c608-4c28-b0e5-047fa90e5089`
- **参数名**: `DemEventMemoryEntryFdcThresholdStorageValue`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..0]
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Threshold to allocate an event memory entry and to capture the Freeze Frame.
(Not supported - please use /DemConfigSet/DemDebounceTimeBasedClass/DemTimeBasedFdcThresholdStorageValue
or
DemConfigSet/DemDebounceCounterBasedClass/DemCounterBasedFdcThresholdStorageValue
instead.)

########## 4. DemEventPriority

- **UUID**: `d4d82b7d-eab1-47a8-a774-99f2358bc290`
- **参数名**: `DemEventPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Set the priority of the event.

A lower number represent higher priority. Same level priority is allowed.
This value influences the event displacement in case the respective memory is completely filled at the time of event confirmation.
Lower priority events will never displace higher priority events. Equal priority events will displace passive entries.

This parameter only has an effect if event displacement is supported (see DemGeneral/DemEventDisplacementSupport).

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. DemAgingAllowed

- **UUID**: `9210dacf-f225-496c-b200-2408f86318ea`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Switch to allow aging/unlearning of the event.

If aging is supported, a valid aging cycle must be referenced (see DemAgingCycleRef) and a valid number of aging cycles must be set (see DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold).

########## 3. DemFFPrestorageSupported

- **UUID**: `6c2a66d8-1b9b-43df-8cec-2d244e57db03`
- **参数名**: `DemFFPrestorageSupported`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Select the event for prestorage support.

Only events with this flag enabled may request data prestorage (Port interface DiagnosticMonitor, operation PrestoreFreezeFrame).

This parameter only has an effect if prestorage is enabled (see DemGeneral/DemMaxNumberPrestoredFF).

########## 4. DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification

- **UUID**: `d376fec8-70f5-4d79-8511-9ddca9bc6263`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines when the ConfirmedDTC and the WarningIndicatorRequested status bits of an OBD relevant event become externally visible. Depending on the configuration, permanent DTC and the OBD FreezeFrame of the event are also only externally visible after the related status bit becomes externally visible.
If this parameter is enabled, the two status bits are not visible before the OBD driving cycle is qualified.
If this parameter is disabled, the two status bits are visible without the need for a qualified driving cycle.

If this parameter is missing, the Dem will handle the visibility of the two status bits according to the settings of Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold:
If DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold is 0, the value of DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification is assumed as disabled.
If DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold is > 0, the value of DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification is assumed as enabled.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_NOXCAT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_OTHER` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_O2SENS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_O2SENSHT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_PCV` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_PMFLT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_SECAIR` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_VVT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_EGHSENS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_CSAS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Select the Event OBD Readiness group.

This parameter is only needed if OBD support is enabled (see DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport).
The parameter is needed for PID $01, PID $F501, PID $41, J1939 diagnostic readiness 1 and 3 computation and to classify faults according to OBD requirements (e.g. OBD freeze frame related to misfire or fuel system faults can displace other freeze frames).

The different values for this parameter are described in the Dem AUTOSAR SWS. In addition to the Readiness groups described there, the following groups are supported for spark and compression engines:
- DEM_OBD_RDY_EGHSENS: Exhaust gas sensor heater monitoring
- DEM_OBD_RDY_CSAS: Cold start aid system monitoring.

########## 2. DemEventSignificance

- **UUID**: `7308212f-0bc9-4f5b-86f8-2222fb3c585f`
- **参数名**: `DemEventSignificance`

- **描述**: Significance of the event, which indicates additional information concerning fault classification and resolution.

This value can optionally be selected to be reported as event data (e.g. as data element inside an extended data record).
It has no further effect on the Dem.

########## 1. DemEnableConditionGroupRef

- **UUID**: `c2dba015-6467-425b-864d-7948e79d71f8`
- **引用名**: `DemEnableConditionGroupRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `DemEnableConditionGroup`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Dem/DemGeneral/DemEnableConditionGroup`

- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: References an enable condition group.

An event status report ((Pre)Failed or (Pre)Passed) will only have an effect if all EnableConditions belonging to the referenced group are met at the time of report. Otherwise the result will be discarded.

########## 2. DemEventFailureCycleRef

- **UUID**: `99e39a6e-030d-4ebb-a5c5-eb907f009f12`
- **引用名**: `DemEventFailureCycleRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `DemOperationCycle`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Dem/DemGeneral/DemOperationCycle`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC V1.0.0
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Select the cycle which is used as base of counting failures of this event.

This must be the same value as the operation cycle of the event (see DemOperationCycleRef).
Therefore this parameter value here is always ignored and (internally) substituted by the DemOperationCycleRef value. You can safely delete this parameter - it has no impact on your configuration.

########## 3. DemOperationCycleRef

- **UUID**: `eb714d52-d82f-490d-9fa7-1ed2cffe1ede`
- **引用名**: `DemOperationCycleRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **目标**: `DemOperationCycle`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Dem/DemGeneral/DemOperationCycle`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC V1.0.0
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Select the operation/monitoring cycle for the event.

This cycle is used:
 - to reset the NotTestedThisCycle and FailedThisCycle bits of the event status,
 - as the basis of event confirmation in case multiple trips are required (see DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold),
 - as the basis for healing of the WarningIndicatorRequests bit in case multiple passed cycles are required (e.g. to disable the MIL).

########## 4. DemStorageConditionGroupRef

- **UUID**: `34ee9c50-0ac1-4598-bf9c-c70be8bba959`
- **引用名**: `DemStorageConditionGroupRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `DemStorageConditionGroup`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Dem/DemGeneral/DemStorageConditionGroup`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC V1.0.0
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: References a storage condition group.

An event status report will not result in an event entry to be created or updated unless all StorageConditions belonging to the referenced group are met at the time of report.
This means PendingDTC or ConfirmedDTC will not be set, and Indicators will not be enabled.
Already stored events can still stop to age and heal, and the remaining status bits are modified if needed.

####### 7. DemDebounceCounterBased

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `a3cd5d3d-3f7f-4641-9330-1bc37ff8750b`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDebounceCounterBased`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Counter based debouncing will increment a counter with each Prefailed result, and decrement the counter with each Prepassed result.
A qualified 'Passed' or 'Failed' result will be generated when a given threshold value is reached.

This debouncing type is ideal for cyclic monitors that generate a monitoring result regularly since it involves little overhead.

The debounce value is mapped to a faultdetection count (FDC) as such:
If the Failed threshould is reached, FDC is 127.
If the DebounceCounter is 0, FDC is 0.
If the Passed threshold is reached, FDC is -128.
Values between 0 and the threshold values are linearly mapped to 1..126 respectively -1..-127.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

- **UUID**: `98d456f6-a83d-4cf3-8276-1ba3d43e6130`
- **引用名**: `DemDebounceCounterBasedClassRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE

- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Dem/DemConfigSet/DemDebounceCounterBasedClass`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This reference selects the DemDebounceCounterBasedClass applied for the
debouncing of the DemEventParameter.

####### 8. DemDebounceMonitorInternal

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `5eb9ae34-0ec8-4eba-a682-38442523bf19`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDebounceMonitorInternal`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Monitor internal debouncing is used for monitors that implement the debouncing themselves, and only report qualified results (PASSED/FAILED) to the Dem.

In order to implement the diagnostic services that report the fault detection counter, a callout function is required.

######## 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

######### 1. DemCallbackGetFDC

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `a19cf877-d405-47de-b578-58268ab4493c`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemCallbackGetFDC`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container sets up the callout function 'DemCallbackGetFDC' for this Event. The Dem will call this function each time the value of the fault detection counter is needed, e.g. requested by the Dcm.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackGetFDCFnc'.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

########### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

############ 1. DemCallbackGetFDCFnc

- **UUID**: `8677b07f-c109-4c0f-a878-a3027649057e`
- **参数名**: `DemCallbackGetFDCFnc`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Function name of a 'DemCallbackGetFDC' callout.

If this parameter exists, it must give a valid C-Function which is called instead of using an RTE port interface.
See DemGeneral/DemHeaderFileInclusion to specify a header file which declares this function.

####### 9. DemCallbackGetFDC

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `a19cf877-d405-47de-b578-58268ab4493c`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemCallbackGetFDC`
- **多重性**: [0..1]

- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container sets up the callout function 'DemCallbackGetFDC' for this Event. The Dem will call this function each time the value of the fault detection counter is needed, e.g. requested by the Dcm.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackGetFDCFnc'.

####### 10. DemDebounceTimeBased

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bc30101f-b22d-4931-9e37-03f45638a955`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDebounceTimeBased`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Time based debouncing will use a timer started in either passed or failed direction with an unqualified result.
The timer is not restarted in case it is already running, unless a direction change is detected - e.g. after a PrePassed result the next PrePassed result will not restart the timer, a following PreFailed will stop the passed timer and start the failed timer.

Timer supervision incurs a substantial runtime and RAM overhead compared to counter based denouncing and should be used sparingly.

The debounce value is mapped to a faultdetection count as such:
If the Failed time is reached, FDC is 127.
If no timer is running (and no qualified result has been reached), FDC is 0.
If the Passed time is reached, FDC is -128.
If the timer is currently running in either direction, the timer is linearly mapped to 1..126 respectively -1..-127.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. DemDebounceTimeBasedClassRef

- **UUID**: `a560970d-ad70-4dbd-be91-1180035dd1eb`
- **引用名**: `DemDebounceTimeBasedClassRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **目标**: `DemDebounceTimeBasedClass`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Dem/DemConfigSet/DemDebounceTimeBasedClass`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This reference selects the DemDebounceTimeBasedClass applied for the debouncing
of the DemEventParameter.

####### 11. DemDebounceAlgorithmClass

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `6111de60-51d3-4846-9870-b598a5bdcbae`
- **容器类型**: CHOICE-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDebounceAlgorithmClass`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器选择**:

- **DemDebounceCounterBased**
  - UUID: `a3cd5d3d-3f7f-4641-9330-1bc37ff8750b`
  - 描述: Counter based debouncing will increment a counter with each Prefailed result, and decrement the counter with each Prepassed result.
A qualified 'Passed' or 'Failed' result will be generated when a given threshold value is reached.

This debouncing type is ideal for cyclic monitors that generate a monitoring result regularly since it involves little overhead.

The debounce value is mapped to a faultdetection count (FDC) as such:
If the Failed threshould is reached, FDC is 127.
If the DebounceCounter is 0, FDC is 0.
If the Passed threshold is reached, FDC is -128.
Values between 0 and the threshold values are linearly mapped to 1..126 respectively -1..-127.
- **DemDebounceMonitorInternal**
  - UUID: `5eb9ae34-0ec8-4eba-a682-38442523bf19`
  - 描述: Monitor internal debouncing is used for monitors that implement the debouncing themselves, and only report qualified results (PASSED/FAILED) to the Dem.

In order to implement the diagnostic services that report the fault detection counter, a callout function is required.
- **DemCallbackGetFDC**
  - UUID: `a19cf877-d405-47de-b578-58268ab4493c`
  - 描述: This container sets up the callout function 'DemCallbackGetFDC' for this Event. The Dem will call this function each time the value of the fault detection counter is needed, e.g. requested by the Dcm.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackGetFDCFnc'.
- **DemDebounceTimeBased**
  - UUID: `bc30101f-b22d-4931-9e37-03f45638a955`
  - 描述: Time based debouncing will use a timer started in either passed or failed direction with an unqualified result.
The timer is not restarted in case it is already running, unless a direction change is detected - e.g. after a PrePassed result the next PrePassed result will not restart the timer, a following PreFailed will stop the passed timer and start the failed timer.

Timer supervision incurs a substantial runtime and RAM overhead compared to counter based denouncing and should be used sparingly.

The debounce value is mapped to a faultdetection count as such:
If the Failed time is reached, FDC is 127.
If no timer is running (and no qualified result has been reached), FDC is 0.
If the Passed time is reached, FDC is -128.
If the timer is currently running in either direction, the timer is linearly mapped to 1..126 respectively -1..-127.

**容器描述**:

Select the debouncing algorithm used for the event.

##### 8. DemCallbackEventDataChanged

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `4ce929e0-bbe7-482c-a839-8ef6789fcc9d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemCallbackEventDataChanged`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

The presence of this container sets up a callout function 'EventDataChanged' for this DTC. The Dem will call this function each time the stored data (extended record, snapshot record) has changed.

Please note, data elements can be mapped to internal data. This callback is NOT called if such data changes, only if the stored data record is modified due to a new event report.
Example: If an extended record is mapped to the fault detection counter, this API is not called each time the FDC changes even though a diagnostic service $19 06 would indeed return different data.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackEventDataChangedFnc'.
The presence of this container indicates that the Dem has access to an "EventDataChanged" callback.

This container is ignored, if parameter DemGeneral/DemTriggerEventDataChangedCallback exists and has value False.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Function name of a 'EventDataChanged' callout.

If this parameter exists, it must give a valid C-Function which is called instead of using an RTE port interface.

##### 9. DemCallbackEventStatusChanged

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `8384ec0e-bb4c-4a7c-9b94-62407681b15e`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemCallbackEventStatusChanged`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

The presence of this container sets up a callout function 'EventStatusChanged' for this DTC. The Dem will call this function each time the event status changes.

Please note, the event status is independent of the DTC status availability mask (DemGeneral/DemDtcStatusAvailabilityMask). All transitions of status bits will be reported.
Attention: Due to preemption it is possible that event status changes are reported in the wrong order. This is no issue if the Dem_Mainfunction is mapped to a non-preemptive task.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackEventStatusChangedFnc'.

This container is ignored, if parameter DemGeneral/DemTriggerEventStatusChangedCallback exists and has value False.

##### 11. DemEventClass

**容器属性**:

- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemEventClass`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains further configuration parameters for the event.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **10** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold

- **UUID**: `e185a2d4-9d71-4434-b9d9-3a4835237bfa`
- **参数名**: `DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Number of aging cycles needed to unlearn/delete the event.

A value of 0 means, the event is aged immediately with a passed event status.
A value of 1 means the event is aged at the end of the current aging cycle (if the event is passive at that moment).
Larger values represent the n-th cycle e.g. 2 is the next, 3 the cycle after the next etc.

If this parameter is missing:
- If aging allowed is set to true (refer to DemAgingAllowed), this value defaults to 0.
- If aging allowed is set to false, this value defaults to 255.

If aging allowed is set to false the value is still used as upper limit for the aging counter, given that the aging counter behavior (refer to Dem/DemGeneral/DemAgingCounterBehavior) is set to DEM_AGING_COUNT_ALWAYS. In this case the value is also used as initial value for the inverted aging counter.
If the aging counter behavior is set to DEM_AGING_COUNT_ONLY_AGEABLE, this value is reported for the inverted aging counter, but has no effect for the incrementing aging counter (with aging allowed set to false).

######## 2. DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold

- **UUID**: `449ab15b-9d1b-4abc-a8e9-aa44aa78a214`
- **参数名**: `DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..254]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Number of operation cycles needed to confirm the event.

When the parameter is missing or has the value 0, use the standard UDS behavior of confirming the event (ConfirmedDTC status bit becomes set) with the first qualified FAILED result.
A value of 1 means the event becomes confirmed if it fails again in the next operation cycle.
Larger values represent confirmation in the n-th cycle e.g. 2 is confirmation in cycle after the next cycle, etc.

All DTCs, that are in the same MIL Group, must have the same threshold value (refer to parameter /Dem/DemGeneral/DemMILGroup).

######## 3. DemEventMemoryEntryFdcThresholdStorageValue

- **UUID**: `7b0f1c3f-c608-4c28-b0e5-047fa90e5089`
- **参数名**: `DemEventMemoryEntryFdcThresholdStorageValue`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..0]
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Threshold to allocate an event memory entry and to capture the Freeze Frame.
(Not supported - please use /DemConfigSet/DemDebounceTimeBasedClass/DemTimeBasedFdcThresholdStorageValue
or
DemConfigSet/DemDebounceCounterBasedClass/DemCounterBasedFdcThresholdStorageValue
instead.)

######## 4. DemEventPriority

- **UUID**: `d4d82b7d-eab1-47a8-a774-99f2358bc290`
- **参数名**: `DemEventPriority`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Set the priority of the event.

A lower number represent higher priority. Same level priority is allowed.
This value influences the event displacement in case the respective memory is completely filled at the time of event confirmation.
Lower priority events will never displace higher priority events. Equal priority events will displace passive entries.

This parameter only has an effect if event displacement is supported (see DemGeneral/DemEventDisplacementSupport).

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

If aging is supported, a valid aging cycle must be referenced (see DemAgingCycleRef) and a valid number of aging cycles must be set (see DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold).

######## 4. DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification

- **UUID**: `d376fec8-70f5-4d79-8511-9ddca9bc6263`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines when the ConfirmedDTC and the WarningIndicatorRequested status bits of an OBD relevant event become externally visible. Depending on the configuration, permanent DTC and the OBD FreezeFrame of the event are also only externally visible after the related status bit becomes externally visible.
If this parameter is enabled, the two status bits are not visible before the OBD driving cycle is qualified.
If this parameter is disabled, the two status bits are visible without the need for a qualified driving cycle.

If this parameter is missing, the Dem will handle the visibility of the two status bits according to the settings of Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold:
If DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold is 0, the value of DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification is assumed as disabled.
If DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold is > 0, the value of DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification is assumed as enabled.

  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_HCCAT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)

  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_MISF` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_NONE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_NOXADSORB` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_NOXCAT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_OTHER` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_O2SENS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_O2SENSHT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_PCV` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_PMFLT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_SECAIR` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_VVT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_EGHSENS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_RDY_CSAS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Select the Event OBD Readiness group.

This parameter is only needed if OBD support is enabled (see DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport).
The parameter is needed for PID $01, PID $F501, PID $41, J1939 diagnostic readiness 1 and 3 computation and to classify faults according to OBD requirements (e.g. OBD freeze frame related to misfire or fuel system faults can displace other freeze frames).

The different values for this parameter are described in the Dem AUTOSAR SWS. In addition to the Readiness groups described there, the following groups are supported for spark and compression engines:
- DEM_OBD_RDY_EGHSENS: Exhaust gas sensor heater monitoring
- DEM_OBD_RDY_CSAS: Cold start aid system monitoring.

######## 2. DemEventSignificance

- **UUID**: `7308212f-0bc9-4f5b-86f8-2222fb3c585f`
- **参数名**: `DemEventSignificance`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_EVENT_SIGNIFICANCE_FAULT`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Significance of the event, which indicates additional information concerning fault classification and resolution.

This value can optionally be selected to be reported as event data (e.g. as data element inside an extended data record).
It has no further effect on the Dem.

- **描述**: References an enable condition group.

An event status report ((Pre)Failed or (Pre)Passed) will only have an effect if all EnableConditions belonging to the referenced group are met at the time of report. Otherwise the result will be discarded.

######## 2. DemEventFailureCycleRef

- **UUID**: `99e39a6e-030d-4ebb-a5c5-eb907f009f12`
- **引用名**: `DemEventFailureCycleRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `DemOperationCycle`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Dem/DemGeneral/DemOperationCycle`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC V1.0.0
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Select the cycle which is used as base of counting failures of this event.

This must be the same value as the operation cycle of the event (see DemOperationCycleRef).
Therefore this parameter value here is always ignored and (internally) substituted by the DemOperationCycleRef value. You can safely delete this parameter - it has no impact on your configuration.

######## 3. DemOperationCycleRef

- **UUID**: `eb714d52-d82f-490d-9fa7-1ed2cffe1ede`
- **引用名**: `DemOperationCycleRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **目标**: `DemOperationCycle`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Dem/DemGeneral/DemOperationCycle`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC V1.0.0
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Select the operation/monitoring cycle for the event.

This cycle is used:
 - to reset the NotTestedThisCycle and FailedThisCycle bits of the event status,
 - as the basis of event confirmation in case multiple trips are required (see DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold),
 - as the basis for healing of the WarningIndicatorRequests bit in case multiple passed cycles are required (e.g. to disable the MIL).

######## 4. DemStorageConditionGroupRef

- **UUID**: `34ee9c50-0ac1-4598-bf9c-c70be8bba959`
- **引用名**: `DemStorageConditionGroupRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `DemStorageConditionGroup`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Dem/DemGeneral/DemStorageConditionGroup`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC V1.0.0
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: References a storage condition group.

An event status report will not result in an event entry to be created or updated unless all StorageConditions belonging to the referenced group are met at the time of report.
This means PendingDTC or ConfirmedDTC will not be set, and Indicators will not be enabled.
Already stored events can still stop to age and heal, and the remaining status bits are modified if needed.

##### 13. DemDebounceCounterBased

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `a3cd5d3d-3f7f-4641-9330-1bc37ff8750b`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDebounceCounterBased`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Counter based debouncing will increment a counter with each Prefailed result, and decrement the counter with each Prepassed result.
A qualified 'Passed' or 'Failed' result will be generated when a given threshold value is reached.

This debouncing type is ideal for cyclic monitors that generate a monitoring result regularly since it involves little overhead.

The debounce value is mapped to a faultdetection count (FDC) as such:
If the Failed threshould is reached, FDC is 127.
If the DebounceCounter is 0, FDC is 0.
If the Passed threshold is reached, FDC is -128.
Values between 0 and the threshold values are linearly mapped to 1..126 respectively -1..-127.

###### 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

####### REFERENCE 类型引用

######## 1. DemDebounceCounterBasedClassRef

- **UUID**: `98d456f6-a83d-4cf3-8276-1ba3d43e6130`
- **引用名**: `DemDebounceCounterBasedClassRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE

- **目标**: `DemDebounceCounterBasedClass`

##### 14. DemDebounceMonitorInternal

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `5eb9ae34-0ec8-4eba-a682-38442523bf19`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDebounceMonitorInternal`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Monitor internal debouncing is used for monitors that implement the debouncing themselves, and only report qualified results (PASSED/FAILED) to the Dem.

In order to implement the diagnostic services that report the fault detection counter, a callout function is required.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container sets up the callout function 'DemCallbackGetFDC' for this Event. The Dem will call this function each time the value of the fault detection counter is needed, e.g. requested by the Dcm.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackGetFDCFnc'.

If this parameter exists, it must give a valid C-Function which is called instead of using an RTE port interface.

##### 16. DemDebounceTimeBased

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `bc30101f-b22d-4931-9e37-03f45638a955`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDebounceTimeBased`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Time based debouncing will use a timer started in either passed or failed direction with an unqualified result.
The timer is not restarted in case it is already running, unless a direction change is detected - e.g. after a PrePassed result the next PrePassed result will not restart the timer, a following PreFailed will stop the passed timer and start the failed timer.

Timer supervision incurs a substantial runtime and RAM overhead compared to counter based denouncing and should be used sparingly.

The debounce value is mapped to a faultdetection count as such:
If the Failed time is reached, FDC is 127.
If no timer is running (and no qualified result has been reached), FDC is 0.
If the Passed time is reached, FDC is -128.
If the timer is currently running in either direction, the timer is linearly mapped to 1..126 respectively -1..-127.

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

- **作用域**: LOCAL

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This reference selects the DemDebounceTimeBasedClass applied for the debouncing
of the DemEventParameter.

######## 3. DemDebounceCounterFailedThreshold

######## 4. DemDebounceCounterPassedThreshold

- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If a PREPASSED monitor result decrements the debounce counter to (or below) this value, a qualified PASSED result is generated.

######## 5. DemDebounceCounterJumpDownValue

- **UUID**: `68abecbc-264b-49a3-97d4-6bd653c66f0a`

- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **取值范围**: [-32768..32767]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This is the debounce counter value set if a 'JumpDown' is triggered.

This parameter is used only if DemDebounceCounterJumpDown is enabled.

######## 6. DemCounterBasedFdcThresholdStorageValue

- **UUID**: `7cfd42fa-7110-4f6e-bfaf-4cfa5fc21f69`
- **参数名**: `DemCounterBasedFdcThresholdStorageValue`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `127`
- **取值范围**: [1..32767]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If a PREFAILED monitor result increments the debounce counter to (or above) this value, a FDC TRIP result is generated.

This value has to be less or equal to DemDebounceCounterFailedThreshold.

This parameter is only relevant if data is stored at a time where the DTC fault detection counter passes a threshold lower than the failed threshold, or if parameter Dem/DemGeneral/DemSupportExtendedFiM is available and enabled.

######## 7. DemDebounceCounterJumpUpValue

- **UUID**: `d9fc3a7e-4030-4f4e-8d75-37738df93dae`
- **参数名**: `DemDebounceCounterJumpUpValue`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [-32768..32767]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This is the debounce counter value which is set if a 'JumpUp' is triggered.
Usually this is set to 0 as shown in ISO14229.

This parameter is used only if DemDebounceCounterJumpUp is enabled.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemDebounceCounterJumpUp

- **UUID**: `996f6e5e-0d0e-467a-95c9-58c281c030f8`
- **参数名**: `DemDebounceCounterJumpUp`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Select the jump up behavior.

If enabled, the debounce counter is set to the value of DemDebounceCounterJumpUpValue before incrementing, unless the counter already is larger.
This can be used to achieve constant times for a qualified FAILED result.

######## 2. DemDebounceCounterJumpDown

- **UUID**: `d9f760ba-4b5f-4297-a83b-ef41a850ec5c`
- **参数名**: `DemDebounceCounterJumpDown`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Select the jump down behavior.

If enabled, the debounce counter is set to the value of DemDebounceCounterJumpDownValue before decrementing, unless the counter already is smaller.
This can be used to achieve constant times for a qualified PASSED result.

######## 3. DemDebounceCounterStorage

######## 4. DemDebounceContinuous

- **UUID**: `284574f7-178e-4432-a085-709f10be96fd`
- **参数名**: `DemDebounceContinuous`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the event debounce algorithm will continue counting while enable conditions are not fulfilled / ControlDTCSetting is disabled.
To prevent the event from qualifying after the enable conditions become enabled / ControlDTCSetting is enabled again, it is recommended to set DemDebounceBehavior to DEM_DEBOUNCE_RESET if this option is used.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemDebounceBehavior

- **参数名**: `DemDebounceBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_DEBOUNCE_FREEZE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_DEBOUNCE_FREEZE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_DEBOUNCE_RESET` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines how the event debounce algorithm will behave, if a related enable condition is not fulfilled or ControlDTCSetting of the related event is disabled.

FREEZE: The FDC will continue at the current value when enable conditions / ControlDTCSetting allow event reports again.
RESET: The FDC will revert to 0. See also (DemGeneral/DemResetDebounceBehavior)

##### 23. DemDebounceTimeBasedClass

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `a2f33e7d-f7c3-4a7b-84c7-c81461e712a9`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDebounceTimeBasedClass`
- **多重性**: [0..65535]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Inside this container the parameters for the debouncing of time based debouncing are configured.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemDebounceContinuous

- **UUID**: `68490467-0e1c-4b3e-bc16-810383de0d85`
- **参数名**: `DemDebounceContinuous`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines whether the event debounce algorithm will continue counting while enable conditions are not fulfilled / ControlDTCSetting is disabled.
To prevent the event from qualifying after the enable conditions become enabled / ControlDTCSetting is enabled again, it is recommended to set DemDebounceBehavior to DEM_DEBOUNCE_RESET if this option is used.
If DemDebounceBehavior is set to DEM_DEBOUNCE_FREEZE, an active debounce timer is paused, but the timer can be re-started while enable conditions are inactive.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. DemDebounceTimeFailedThreshold

- **UUID**: `a0d0e1b6-5680-4dc3-9ea6-682843d41653`
- **参数名**: `DemDebounceTimeFailedThreshold`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [0.001..3600]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Time after a PreFailed report, after which a qualified FAILED result is generated.

The AUTOSAR configuration standard is to use SI units, so in the EcuC file this parameter is stored as float value in seconds - the DaVinci Configurator 5 GUI will convert the value when using a diffenent unit.

######## 2. DemDebounceTimePassedThreshold

- **UUID**: `74e0d2b7-462a-40f1-a0e0-fe75ebdc6c32`
- **参数名**: `DemDebounceTimePassedThreshold`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [0.001..3600]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Time after a PrePassed report, after which a qualified PASSED result is generated.

The AUTOSAR configuration standard is to use SI units, so in the EcuC file this parameter is stored as float value in seconds - the DaVinci Configurator 5 GUI will convert the value when using a diffenent unit.

######## 3. DemTimeBasedFdcThresholdStorageValue

- **UUID**: `dae9569d-d885-4774-8b74-a020f87bfeeb`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeBasedFdcThresholdStorageValue`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0.001..3600]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If a monitor fails for this time, a FDC TRIP result is generated.

This value has to be less than or equal to DemDebounceTimeFailedThreshold.

This parameter is only relevant if data is stored at a time where the DTC fault detection counter passes a threshold lower than the failed threshold, or if parameter Dem/DemGeneral/DemSupportExtendedFiM is available and enabled.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemDebounceBehavior

- **UUID**: `228d2412-1a4b-4d91-8893-aeb2c55fec8d`
- **参数名**: `DemDebounceBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_DEBOUNCE_FREEZE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_DEBOUNCE_FREEZE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_DEBOUNCE_RESET` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines how the event debounce algorithm will behave, if a related enable condition is not fulfilled or ControlDTCSetting of the related event is disabled.

FREEZE: The debounce timer will pause while enable conditions are not active. To continue, report prepassed/prefailed after the enableconditions are fulfilled / ControlDTCSetting is enabled
RESET: The FDC will revert to 0. See also (DemGeneral/DemResetDebounceBehavior)

##### 24. DemGeneral

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `48e2a922-ce7c-4cf2-bed9-a176b17a982b`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemGeneral`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container groups the constant configuration options, e.g. options that can not have different values in multiple configurations.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **101** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemBswErrorBufferSize

- **UUID**: `856ab3be-6615-4dc3-bc9d-aa095f450fe8`
- **参数名**: `DemBswErrorBufferSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Dem processing queue size.
(This parameter is no longer supported. The number of events, which can be queued, is not limited to this parameter value.)

The Dem will process qualified events (Failed/Passed) during the next task cycle. This value specifies how many events can be queued.
When this queue overflows you will not lose reports, but the surplus events are not processed in reporting order. Also run-time is greately increased as surplus events need to be searched for the pending event change.

A sufficient queue size can not be deduced from the Dem configuration, you need to analyze how your system reports events:
set the size to the maximum amount of monitors that may report a qualified result (e.g. finish debouncing) during a single task cycle of the Dem.

With queue overflow the run time of the Dem_MainFunction can spike, so you need to verify its run-time does not exceed your system limit (e.g. causes a task overflow) when the maximum amount of your monitors qualify at the same time.

######## 2. DemDtcStatusAvailabilityMask

- **UUID**: `95499469-08ed-40b4-a63d-62e66919c2da`
- **参数名**: `DemDtcStatusAvailabilityMask`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `255`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Mask for the supported DTC status bits of the Dem. This mask is used by UDS service 0x19.

Internally, all status bits are supported as required by the Dem specification. This internal status byte is used e.g. for internal event notification callout functions.
Externally, all status bits are ANDed with this mask, e.g. for diagnostic services like 0x19 or DTC status change notifications.
This mask is also reported as status availability mask in the respective 0x19 subfunctions.

######## 3. DemImmediateNvStorageLimit

- **UUID**: `cc8c4b2b-3f31-4d79-af01-4925a81788d8`
- **参数名**: `DemImmediateNvStorageLimit`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines a limit for the number of immediate write accesses to the non-volatile memory.
This option is only relevant if immediate NV storage is used for DTCs (see DemGeneral/DemImmediateNvStorageSupport and DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemImmediateNvStorage).

If configured the non-volatile immediate storage for a DTC is only triggered until the OccurenceCounter reaches the configured value.
Note that the number of immediate write accesses for a DTC can be higher than the configured value (for detailed description see Technical Reference).

######## 4. DemMaxNumberEventEntryMirror

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE

The mirror memory is currently not supported, so this value should be set to 0.

######## 5. DemMaxNumberEventEntryOBDFreezeFrame

######## 9. DemCompiledConfigId

- **UUID**: `0a27356b-b757-43f8-a94d-52caf6a2eb91`
- **参数名**: `DemCompiledConfigId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL

- **符号名值**: false

- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Change this value to force the Dem to clear all NV data on next initialization (startup).

This is useful if the flash eeprom emulation is not cleared during flashing of a new software version.
When this parameter is missing, the value 0 is assumed.

######## 10. DemCompiledPostbuildCrc

- **UUID**: `1dc756fc-261a-46cc-ac75-ebeaf06df55c`
- **参数名**: `DemCompiledPostbuildCrc`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter contains a variant specific checksum (CRC) over all postbuiild-changeable configuration items. This value is internally used to detect changes in the configuration.

The non-volatile stored data is always configuration specific. Reading the NvData with a different configuration will give a new interpretation to that binary data, that is usually wrong. So with configuration changes the DEM shall typically discard the previous NvData.

In mode AUTOMATIC (see parameter DemCompiledPostbuildCrcMode) the DaVinci Configurator5 will automatically evaluate a checksum over the relevant data, that will differ between incompatible configurations.

In mode MANUAL the user himself is responsible to use different values for incompatible configurations.

Generally: a value here, that is different from the previous value (that was used when storing the non-volatile data) will cause the DEM to discard the previously stored NvData.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemAgingRequieresTestedCycle
