(Not supported, please use DemGeneral/DemAgingBehavior instead)

######## 2. DemAvailabilityStorage

- **UUID**: `8d78a35c-e118-4bb9-a6ff-832960853b71`
- **参数名**: `DemAvailabilityStorage`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines, whether runtime changes of the event's availability are persisted in NVRAM.

If this option is disabled, changing the event availability is not persisted and API Dem_SetEventAvailable() will work as described in the AUTOSAR SWS.

When activating this feature, the configuration needs an additional NV-RAM block with DemNvRamBlockIdType "DEM_NVBLOCK_AVAILABILITY".
The referenced NV block must be configured to hold the configured number of DemEventParameter containers + 1, divided by 8, fraction rounded up to integer.
Enabling this option will prevent API Dem_SetEventAvailable() from working before Dem_Init(), as the NV data must be restored first.

If this option is enabled, you also need DemGeneral/DemAvailabilitySupport == TRUE - otherwise the availability is not persisted in NVRAM.

######## 3. DemDebounceCounterBasedSupport

- **UUID**: `78fafda0-89e2-4755-ab3c-20c533076cc9`
- **参数名**: `DemDebounceCounterBasedSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines, whether support for counter based debouncing is enabled or not (see DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemDebounceAlgorithmClass/DemDebounceCounterBased/DemDebounceCounterBasedClassRef).

If events are configured to use counter based debouncing, this option must be enabled.

######## 4. DemDebounceTimeBasedSupport

- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines, whether support for time based debouncing is enabled or not (see DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemDebounceAlgorithmClass/DemDebounceTimeBased/DemDebounceTimeBasedClassRef).

If events are configured to use timer based debouncing, this option must be enabled.

######## 5. DemDevErrorDetect

- **UUID**: `d460ad1e-22e3-4293-b697-669b240f840f`
- **参数名**: `DemDevErrorDetect`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Activate/Deactivate the Development Error Detection and Notification.

If this option is enabled, integration errors are reported to the DET module (e.g. calling APIs with invalid arguments that would cause a malfunction of the Dem module).
In a production system this option can be disabled as all code should be tested to correctly use the Dem API.

######## 6. DemEnableConditionSupport

- **UUID**: `30310b82-d789-4ccb-851b-ec571e54b291`
- **参数名**: `DemEnableConditionSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines, whether support for enable conditions is enabled or not.

If enable conditions are set up for events (DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEnableConditionGroupRef) this option must be enabled.

######## 7. DemGeneralInterfaceSupport

- **UUID**: `d6f47c8d-f57f-45b8-b542-7729f1165a6d`
- **参数名**: `DemGeneralInterfaceSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The interfaces GeneralEvtInfo, GeneralCallbackEventDataChanged and GeneralCallbackEventStatusChange are provided if DemGeneralInterfaceSupport is equal to true.
(Not supported, please use
- DemGeneral/DemGeneralDiagnosticInfoSupport,
- DemGeneral/DemTriggerGlobalEventDataChangedCallback,
- DemGeneral/DemTriggerGlobalEventStatusChangedCallback
instead.)

######## 8. DemOperationCycleStatusStorage

- **UUID**: `dae4fe4f-50a1-4d19-a92a-efcde55a5089`
- **参数名**: `DemOperationCycleStatusStorage`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines if the operation cycle state is available over the power cycle (stored non-volatile) or not.

If this value is disabled, operation cycles are considered 'stopped' at the time of Dem_Init. Cycles must be started first to allow monitor reports to be processed, but starting a cycle involves event status changes.
So if you have operation cycles that are longer than the power cycle of your ECU, you need to enable this option.

This parameter is only valid if DemOperationCycleProcessing is set to OPCYC_STATE.

######## 9. DemPTOSupport

- **UUID**: `1dc26fbe-bed0-4bd9-99f7-fd32933583ed`
- **参数名**: `DemPTOSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines, whether PTO support (and therefore PID $1E support) is enabled or not.

This parameter is not supported. Use parameter /Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemConsiderPtoStatus to configure an event as affected by PTO and activate the Dem PTO handling.

######## 10. DemResetConfirmedBitOnOverflow

- **UUID**: `c3dee274-4b07-4248-b314-c646f0a5a84e`
- **参数名**: `DemResetConfirmedBitOnOverflow`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines, whether the ConfirmedDTC UDS status bit is reset or not when an event memory entry will be displaced.

If enabled, a DTC will immediately 'age', i.e. lose its ConfirmedDTC bit if it is displaced.

If disabled, a DTC will stop aging on displacement. Its ConfirmedDTC status bit will stay active until the DTC can allocate a new aging counter, at which point the aging process will start from the beginning. Alternatively, ClearDTC will also clear such a DTC.

######## 11. DemStatusBitStorageTestFailed

- **UUID**: `181781cb-6e00-400e-8851-2311eaad52c0`
- **参数名**: `DemStatusBitStorageTestFailed`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Activate/Deactivate the permanent storage of the "TestFailed" status bits.

If disabled, the TestFailed bit is reset during initilization.
Please note that this does not suffice to start aging of the event.

######## 12. DemStorageConditionSupport

- **UUID**: `ff53d5bf-811f-458e-9045-95801ed75faf`
- **参数名**: `DemStorageConditionSupport`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines, whether support for storage conditions is enabled or not.

If storage conditions are set up for events (DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemStorageConditionGroupRef) this option must be enabled.

######## 13. DemTriggerDcmReports

- **UUID**: `4cff30c0-8fd0-4812-9557-777c866d4924`
- **参数名**: `DemTriggerDcmReports`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If enabled, the Dcm module is notified about DTC status changes in primary memory.
Status changes caused by Dem_<...>ClearDTC do not trigger this callback. Notifications must be enabled using API Dem_DcmControlDTCStatusChangedNotification.

For ASR4.0.3 compatible behavior, disable this option, and configure the Dcm trigger function in parameter 'DemCallbackDTCStatusChangedFnc'.

This is required to implement RoE - OnDTCChange supervision, please refer to the Dcm configuration for details.

######## 14. DemTriggerDltReports

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Activate/Deactivate the notification to the Diagnostic Log and Trace.

######## 15. DemTriggerFiMReports

- **UUID**: `a60df93c-7e66-4994-ab80-dbad3f833fd7`
- **参数名**: `DemTriggerFiMReports`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Activate/Deactivate the notification to the Function Inhibition Manager.

This option must be enabled if the FiM requires monitor status change notification (see FiM/FiMGeneral/FiMEventUpdateTriggeredByDem).
It enables MonitorStatusChange and FiM_DemInit according AR4.3.0.

######## 16. DemTriggerFiMReportsExtended

- **UUID**: `008447bb-543b-4dd0-a4d7-6b3470b66629`
- **参数名**: `DemTriggerFiMReportsExtended`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Activate/Deactivate the notification to the Function Inhibition Manager.

This option must be enabled if the FiM requires event status change notification.
It is used for Vector FiM's OBD extension, and also provides backward compatibility for older FiM version (ASR 4.2 and older)

######## 17. DemTriggerMonitorInitBeforeClearOk

- **UUID**: `287714da-719e-4b0f-adb5-86840fd72b52`
- **参数名**: `DemTriggerMonitorInitBeforeClearOk`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines if the monitor re-initialization has to be triggered before or after the Dem module returns DEM_CLEAR_OK.

This option is not supported.
InitMonitor callbacks are always triggered during the clear operation, and have completed before the final result is returned to the Dcm.

######## 18. DemTriggerMonitorInitForEnableConditions

- **UUID**: `d2c27fee-a643-4440-868e-f22623a385db`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines if the monitor re-initialization is triggered when enable conditions re-activate a disabled monitor
This option requires AR4.1.2 API compatibility.

######## 19. DemVersionInfoApi

- **UUID**: `0f68c003-bc45-4e19-aa69-608ff59c8302`

######## 20. DemAgingRetainEnvironmentalData

- **UUID**: `c4f60c40-f12a-453a-9fcd-7b53ae3faea6`
- **参数名**: `DemAgingRetainEnvironmentalData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this option is enabled, environmental data stored with a DTC is not deleted on aging.

This includes snapshot data and extended data records.
The data is reported for UDS services as if the DTC was still confirmed, although Bit3 is not set anymore in the DTC status byte.
Aged event entries will be treated as empty as far as displacment is concerned, even if DTC displacement is deactivated by DemEventDisplacementStategy.

If this option is disabled, event memory entries including all stored data are cleared on aging. This setting corresponds to the behavior defined by AUTOSAR.

######## 21. DemDcmSupport

- **UUID**: `7fb1894c-201f-4227-8201-aa5eef6bdca0`
- **参数名**: `DemDcmSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: When this parameter is missing, the Dem assumes the Dcm being configured (that means you use a standard AUTOSAR stack).

If the parameter exists with value FALSE there is no Dcm module in the system: the Dem doesn't offer the APIs defined in file "Dem_Dcm.h" any more, and it does not offer the Dcm port interfaces. Use this setting when your ECU doesn't implement UDS diagnostic services. A J1939Dcm in system will then still offer J1939 diagnostic services.

If the parameter exists with value TRUE (or is missing) the standard Dem behaviour and APIs are available.

######## 22. DemEvMemOverflowIndicationSupport

- **UUID**: `9440888b-63ec-4a0a-ba07-d057f60efa56`
- **参数名**: `DemEvMemOverflowIndicationSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Provide the Port-Interface EvMemOverflowIndication.

If this parameter is disabled, the Dem will not provide the port interface or the C functions to read the overflow status of the event memories.

######## 23. DemGeneralDiagnosticInfoSupport

- **UUID**: `3a6428f4-0a5f-45d1-9d58-7f00c7ce39d6`

######## 24. DemImmediateNvStorageSupport

- **UUID**: `fa2214c5-4530-4a4e-9cd4-f67caa6f721b`
- **参数名**: `DemImmediateNvStorageSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: TRUE: For all DTCs for which DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemImmediateNvStorage parameter is enabled, environmental data is stored immediately in NvRam.
Independent of the value of DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemImmediateNvStorage OBD relevant data (OBD freeze frames and permanent DTCs) is always synchronized to NvRAM immediately.

FALSE or when this parameter is missing: If at least one of the following conditions is fulfilled, the feature will be switched on (same behavior as TRUE):
- at least one of the DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemImmediateNvStorage parameters is enabled
-  DemGeneral/DemNvSynchronizeSupport is set to TRUE
-  DemGeneral/DemClearDTCBehavior is set to DEM_CLRRESP_NONVOLATILE_FINISH
Otherwise the feature is disabled.

Note: Immediate storage of other data (e.g., event status, IUMPR, DTR) than environmental data is not triggered by enabling this option.
For more details regarding NvRAM synchronization see chapters 'NVRAM Write Frequency' in Technical Reference and Addendum for OBD.

######## 25. DemJ1939Support

- **UUID**: `413539c0-bb5e-4ccb-9568-27a338568a79`
- **参数名**: `DemJ1939Support`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Globally activate the support for J1939.

If this feature is active, the configuration of J1939 in container DemGeneral/DemGeneralJ1939 is mandatory.

######## 26. DemMultipleTripSupport

- **UUID**: `d1a7f8a3-504f-4b9a-b90f-bd6f584291cc`
- **参数名**: `DemMultipleTripSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: FALSE: Multitrip behavior is not allowed for any event. DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold has to be 0 or 1.
TRUE:  Multitrip behavior is possible, but not mandatory, i.e. confirmation handling is still according to the settings of DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold. Trip counter information is  available for all events.

Example: If all events have a single trip behavior, enabling MultipleTripSupport  may be useful for configuration variants, e.g. to calibrate a different value for DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold later.
When this parameter is missing, the Dem will select the confirmation handling according to the settings of DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold or DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemIndicatorAttribute/DemIndicatorHealingCycleCounterThreshold.

######## 27. DemNvSynchronizeSupport

- **UUID**: `c7ffce57-433d-4dc3-8896-60ae63e625c7`
- **参数名**: `DemNvSynchronizeSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Provides API Dem_RequestNvSynchronization.

If this option is enabled, API Dem_RequestNvSynchronization is provided which can be used to request full NV synchronization. For SWC, the API is available as operation SynchronizeNvData in port interface DemServices.

Use this API in case the AUTOSAR defined synchronization points do not suffice for your project.

######## 28. DemResetTestFailedOnOperationCycleStart

- **UUID**: `69286379-a7d2-466e-bb9b-fa88f4e9716b`
- **参数名**: `DemResetTestFailedOnOperationCycleStart`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this option is enabled, (re)starting an operation cycle also resets the TestFailed bit of events using the cycle as operation cycle.
This is not counted as a qualified test result.

######## 29. DemRetryStorageSupport

- **UUID**: `96c0bf33-9087-4c7a-b53a-c561ac92bb51`
- **参数名**: `DemRetryStorageSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: When enabled, the Dem will retry to store events that could not be stored; e.g. due to missing storage conditions, or filled up event memory.
An attempt to store is triggered for FAILED DTCs (Bit0 set) with every PRE_FAILED report (while the FDC is at 128) or FAILED report.

######## 30. DemSafeBswChecks

- **UUID**: `a65422b5-fa56-4fde-8b00-cbb97e3ba647`
- **参数名**: `DemSafeBswChecks`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Selects if safety checks are used. These checks improve robustness as e.g. invalid parameters are checked before function is executed. By default, this parameter is enabled. For projects where this component is not mapped to an ASIL partition, this parameter could be disabled.

######## 31. DemTriggerControlDTCSetting

- **UUID**: `3c0100a7-5ffb-4aba-97dd-4fd609f867ee`
- **参数名**: `DemTriggerControlDTCSetting`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The application is triggered if diagnostic service 0x85 - ControlDtcSetting is processed in DEM.
In case the ECU does not support an RTE (DemUseRte == False), the Dem calls function Appl_Dem_CBControlDTCSetting_ControlDTCSettingChanged, which must be supplied by the application

######## 32. DemTriggerDtcStatusChangedCallback

- **UUID**: `369b20e2-2f8c-4d2d-92d9-5e7918313428`
- **参数名**: `DemTriggerDtcStatusChangedCallback`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Globally disable DTC status notifications.

You can use this parameter to suppress all dtc status notification callbacks, usually because none are used in the first place.
You can also use this parameter as a temporary measure to allow compilation until all notification callouts are actually available.

If you want notifications, it is recommended to remove the parameter completely.

######## 33. DemTriggerEventDataChangedCallback

- **UUID**: `90be8eed-b49d-45f5-860c-f878fbf025c8`

- **多重性**: [0..1]

- **作用域**: LOCAL

- **符号名值**: false

- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Globally disable DTC data change notifications.

You can use this parameter to suppress all data change notification callbacks, usually because none are used in the first place.
You can also use this parameter as a temporary measure to allow compilation until all notification callouts are actually available.

If you want notifications, it is recommended to remove the parameter completely.

######## 34. DemTriggerEventStatusChangedCallback

- **UUID**: `85655cf5-3faf-4d91-b3b7-614a301b8012`
- **参数名**: `DemTriggerEventStatusChangedCallback`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Globally disable event status notifications.

You can use this parameter to suppress all event status notification callbacks, usually because none are used in the first place.
You can also use this parameter as a temporary measure to allow compilation until all notification callouts are actually available.

If you want notifications, it is recommended to remove the parameter completely.

######## 35. DemTriggerGlobalEventDataChangedCallback

- **UUID**: `4a634353-fa18-4e60-be5d-0aed1f00d1b2`
- **参数名**: `DemTriggerGlobalEventDataChangedCallback`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this parameter exists, you can explicitly control if the Dem requires the port interface 'GlobalEventDataChanged' (set: True) or not (set: False).
It is used as a 'one for all' callout in case event data changes.

If this parameter is missing (not set), the global interface is always required in addition to any DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemCallbackEventDataChanged callout as demanded by AUTOSAR.

This parameter here is irrelevant, if parameter DemGeneral/DemTriggerEventDataChangedCallback exists and has value False.

In case the ECU does not support an RTE (DemUseRte == False), the Dem calls function Appl_Dem_GeneralCBDataEvt_EventDataChanged, which must be supplied by the application.

######## 36. DemTriggerGlobalEventStatusChangedCallback

- **UUID**: `30c58e92-840a-44da-8de4-1dbcc60be52c`
- **参数名**: `DemTriggerGlobalEventStatusChangedCallback`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this parameter exists, you can explicitly control if the port interface 'GlobalEventStatusChanged' will be required (set: True) or not (set: False) by the Dem.
It is used as a 'one for all' callout in case event status changes.

If this parameter is missing (not set), the global interface is always required in addition to any DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemCallbackEventStatusChanged callout as demanded by AUTOSAR.

This parameter here is irrelevant, if parameter DemGeneral/DemTriggerEventStatusChangedCallback exists and has value False.

In case the ECU does not support an RTE (DemUseRte == False), the Dem calls function Appl_Dem_GeneralCBStatusEvt_EventStatusChanged, which must be supplied by the application.

######## 37. DemUseMemcopyMacros

- **UUID**: `490c56ef-e9bc-4b94-9e89-1d5efd435a87`
- **参数名**: `DemUseMemcopyMacros`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Control use of VStdLib.

If this switch is enabled, the Dem implementation uses macro implementations for memcopy and memset routines.

These macros can be defined within a user config file (see DemUserConfigFile) if so desired:
#define Dem_MemCpy_Macro(dst, src, len) /* some implementation */
#define Dem_MemSet_Macro(dst, val, len) /* some implementation */
If no definition is given by user config file, the Dem automatically uses a default implementation.

If the switch is disabled, VStdLib is used.

######## 38. DemUseNvm

- **UUID**: `6b322ab8-3dcc-49a9-b2d8-b12fe39a9cde`
- **参数名**: `DemUseNvm`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: When this parameter is missing, the Dem will determine if it uses nonvolatile memory blocks based on the configuration of DemNvRamBlockId containers. 
If the configuration of the containers is incorrect, NvM usage will be deactivated which is also indicated by a warning during generation.
If the configuration of the containers is correct, NvM usage will be active.

If NvM usage is deactivated, the Dem will not report modified blocks to the NvM and will run completely from RAM mirrors without persistence. All features concerning NV memory will not be available.

If the parameter exists, you can control manually if NvM usage is active.
This can be useful in early integration phases where you have not yet completely set up the NvM but still need the Dem for production error reporting of the communication stack.
Creating and manually setting this parameter is discouraged in production ECUs, i.e., the parameter should not exist in production ECUs. It is expected that the NvM shall be set up completely for production and the configuration of DemNvRamBlockId containers is correct.

######## 39. DemUseRte

- **UUID**: `ff6f1901-2976-403d-913c-0ceca18277e1`
- **参数名**: `DemUseRte`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Set to FALSE to disable the usage of RTE callbacks.

If disabled, all callouts are replaced by direct function calls. This is supported only for ECUs with neither RTE nor BRE.
If the ECU uses an RTE or BRE, this option must be enabled.

######## 40. DemUserDefinedCompareAndSwap

- **UUID**: `e4017b12-6fa6-47ce-84bf-4a38047b921e`
- **参数名**: `DemUserDefinedCompareAndSwap`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Implement and use a hardware optimized ApplDem_SyncCompareAndSwap() function.

The API ApplDem_SyncCompareAndSwap(uint32* AddressPtr, uint32 OldValue, uint32 NewValue) performs an atomic compare and swap of memory contents.
If disabled, the DEM uses an internal implementation, that needs the critical section 'CrossCoreComm'.
If enabled, the DEM uses an external implementation.

######## 41. DemUserControlledWirSupport

- **UUID**: `d91d3a78-033d-4d44-aa2d-73184989b966`
- **参数名**: `DemUserControlledWirSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Activate the external modifiability of the WarningIndicatorRequest Status Bit.

If this option is enabled, the API Dem_SetWIRStatus is provided, which allows the user to override the current WarningIndicatorRequest Bit of the DTC status.
Additionally, the API Dem_GetWIRStatus can be used the read the current override status (WIR controlled / not controlled by user).

If this option is disabled, overriding the WarningIndicatorRequest Bit is deactivated, and the APIs Dem_SetWIRStatus and Dem_GetWIRStatus are not available.

######## 42. DemUseMultiPartitionFiM

- **UUID**: `a846a007-b572-4bff-8e60-812a46488ef9`
- **参数名**: `DemUseMultiPartitionFiM`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines, whether a Multi Partition FiM is used. 

If this parameter is enabled, the Dem calls the APIs FiM_DemInitMaster and FiM_DemInitSatellite instead of FiM_DemInit during initialization.

This parameter is only relevant, if the FiM is notified about monitor status changes (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemTriggerFiMReports).

######## 43. DemReportSuppressedDTCsInService19x0A

- **UUID**: `f0a5282d-c2ac-49ec-8552-d252ddce3896`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this parameter is enabled, suppressed DTCs are reported in UDS service $19 0A.

If this parameter is disabled, suppressed DTCs are not reported in UDS service $19 0A.

######## 44. DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs

- **UUID**: `8de4ba95-0814-4d13-a49c-c63442242586`
- **参数名**: `DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this parameter is enabled, all DTCs can age independently of a memory entry.

If this parameter is disabled, only DTCs with a memory entry can age.

######## 45. DemSupportStorageIndependentCycleCounters

- **UUID**: `b91cdeb9-d3d1-4b7b-9ef0-7d17efb7fffb`
- **参数名**: `DemSupportStorageIndependentCycleCounters`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this parameter is enabled, Dem internal data elements FAILED_CYCLES and FAULT_PENDING_COUNTER are calculated and reported independently of an event memory entry (if configured for event).

When activating this feature, the configuration needs an additional NV-RAM block with DemNvRamBlockIdType "DEM_NVBLOCK_CYCLECOUNTER". Additional two bytes per DTC are required for NVRAM. 

If this parameter is disabled or does not exist, the counters are only tracked and reported if the event has a memory entry.

Set Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility to 'ALL_DTC' for full benefit of this feature.

######## 46. DemSupportExtendedFiM

- **UUID**: `2caeb72d-bf1e-4fed-86e5-98c58207b56e`
- **参数名**: `DemSupportExtendedFiM`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines whether the extended communication between FiM and Dem is enabled.

When enabled, the extended status byte, in addition to the DTC status byte, and the APIs for the extended status byte handling, namely Dem_GetSI30Status() and Dem_SetSI30Symptom(), are supported. The DEM also invokes the extended FiM API FiM_DemTriggerOnUnconfirmed() when the event reaches the storage threshold.

Precondition: If the extended communication between FiM and Dem is enabled, support for Powertrain features must be enabled (/Dem/DemGeneral/DemSupportPowertrain == true).

######## 49. DemSupportService19x56

######## 51. DemSupportService19x1A

Subfunction 0x1A provides a list of all DTCs and their UDS status that are configured for a requested Extended Data Record.

######## 52. DemSupportService19x04DIDLength

- **UUID**: `5b690d42-b895-4c93-a0c1-8ad910e3beb5`

This additional size byte is located between the DID and the DID data.

######## 53. DemCalibrationSupport

- **UUID**: `9234dbd2-5a9d-425a-a9cf-bdf6b4f3e196`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE

If this parameter is set to false, all configuration options under Dem/DemGeneral/DemCalibrationGeneral will be ignored.

######## 54. DemSupportStorageIndependentMaxFDCSinceLastClear

- **UUID**: `5d342162-c8de-449e-a3e0-ce45748234c7`
- **参数名**: `DemSupportStorageIndependentMaxFDCSinceLastClear`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this parameter is enabled, the Dem internal data element MAX_FDC_SINCE_LAST_CLEAR (if configured for the event) is calculated and stored independently of an event memory entry. In order for the data element to be reported for all DTCs independent of an existing memory entry, Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility must be set to ALL_DTC.

If this parameter is enabled, the data element is stored in the Admin data block instead of in the memory entry and one additional byte per DTC is required for NVRAM. 

If this parameter is disabled or does not exist, MAX_FDC_SINCE_LAST_CLEAR is only tracked and reported if the event has a memory entry.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. DemTaskTime

- **UUID**: `50f5cea1-c9f4-43ba-9e77-521b6cfcfb97`
- **参数名**: `DemTaskTime`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0.01`
- **取值范围**: [0.001..0.1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Allow to configure the time for the periodic cyclic task.

This value is used to set up the Dem Service-Description and BSW scheduler. This time is also used as time base in case time based debouncing is supported (see DemDebounceTimeBasedSupport, DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemDebounceAlgorithmClass/DemDebounceTimeBased/DemDebounceTimeBasedClassRef).

The AUTOSAR configuration standard is to use SI units, so in the EcuC file this parameter is stored as float value in seconds - the DaVinci Configurator 5 GUI will convert the value when using a diffenent unit.

######## 2. DemDebounceTimeLowResThreshold

- **UUID**: `867a4ebd-09ec-4aa6-906a-dc589fa003b4`
- **参数名**: `DemDebounceTimeLowResThreshold`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `5`
- **取值范围**: [0.15..3600]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Largest time duration (excl.) for using the high resolution timer.

To reduce CPU load and reduce configuration code size, the feature Time Base Debounce can use 2 different timers: the high resolution timer will tick with the configured DemTaskTime (typ. 10ms), the additional low resolution timer will tick with 150ms.

This configuration value defines the min. debounce time for an event to be handled by the low-res timer. Default: 5s

Refer to configuration values DemDebounceTimeFailedThreshold and DemDebounceTimePassedThreshold of each event:
- if both time values of an event are lower than the threshold (typ. 5s), the event's debounce is using the high-res timer with a jitter of DemTaskTime (typ. 10ms);
- otherwise the debounce algorithms are using the low-res timer (with a jitter of 150ms) and a reduced CPU load.

The AUTOSAR configuration standard is to use SI units, so in the EcuC file this parameter is stored as float value in seconds - the GUI will convert the value when using a diffenent unit.

####### STRING 类型参数

######## 1. DemHeaderFileInclusion

- **UUID**: `d87fcee2-94d0-4009-9756-571ef305a101`
- **参数名**: `DemHeaderFileInclusion`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Name of the header file(s) to be included by the Dem module containing the used C-callback declarations.

The different callout configurations allow to specify a C-Callback instead of a port interface. If you choose so, you need to add a header file here with the appropriate declaration of the function.
see DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemCallbackInitMForF/DemCallbackInitMForFFnc,
see DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemCallbackClearEventAllowed/DemCallbackClearEventAllowedFnc,
see DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemCallbackEventDataChanged/DemCallbackEventDataChangedFnc,
see DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemCallbackEventStatusChanged/DemCallbackEventStatusChangedFnc,
see DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemCallbackInitMForE/DemCallbackInitMForEFnc,
see DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemDebounceAlgorithmClass/DemDebounceMonitorInternal/DemCallbackGetFDC/DemCallbackGetFDCFnc,
see DemGeneral/DemCallbackDTCStatusChanged/DemCallbackDTCStatusChangedFnc,
see DemGeneral/DemDataClass/DemDataElementReadFnc,
see DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemClearDTCNotification/DemClearDtcNotificationFnc.

######## 2. DemUserConfigFile

- **UUID**: `772ff249-ac6f-48da-a62b-a59753433f9b`
- **参数名**: `DemUserConfigFile`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Path to an external user configuration file used by the DaVinci Configurator 5 generation tool.

If you want to overwrite some of the settings in the generated configuration file (Dem_Cfg.h), you can specify a path to a user defined configuration file. The user defined configuration file will be included at the end of the generated preprocessor switches. Definitions in the user defined configuration file can overwrite definitions in the generated configuration file.

To specify the file name, you can use "$(var)" sequence(s) in the specified path. Each such sequence is replaced by the content of the related environment variable "var".
Additionally you can use following predefined sequences:
$(DpaProjectFolder) Absolute directory of the loaded *.dpa file.
$(EcuProjectName) Name of the project defined in the loaded *.dpa file.
$(EcucFile) Absolute directory "ECUC", defined by the loaded *.dpa file, containing files $(EcuProjectName).ecuc.arxml and $(EcuProjectName).ecuc.Initial.arxml .
$(EcucFolder) Parent directory of $(EcucFile).
$(SipRootPath) Absolute directory "SIP", defined by the loaded *.dpa file.
$(OutputFolder) (Relative) directory "GenData", defined by the loaded *.dpa file. See setting "Root Target Folder" of Configurator 5.
$(ServiceSwcFolder) Absolute directory "ServiceComponents", defined by the loaded *.dpa file.
$(cwd) Current working directory when starting Configurator 5.

RESTRICTIONS: Not all of the settings can be overwritten simply by triggering the preprocessor switch. Some of the options are complex and the code generator generates less or more code depending on the setting.

USE SUCH A FILE ONLY AFTER DISCUSSION WITH VECTOR.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE

- **描述**: Path to an external user configuration file used by the DaVinci Configurator 5 generation tool.

If you want to overwrite some of the settings in the generated configuration file (Dem_LCfg.h), you can specify a path to a user defined configuration file. The user defined configuration file will be included at the end of the file. Definitions in the user defined configuration file can overwrite definitions in the generated configuration file.

To specify the file name, you can use "$(var)" sequence(s) in the specified path. Each such sequence is replaced by the content of the related environment variable "var".
Additionally you can use following predefined sequences:
$(DpaProjectFolder) Absolute directory of the loaded *.dpa file.
$(EcuProjectName) Name of the project defined in the loaded *.dpa file.
$(EcucFile) Absolute directory "ECUC", defined by the loaded *.dpa file, containing files $(EcuProjectName).ecuc.arxml and $(EcuProjectName).ecuc.Initial.arxml .
$(EcucFolder) Parent directory of $(EcucFile).
$(SipRootPath) Absolute directory "SIP", defined by the loaded *.dpa file.
$(OutputFolder) (Relative) directory "GenData", defined by the loaded *.dpa file. See setting "Root Target Folder" of Configurator 5.
$(ServiceSwcFolder) Absolute directory "ServiceComponents", defined by the loaded *.dpa file.
$(cwd) Current working directory when starting Configurator 5.

RESTRICTIONS: Usually it is sufficient to use only the DemUserConfigFile. This option should be only used with extreme caution.

USE SUCH A FILE ONLY AFTER DISCUSSION WITH VECTOR.

  - `DEM_NO_AVAILABILITY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)

######## 3. DemClearDTCBehavior

- **UUID**: `fd4ae8f6-b231-4ec2-8f09-a3bc988d5573`
- **参数名**: `DemClearDTCBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_CLRRESP_VOLATILE`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_CLRRESP_NONVOLATILE_FINISH` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_CLRRESP_NONVOLATILE_TRIGGER` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_CLRRESP_VOLATILE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Select the point in time a clear DTC request will result in a positive response from the Dcm.

VOLATILE: The Dem will clear all DTCs in RAM before the API Dem_ClearDTC returns a final result to the Dcm. If power is cut right after that and your ECU does not allow for a controlled shut down in that case, DTCs will still be visible after the ECU restarts.

NONVOLATILE_TRIGGER: ClearDTC will return a final result after the NvM has been informed about the modification. This setting is not supported by this implementation.

NONVOLATILE_FINISH: ClearDTC will return a final result only after the NvM has finished persisting the cleared state of DTCs. Depending on your NV system this can result in several seconds of NRC $78 - response pending. On the upside, if power is cut after the positive response, all DTCs remain cleared.

######## 4. DemClearDTCLimitation

- **UUID**: `2d397024-6014-4919-8d9a-1e8560fdada0`
- **参数名**: `DemClearDTCLimitation`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_ALL_SUPPORTED_DTCS`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_ALL_SUPPORTED_DTCS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_ONLY_CLEAR_ALL_DTCS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_ONLY_CLEAR_OBDII_AND_ALL_DTCS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_ONLY_CLEAR_SINGLE_AND_ALL_DTCS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_ONLY_CLEAR_EMISSIONS_SYSTEM_GROUP_AND_ALL_DTCS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the supported Dem_{...}ClearDTC API scope.

ALL_SUPPORTED_DTCS: The API accepts all supported DTC values, as well as all DTC values which are configured in DemGroupDTCs and DEM_DTC_GROUP_ALL_DTCS (default).

ONLY_CLEAR_ALL_DTCS: The API accepts ClearAllDTCs only (DTC Group 0xFFFFFF).

ONLY_CLEAR_OBDII_AND_ALL_DTCS: The API accepts ClearAllDTCs only (DTC Groups 0x000000, 0xFFFFFF) in OBDII configuration variants.

ONLY_CLEAR_SINGLE_AND_ALL_DTCS: The API accepts DTC numbers and ClearAllDTCs (DTC Group, 0xFFFFFF).

ONLY_CLEAR_EMISSIONS_SYSTEM_GROUP_AND_ALL_DTCS: The API accepts ClearAllDTCs only (DTC Groups 0xFFFF33, 0xFFFFFF) in WWHOBD, OBD on UDS and ZEV on UDS configuration variants.

######## 6. DemEventCombinationSupport

- **UUID**: `d4a252fd-2d76-4a4d-93d3-69a9e6844c5f`
- **参数名**: `DemEventCombinationSupport`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_EVCOMB_DISABLED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_EVCOMB_DISABLED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_EVCOMB_TYPE1` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_EVCOMB_TYPE2` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_EVCOMB_TYPE3` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Merge several events to one specific DTC and a combined UDS DTC status byte.

DISABLED: No event combination is supported.

TYPE1: Multiple events can be combined to a single DTC. The events are combined on storage as specified in AUTOSAR.

TYPE2: Multiple events can be combined to a single DTC. The events are combined on retrieval as specified in AUTOSAR.

TYPE3: This type of event combination is only relevant if OBDonUDS is supported in at least one configuration variant.
For configuration variants without OBDonUDS support:
- The behavior of all DTCs corresponds to event combination TYPE1.

For configuration variants with OBDonUDS support:
- The calculation of the DTC status of OBD relevant DTCs deviates from TYPE1. It corresponds to the behavior specified in the Digital Annex of SAE J1979.
- The behavior of DTCs that are not OBD relevant still corresponds to TYPE1.

######## 7. DemEventDisplacementStrategy

- **UUID**: `fe2f02c4-54f8-45f0-8e12-3e918142db3e`
- **参数名**: `DemEventDisplacementStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_DISPLACEMENT_LEGACY_403`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_DISPLACEMENT_FULL` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_DISPLACEMENT_NONE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_DISPLACEMENT_PRIO_OCC` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_DISPLACEMENT_LEGACY_403` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch is back-ported from AR4.1.1 and specifies the event displacement when the event memory is currently filled completely.
It replaces the AR4.0.3 parameter DemEventDisplacementSupport.

Please note, the DTC selected by the displacement algorithm is only displaced if it is a better match than the new DTC that has to be entered into the memory.
If the new DTC is the better match, or no matching DTCs are found in the event memory, the fall-back strategy DemEventDisplacementFallbackStrategy is implemented.

NONE: No displacement.
If the event memory is filled, a new event will always be displaced immediately.
Please note, the only supported fall-back strategy for this case is DISCARD_NEW_EVENT.

PRIO_OCC: Displacement is active.
If the event memory is filled, the DTC(s) with lowest priority are selected.
If multiple DTCs share this lowest priority, the oldest is selected.

FULL: Displacement is active.
If the event memory is filled, the DTC(s) with the lowest priority are selected.
If multiple DTCs share this lowest priority, the oldest passive DTC is selected.
If no passive DTCs are among the lowest priority list, the oldest active, but untested DTC is selected.

LEGACY_403: Vector extension that corresponds to the displacement behavior defined in Autosar 4.0.3.
If the event memory is filled, the DTC(s) with the lowest priority are selected.
If multiple DTCs share this lowest priority, the oldest passive DTC is selected.

######## 8. DemExtendedDataCapture

- **UUID**: `98012dad-9897-44f0-ab6d-854c7766db73`
- **参数名**: `DemExtendedDataCapture`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_TRIGGER_EVENT_MEMORY_STORAGE`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_EVENT_MEMORY_STORAGE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_TESTFAILED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the point in time, when the extended data collection is done.

TESTFAILED: The data is collected directly in the context of the SetEventStatus call. This option is not supported.

MEMORY_STORAGE: The data is collected during the next Dem task cycle, in case a memory entry can be created for the DTC (see DemEventDisplacementSupport)

######## 9. DemExtendedDataVisibility

- **UUID**: `bbd0f67f-b9bc-4bc9-ac90-36026d29eeaa`
- **参数名**: `DemExtendedDataVisibility`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_VISIBILITY_ALL_DTC`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_VISIBILITY_ALL_DTC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_VISIBILITY_STOREDONLY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the point in time when extended data will be reported in diagnostic response messages.

This parameter affects extended data records that consist solely of statistical data which is by its nature at any time available, see DemInternalDataElement.

Using this parameter allows to reduce visibility of such extended data records until the DTC is actually stored.

ALL_DTC: The data is reported independent of an existing memory entry.

STOREDONLY: The data is reported only if a memory entry exists for the reported DTC.

Example: The current fault detection counter exists at any time for all DTCs. If this parameter is set to ALL_DTC, service 19 06 will report the value of the FDC for DTCs that are not currently stored.
If this parameter is set to STOREDONLY, Dcm will instead send a positive response without any content for those DTCs.

######## 10. DemFreezeFrameCapture

- **UUID**: `4f3f3aec-d7bc-45b1-8171-40d14e1066eb`
- **参数名**: `DemFreezeFrameCapture`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_TRIGGER_EVENT_MEMORY_STORAGE`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false

- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the point in time, when the freeze frame data collection is done.

TESTFAILED: The data is collected directly in the context of the SetEventStatus call. This option is not supported.

MEMORY_STORAGE: The data is collected during the next Dem task cycle, in case a memory entry can be created for the DTC (see DemEventDisplacementSupport).

######## 11. DemOBDSupport

- **UUID**: `265acb5a-ef8a-497e-bda8-141684d99f8e`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDSupport`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_OBD_NO_OBD_SUPPORT`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_OBD_DEP_SEC_ECU` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_MASTER_ECU` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_NO_OBD_SUPPORT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_OBD_PRIMARY_ECU` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: DEM_OBD_NO_OBD_SUPPORT: OBD support is disabled, the DEM operates in UDS mode.

DEP_SEC_ECU: OBD support is enabled, the DEM operates in secondary/dependent ECU mode. (not supported)

PRIMARY_ECU: OBD support is enabled, the DEM operates in primary ECU mode.

MASTER_ECU: OBD support is enabled, the DEM operates in master ECU mode. (not supported)

If OBD support is NOT disabled, use parameter Dem/DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation to choose either OBD II or WWH-OBD or OBDonUDS or ZEVonUDS support.
If OBD support is disabled, all configured OBD relevant parameters (e.g. the parameters in Dem/DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD) are ignored.

######## 12. DemOccurrenceCounterProcessing

- **UUID**: `a222c05f-3b8d-4e41-bd16-f08c2d326cdb`
- **参数名**: `DemOccurrenceCounterProcessing`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_PROCESS_OCCCTR_TF`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_PROCESS_OCCCTR_CDTC` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_PROCESS_OCCCTR_TF` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The occurrence counter is incremented each time the event status transitions from 'passed' to 'failed'.

TF (TestFailed) : The counter is incremented each time.

CDTC (Confirmed): The counter is incremented only after the event is confirmed - Effectively the counter starts incrementing after N cycles (see DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold).

######## 13. DemOperationCycleProcessing

- **UUID**: `f1b89640-4887-4e3b-b383-3e40cf2f3cfe`
- **参数名**: `DemOperationCycleProcessing`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_PROCESS_OPCYC_STATE`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_PROCESS_OPCYC_COUNTER` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_PROCESS_OPCYC_STATE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is obsolete since the option DEM_PROCESS_OPCYC_COUNTER is not longer supported by AUTOSAR.

OPCYC_STATE: Cycles are reported by SetOperationCycleState(Start/Stop) and the Dem counts the cycles internally.

OPCYC_COUNTER: Cycles are counted externally. The current count is reported to the Dem by SetOperationCycleCounter.

Only OPCYC_STATE is supported at the moment.

######## 14. DemStatusBitHandlingTestFailedSinceLastClear

- **UUID**: `68becbca-6f12-4160-940c-604ce5e42329`
- **参数名**: `DemStatusBitHandlingTestFailedSinceLastClear`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_STATUS_BIT_NORMAL`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_STATUS_BIT_AGING_AND_DISPLACEMENT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_STATUS_BIT_NORMAL` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_STATUS_BIT_AGING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines whether the aging and displacement mechanism shall be applied to the "TestFailedSinceLastClear" status bits.

AGING_AND_DISPLACEMENT: The status bit 'TestFailedSinceLastClear' is only set if an event can be enteren into its event memory. (In case the event is not mapped to any destination it is never set!) The status bit is reset due to aging, clearing and displacement of the memory entry.

NORMAL: The status bit 'TestFailedSinceLastClear' set for all events, irrespective of successful allocation of an event memory entry. It is reset to 0 only when the event is cleared.

AGING: The status bit 'TestFailedSinceLastClear' set for all events, irrespective of successful allocation of an event memory entry. It is reset to 0 when the event ages, or is cleared.

######## 15. DemSuppressionSupport

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines, whether support for suppression is enabled or not.
According AUTOSAR 4.3, this DEM implementation only supports the suppression by DTC, not the suppression by Event.

DTC_SUPPRESSION: Support suppression by DTC.

EVENT_AND_DTC_SUPPRESSION: Support suppression by Event and DTC.
Equivalent to DTC_SUPPRESSION, as  suppression by Event is not supported.

EVENT_SUPPRESSION: Support suppression by Event.
Equivalent to NO_SUPPRESSION, as  suppression by Event is not supported.

NO_SUPPRESSION: Suppression is not supported.

Its value is returned by some of the $19 services.

ISO14229_1: ISO14229-1 DTC format.

SAEJ1939_73: SAEJ1939-73 DTC format.

J2012DA_FORMAT_04: SAE_J2012-DA_DTCFormat_04 DTC format.

######## 17. DemBswApiVersion

- **UUID**: `a22945c2-90fa-4ca9-a27e-4c0d702a07af`
- **参数名**: `DemBswApiVersion`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_BSW_API_4_XX_XX`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_BSW_API_4_XX_XX` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_BSW_API_3_XX_XX` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Specifies the AUTOSAR version of the integrated BSW stack API that Dem shall be able to interact with.

This setting will modify the symbolic names, that are offered by the DEM and used by other BSW modules.
See the additional parameters for the reverse (the DEM uses a symbolic name that is offered by another module): DemGeneral/DemBswApiVersionNvM, DemGeneral/DemBswApiVersionFiM.

API_4_XX_XX: The DEM offers only symbolic names according the definition of AUTOSAR 4 for interaction with other BSW modules.

API_3_XX_XX: The DEM creates additional symbolic names according the definition of AUTOSAR 3 for interaction with other BSW modules:
The DEM itself will offer additional symbolic names (that may be used by AUTOSAR 3 BSW modules) for:
- DemAgingCycle identifier
- DemEnableConditionId
- DemEventId
- DemGroupDTCs
- DemIndicatorID
- DemMILGroup identifier
- DemOperationCycle identifier
- DemStorageConditionId

######## 18. DemBswApiVersionNvM

- **UUID**: `a14b6896-418d-41d2-a496-61aeee585fe7`
- **参数名**: `DemBswApiVersionNvM`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION

- **描述**: Specifies the AUTOSAR version of the integrated NvM API that Dem shall be able to interact with.

Switches Dem interaction to the corresponding AUTOSAR version of NvM (DemNvRamBlockIdRef->NvMBlockDescriptor).

API_4_XX_XX: The DEM uses symbolic names according the definition of AUTOSAR 4 for interaction with the NvM module. This is the default.

API_3_XX_XX: The DEM uses symbolic names according the definition of AUTOSAR 3 for interaction with the NvM module. Use this setting when the NvM offers its symbolic names only according the AUTOSAR 3 naming convention (for example AUTOSAR 3 "NvM_DemAdminDataBlock" vs. AUTOSAR 4 "NvMConf_NvMBlockDescriptor_DemAdminDataBlock").

######## 19. DemBswApiVersionFiM

- **UUID**: `2eaf5b38-201a-481c-ac6c-480b3f629f9d`

Switches Dem interaction to the corresponding AUTOSAR version of FiM (DemFunctionIdRef->FiMFID, DemSecondaryFunctionIdRef->FiMFID, DemIgnitionCycleFunctionIdRef->FiMFID).

API_4_XX_XX: The DEM uses symbolic names according the definition of AUTOSAR 4 for interaction with the FiM module. This is the default.

API_3_XX_XX: The DEM uses symbolic names according the definition of AUTOSAR 3 for interaction with the FiM module. Use this setting when the FiM offers its symbolic names only according the AUTOSAR 3 naming convention (for example AUTOSAR 3 "FiM_FiMFID_001" vs. AUTOSAR 4 "FiMConf_FiMFID_FiMFID_001").

######## 20. DemAgingAfterHealing

- **UUID**: `fd64c82f-2fca-4936-9bfc-0ba63019b2cf`
- **参数名**: `DemAgingAfterHealing`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_AGING_WHILE_HEALING`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_AGING_WHILE_HEALING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGING_AFTER_HEALING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGING_AFTER_HEALING_ALL_DTC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter selects how aging and healing interact.

DEM_AGING_WHILE_HEALING will allow events that meet the configured aging criteria (DemAgingBehavior) to start aging.
In theory, this could mean that events can age while they still request an indicator. Use well defined operation cycles to avoid this effect, i.e. aging cycles should not complete faster than operation cycles.

DEM_AGING_AFTER_HEALING will allow aging only after the event has finished healing, i.e. after resetting the warningIndicatorRequested status bit. This setting only has an effect on events with an indicators. This is the behavior defined by the AUTOSAR standard.

DEM_AGING_AFTER_HEALING_ALL_DTC behaves like DEM_AGING_AFTER_HEALING, but extends the healing process to events without indicator. I.e. even though the events do not set the WIR bit, they will only start aging after they would have reset it. This way, both events with and without indicators age after the same amount of cycles.

Events configured to use the MIL will always behave according to DEM_AGING_AFTER_HEALING.

######## 21. DemAgingBehavior

- **UUID**: `3d57fc54-1e20-4e80-956d-18a43d8becb4`
- **参数名**: `DemAgingBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_AGING_TYPE_1`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_AGING_TYPE_1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGING_TYPE_2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGING_TYPE_3` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGING_TYPE_4` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGING_TYPE_5` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGING_TYPE_6` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter selects the algorithm for the aging of the events.

AGING_TYPE_1: Initial trigger to start the aging is the test result PASSED.
The aging counter is incremented at the end of the aging cycle.
Events with DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold == 0 will age with the PASSED test result.

AGING_TYPE_2: Initial trigger to start the aging is the end of operation cycle if the event was tested PASSED and was never tested FAILED in this cycle.
The aging counter is incremented at the end of the aging cycle.
Events with DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold == 0 will age at the end of the operation cycle if the event was tested PASSED and was not tested FAILED in this cycle.

AGING_TYPE_3: Initial trigger to start the aging is the end of operation cycle if the event was tested PASSED and was never tested FAILED in this cycle.
The aging counter is incremented at the end of the aging cycle if the event was tested PASSED and was never tested FAILED in this cycle.
Events with DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold == 0 will age at the end of the operation cycle if the event was tested PASSED and was not tested FAILED in this cycle.

AGING_TYPE_4: Initial trigger to start the aging is the test result PASSED.
The aging counter is incremented at the end of the aging cycle if the event was tested PASSED.
Events with DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold == 0 will age with the PASSED test result.

AGING_TYPE_5: Initial trigger to start the aging is the end of operation cycle if the event was tested PASSED and was never tested FAILED in this cycle.
The aging counter is incremented at the end of the aging cycle if the event was tested PASSED and was never tested FAILED in this cycle.
Events with DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold == 0 will age with the PASSED test result, if the event was not tested FAILED in this cycle.

AGING_TYPE_6: Initial trigger to start the aging is the test result PASSED, if the event was never tested FAILED in this cycle.
The aging counter is incremented at the end of the aging cycle if the event was tested PASSED and was never tested FAILED in this cycle.
Events with DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold == 0 will age with the PASSED test result.

######## 22. DemAgingCounterBehavior

- **UUID**: `03ed5750-bbcf-4d93-9bd5-f50a349329c7`
- **参数名**: `DemAgingCounterBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_AGING_COUNT_ALWAYS`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_AGING_COUNT_ALWAYS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGING_COUNT_ONLY_AGEABLE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Control whether an aging counter is incremented / decremented for events that do not support aging (refer to DemAgingAllowed).

DEM_AGING_COUNT_ALWAYS: The aging counter is incremented/decremented for every event. If an event does not support aging (DemAgingAllowed is set to false), its incrementing aging counter latches at aging cycle counter threshold and decrementing aging counters stops at 0.

DEM_AGING_COUNT_ONLY_AGEABLE: The aging counter is incremented/decremented only for events supporting aging. If an event does not support aging, incrementing aging counters remain at 0, decrementing aging counters remain at the aging cycle counter threshold value.

######## 23. DemAgingCounterReporting

- **UUID**: `75997b62-c8b8-4a13-b027-469dce5daa40`
- **参数名**: `DemAgingCounterReporting`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_AGING_NORMAL_REPORTING`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_AGING_NORMAL_REPORTING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGING_ZERO_AS_ONE_REPORTING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter changes how the aging counter is reported in an extended record.

AGING_NORMAL_REPORTING:
The counter is reported as defined by the internal data element (UPCOUNT/DOWNCOUNT)

AGING_ZERO_AS_ONE_REPORTING:
For upward counting, the counter value 0x00 is reported as value 0x01 in the cycle where aging is started.
This has no effect on downward counting and on events with aging cycle counter threshold 0.

######## 24. DemCompiledPostbuildCrcMode

- **UUID**: `4af27cc1-7ed0-4b3c-b868-5b37681e12cf`
- **参数名**: `DemCompiledPostbuildCrcMode`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_POSTBUILD_CRC_AUTOMATIC`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_POSTBUILD_CRC_AUTOMATIC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_POSTBUILD_CRC_MANUAL` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Switch the mode for the calculation of the variant specific checksum (CRC) over all postbuiild-changeable configuration items.
See parameter DemCompiledPostbuildCrc.

AUTOMATIC: the DaVinci Configurator5 calculates the checksum value

MANUAL: the user defines the checksum - use this setting only when the NvData shall not be cleared with a configuration change. Take care to manually change the checksum when the stored non-volatile data becomes incompatible to the current configuration.

######## 25. DemEventDisplacementFallbackStrategy

- **UUID**: `3f72a487-46a0-42b8-ad3a-ca1611ea485a`
- **参数名**: `DemEventDisplacementFallbackStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_FALLBACK_DISCARD_NEW_EVENT`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_FALLBACK_DISCARD_NEW_EVENT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_FALLBACK_DISPLACE_OLDEST_EVENT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Fallback strategy for event displacement:

DISCARD_NEW_EVENT:
Standard behavior defined by AUTOSAR. In case no candidate for displacement is found, a new event is silently discarded.

DISPLACE_OLDEST_EVENT:
In case no better candidate for displacement can be found, the oldest event is displaced unconditionally.
Note: Active OBD DTCs are not affected from this configuration.

######## 26. DemPendingDtcProcessing

- **UUID**: `28a80dc0-acd9-42ae-9324-1ec10a4c40c6`
- **参数名**: `DemPendingDtcProcessing`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_PROCESS_PDTC_STOREDONLY`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_PROCESS_PDTC_STOREDONLY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_PROCESS_PDTC_ALL_DTC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Select behavior of PendingDTC status bit.

STOREDONLY: Set the status bit2 (PendingDTC) only if a memory entry could be allocated successfully. With this setting the status bit PendingDTC is also reset when the event is displaced from memory.

ALL_DTC: Set the status bit2 (PendingDTC) independently from successful event storage. With this setting the status bit PendingDTC is not modified in case the DTC is displaced from memory.

######## 27. DemResetDebounceBehavior

- **UUID**: `0f0efe57-49ea-433d-b470-ba34b7428ee4`
- **参数名**: `DemResetDebounceBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_RESET_DEBOUNCE_ON_DISABLE_ENABLE_CONDITIONS`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_RESET_DEBOUNCE_ON_ENABLE_ENABLE_CONDITIONS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_RESET_DEBOUNCE_ON_DISABLE_ENABLE_CONDITIONS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Controls which enable condition group transition leads to a reset/freeze of the debouncing behaviour, either with the state trasition to enabled or disabled.

DEM_RESET_DEBOUNCE_ON_DISABLE_ENABLE_CONDITIONS
Reset/freeze the debouncing values when the events preconditions are no longer fulfilled.

DEM_RESET_DEBOUNCE_ON_ENABLE_ENABLE_CONDITIONS
Reset/freeze the debouncing values when the events preconditions return to fulfilled state, after they had been unfulfilled.
However, if /Dem/DemConfigSet/DemDebounceTimeBasedClass/DemDebounceContinuous == FALSE, then the debounce value is frozen,  when the events preconditions are no longer fulfilled.

######## 28. DemUserControlledWirBehavior

- **UUID**: `4f33902d-9364-4d55-9245-d12c9ba5d26f`
- **参数名**: `DemUserControlledWirBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_ENABLED_DURING_CONTROLDTCSETTING`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_DISABLED_DURING_CONTROLDTCSETTING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_ENABLED_DURING_CONTROLDTCSETTING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This setting has effect only when DemUserControlledWirSupport == True. (Status Bit WarningIndicatorRequest User Controlled == True)

DISABLED_DURING_CONTROLDTCSETTING: The API Dem_SetWIRStatus will return E_NOT_OK while ControlDTCSetting has disabled DTC storage.

ENABLED_DURING_CONTROLDTCSETTING
Dem_SetWIRStatus will process changes while ControlDTCSetting has disabled DTC storage.

######## 29. DemWarningIndicatorRequestedProcessing

- **UUID**: `4d5e6717-dcfc-4128-85c5-f2eca0bf1f02`
- **参数名**: `DemWarningIndicatorRequestedProcessing`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_PROCESS_WIR_STOREDONLY`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_PROCESS_WIR_STOREDONLY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_PROCESS_WIR_ALL_DTC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Select behavior of WarningIndicatorRequested status bit.

STOREDONLY: Set the status bit7 (WarningIndicatorRequested) only if a memory entry could be allocated successfully.

ALL_DTC: Set the status bit7 (WarningIndicatorRequested) independently from successful event storage.

Independent of the selected behavior the status bit 7 is not cleared due to displacement.

######## 30. DemLatchTestFailedBehavior

- **UUID**: `dfd60f27-efc9-4389-be8d-50ec870d1dbc`
- **参数名**: `DemLatchTestFailedBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_LATCH_TF_CONFIRMED_AND_THIS_CYCLE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_LATCH_TF_CONFIRMED_AND_THIS_CYCLE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_LATCH_TF_ONLY_THIS_CYCLE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the behavior of events that have DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventLatchTestFailed
enabled.

DEM_LATCH_TF_ONLY_THIS_CYCLE:
After a qualified FAILED result, a PASSED result for the event is ignored during the current operation cycle.

DEM_LATCH_TF_CONFIRMED_AND_THIS_CYCLE:
After a qualified FAILED result, a PASSED result for the event is ignored during the current operation cycle. Additionally if the event is confirmed, no PASSED result is processed for that event until it is cleared. As a consequence, the event cannot age.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the indicator representing the MIL.

If no indicator is selected, the MIL is not supported by this ECU.

When J1939 is supported, the MIL indicator must differ from the J1939 indicators.

######## 2. DemMasterOsApplicationRef

- **UUID**: `6042f208-a9cd-43cc-afd9-5e8c4ccd4589`
- **引用名**: `DemMasterOsApplicationRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `OsApplication`
- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Os/OsApplication`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The central partition for a multi partition DEM, see chapter "Multi-Partition support" in the TechnicalReference.

This partition is used as DEM Master partition.
When this configuration value is missing, the pseudo partition "INVALID_OSAPPLICATION" is substituted.

See also configuration parameter /Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventOsApplicationRef for the mapping of events to the DEM satellite's partition.
This value DemMasterOsApplicationRef here acts as a default for not configured DemEventOsApplicationRef values.

######## 3. DemOsApplicationRef

- **UUID**: `b84e694f-6c34-4a2c-966e-ff67fa731f95`
- **引用名**: `DemOsApplicationRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
