  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: List of references to partitions, the DEM is running at.

The Dem directly supports multi partition architectures, i.e. ECUs using multiple cores or memory protection for safety reasons.
See chapter "Multi-Partition support" in the TechnicalReference for details.

This list here implicitly contains all configured /Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventOsApplicationRef references as well as the configured /Dem/DemGeneral/DemMasterOsApplicationRef reference - even if they are not configured here explicitly.

######## 4. DemRestartCycleOnInitRef

- **UUID**: `8c96b2ec-ee04-414d-a65f-8fda36a8ae82`
- **引用名**: `DemRestartCycleOnInitRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]

- **完整路径**: `/AUTOSAR/EcucDefs/Dem/DemGeneral/DemOperationCycle`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Automatically start the referenced cycle with Dem_PreInit().

BSW events need a started cycle (typically the power cycle) to not be discarded.
Event changes will not be notified (Callbacks OnStatusChange, FiM, etc) since the RTE is not necessarily available at this point in time.
Make sure dependent modules (e.g. FiM) are set up accordingly.

####### 2. DemCallbackDTCStatusChanged

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `484fdb01-c751-4442-b524-6f2b57489062`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemCallbackDTCStatusChanged`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

The presence of this container sets up a callout function 'DTCStatusChanged' for this DTC. The Dem will call this function each time a DTC status changes.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackDTCStatusChangedFnc'.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE

If this parameter exists, it must give a valid C-Function which is called instead of using an RTE port interface.

####### 4. DemDataClass

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `64d96937-5282-4ac8-a980-deafe1b22dd3`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDataClass`
- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container groups the configuration (parameters) of a data element.

Data elements are used to build DIDs, PIDs and ExtendedDataRecords

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **7** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemDataElementDataSize

- **UUID**: `453659d9-d2d2-4432-a822-f81676e9f873`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementDataSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..4096]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the size of the data element in bytes.

For Dem-internal data elements, the size shall be predefined according to the selected internal data element (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemDataClass/DemDataElementInternalData).

For Dem-external data elements, the size depends on the data type and number of elements which needs to be stored.
For array data classes, this field must contain the total size of the array in bytes. An example is provided below.

BOOLEAN is a 8 bit data type and therefore needs 1 byte.
UINT8, SINT8, UINT8_N and SINT8_N are 8 bit data types and therefore need 1 bytes per element.
UINT16, SINT16, UINT16_N and SINT16_N are 16 bit data types and therefore need 2 bytes per element.
UINT32, SINT32, UINT32_N and SINT32_N are 32 bit data types and therefore need 4 bytes per element.
FLOAT and FLOAT_N entries are 32 bit data types and therefore need 4 bytes per element.

Example: For example if an UINT16_N array data type is selected, of which 4 entries need to be stored, than a data size of 8 bytes needs to be allocated.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. DemDataElementStoreNonVolatile

- **UUID**: `ae77d962-a3f3-4efd-be39-71e65a82f977`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementStoreNonVolatile`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Select if the data element needs to be stored in NVRam.

Ususally, snapshots of a system value (like temperature, voltage...) need to be stored in NVRam. If the data element is stored, the Dem will collect the current value into NVRam at the DTC storage time. Reading access will return the stored data (e.g. for diagnostic service $19)

Disabling NV storage for this data element will cause the Dem to fetch the data with each read access. This is useful if this data element is something that will not change over time, or is already stored outside the Dem.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. DemDataElementDataType

- **UUID**: `37013dce-9ebb-4e5e-8dc9-4f5746bb979d`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementDataType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BOOLEAN`
  - `SINT16`
  - `SINT32`
  - `SINT8`
  - `SINT8_N`
  - `UINT16`
  - `UINT32`
  - `UINT8`
  - `UINT8_N`
  - `UINT16_N`
  - `SINT16_N`
  - `UINT32_N`
  - `SINT32_N`
  - `FLOAT`
  - `FLOAT_N`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Provide the implementation data type of data belonging to a external data.

For DemDataElementDataSize > 1 the configured data type UINT8/SINT8 is treated as UINT8_N/SINT8_N to establish compatibility with the DCM.

########## 2. DemDataElementEndianness

- **UUID**: `e1ac5655-cd09-457a-91be-7b067a2aba1d`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementEndianness`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BIG_ENDIAN`
  - `LITTLE_ENDIAN`
  - `OPAQUE`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the endianness of the data belonging to an external data.

########## 3. DemDataElementInternalData

- **UUID**: `1452433a-887f-4d98-9622-6a95bab8d169`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementInternalData`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_AGINGCTR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGINGCTR_UPCNT`
  - `DEM_AGINGCTR_INVERTED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGINGCTR_DOWNCNT`
  - `DEM_OCCCTR`
  - `DEM_OCCCTR_2BYTE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OVFLIND`
  - `DEM_SIGNIFICANCE`
  - `DEM_PRIORITY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_CURRENT_FDC`
  - `DEM_MAX_FDC_SINCE_LAST_CLEAR`
  - `DEM_MAX_FDC_DURING_CURRENT_CYCLE`
  - `DEM_CYCLES_SINCE_FIRST_FAILED`
  - `DEM_CYCLES_SINCE_LAST_FAILED`
  - `DEM_FAILED_CYCLES`
  - `DEM_CONSECUTIVE_FAILED_CYCLES` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_CYCLES_TESTED_SINCE_FIRST_FAILED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_CYCLES_TESTED_SINCE_LAST_FAILED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_HEALINGCTR_DOWNCNT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_ROOTCAUSE_EVENTID` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDDTC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDDTC_3BYTE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBD_RATIO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_FAULT_PENDING_COUNTER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGED_COUNTER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_WUC_SINCE_LAST_FAILED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_SI30_STATUS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_IUMPR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_DTR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_MONITOR_ACTIVITY_DATA` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Select the Dem-internal data value, which is mapped to the data element.

AGINGCTR / AGINGCTR_UPCNT: (1 Byte) Current value of the aging counter, counting from 1...DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold. The value latches at aging counter threshold (refer to DemGeneral/DemAgingCounterBehavior).
If DemGeneral/DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs is disabled, this value is processed and readable only for currently stored events.
If DemGeneral/DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs is enabled, depending on the configuration, this value is always readable (refer to Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility).

AGINGCTR_INVERTED / AGINGCTR_DOWNCNT: (1 Byte) Current value of the aging counter, counting backwards from the configured aging cycle counter threshold value down to 0. If DemGeneral/DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs is disabled, this value is processed and readable only for currently stored events.
If DemGeneral/DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs is enabled, depending on the configuration, this value is always readable (refer to Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility).

OCCCTR: (1 Byte) Current value of the occurrence counter, counting from 1...255. The value is incremented on each transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 and latches at 255. This value is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

OCCCTR_2BYTE: (2 Byte) Current value of the occurrence counter, counting from 1...65535. The value is incremented on each transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 and latches at 65535. This value is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

OVFLIND: (1 Byte) Value 1 if the primary memory has overflown, 0 otherwise. Will be reset on ClearAllDTC request. This value is always available.

SIGNIFICANCE: (1 Byte) Value 0 if the event is configured as OCCURRENCE, 1 if the event is configured as FAILURE. This value is always available.

PRIORITY: (1 Byte) The DTC priority which is mapped into the environmental data. This value is always available.

CURRENT_FDC: (1 Byte) Current value of the DTC fault detection counter. This value is always available.

MAX_FDC_SINCE_LAST_CLEAR: (1 Byte) Maximum value of the DTC fault detection counter reached since last clear. 0 is the minimum value reported. 
If Dem/DemGeneral/DemSupportStorageIndependentMaxFDCSinceLastClear is not enabled the data element is processed and readable only for currently stored events. Otherwise it is processed and readable at all times (see also Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility).

MAX_FDC_DURING_CURRENT_CYCLE: (1 Byte) Maximum value of the DTC fault detection counter reached in the current operation cycle. 0 is the minimum value reported. This value is always available.

CYCLES_SINCE_FIRST_FAILED: (1 Byte) Number of completed operation cycles, since the cycle of the first transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 of the DTC. The value latches at 255. The counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

CYCLES_SINCE_LAST_FAILED: (1 Byte) Number of completed operation cycles, since the last transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 of the DTC. The value is reset at the first failed result in the operation cycle, latches at 255. The counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

FAILED_CYCLES: (1 Byte) Number of operation cycles, since last DTC clear in which the DTC was tested 'failed', including the current cycle. The value is incremented at the first failed result in the operation cycle, latches at 255.
If Dem/DemGeneral/DemSupportStorageIndependentCycleCounters is not enabled the counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events. Otherwise the counter is processed and readable at all times (see also Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility).

CONSECUTIVE_FAILED_CYCLES: (1 Byte): Number of consecutive operation cycles in which the DTC was tested 'failed', including the current cycle. The value is incremented at the first failed result in the operation cycle and reset at the end of an operation cycle that was tested and not tested 'failed'.The value latches at 255. The counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

CYCLES_TESTED_SINCE_FIRST_FAILED: (1 Byte) Number of completed and tested operation cycles since the cycle of the first transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 of the DTC. The value latches at 255. The counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

CYCLES_TESTED_SINCE_LAST_FAILED: (1 Byte) Number of completed and tested operation cycles, since the last transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 of the DTC (Healing  Counter). The value is reset at the first failed result in the operation cycle, latches at 255. The value is always available.

HEALINGCTR_DOWNCNT: (1 Byte) Number of complete and tested operation cycles until WIR bit will be cleared (independently of whether Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemIndicatorAttribute/DemIndicatorRef is set or not). If healing has not started yet, Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemIndicatorAttribute/DemIndicatorHealingCycleCounterThreshold is reported. If healing is completed, 0 is reported. The value is always available.

ROOTCAUSE_EVENTID: (2 Byte) The event id that caused the storage/update of the environmental data. Can be used in context of the feature 'combined event' to store the root cause DemEventId value. The value is processed and readable only for currently stored events

OBDDTC: (2 Byte) The corresponding OBD II DTC for the requested UDS DTC. If no OBD II DTC is configured, the returned value will be 0. This value is always available.

OBDDTC_3BYTE: (3 Byte) 
In OBDII configurations: The corresponding OBD II DTC for the requested UDS DTC in format [0x00 LoByte HiByte].
In OBD on UDS configurations: The corresponding 3 byte OBD DTC for the requested UDS DTC, if DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemSupportedObdUdsDtcSeparation == true.
If no OBD II DTC resp. 3 byte OBD DTC is configured, the returned value will be 0. This value is always available.

OBD_RATIO:  (4 Byte) If the DTC has a ratio attached, the numerator and denominator are returned in the following format: [NumHiByte NumLoByte DenomHiByte DenomLoByte].
If the DTC does not have a ratio attached, the returned value is [0x00 0x00 0x00 0x00]. This value is always available.

FAULT_PENDING_COUNTER: (1 Byte) Number of consecutive operation cycles in which the DTC was tested 'failed', including the current cycle. The value is incremented at the first failed result in the operation cycle and reset at the end of an operation cycle that was tested and not tested 'failed'. In contrast to the 'Consecutive Failed Cycle Counter' the value latches at DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold + 1.
If Dem/DemGeneral/DemSupportStorageIndependentCycleCounters is not enabled the counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events. Otherwise the counter is processed and readable at all times (see also Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility).

AGED_COUNTER: (1 Byte) Current value of the aged counter. The value is incremented, if the DTC is aged and reset, if the DTC is cleared. The value latches at 255 and is always available.

WUC_SINCE_LAST_FAILED: (1 Byte) Number of completed warm up cycles, since the last transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 of the DTC. The value latches at 255. The counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

SI30_STATUS: (1 Byte) Extended status byte. The extended status is calculated and is always available.

IUMPR: (4 Byte) The numerator and denominator of the ratio of a DTC are returned in the following format: [NumHiByte NumLoByte DenomHiByte DenomLoByte].
This value can be used to configure extended data record number 0x91 according to SAE J1979-2. The value is only available, if OBD on UDS is supported (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation).

DTR: (1 Byte for the number of test results, 8 Byte for each test result) The Diagnostic Test Results (DTRs) of a DTC are returned in the following format: [NumberOfTestResults] and for each test result: [UaSIdHiByte UaSIdLoByte TestValueHiByte TestValueLoByte MinTestLimitHiByte MinTestLimitLoByte MaxTestLimitHiByte MaxTestLimitLoByte].
The DTRs are returned in ascending order by Monitor Identifier (MID) and Test Identifier (TID).
The size of this internal data element varies for each DTC based on the number of DTRs referencing the corresponding event. Due to this variability, size must be configured to
the maximum supported value of 249 bytes (Dem supports a maximum of 31 DTRs with 8 byte each plus 1 byte for the number of test results, see Dem/DemGeneral/DemDataClass/DemDataElementDataSize). The actual size is calculated at runtime.
This value can be used to configure extended data record number 0x92 according to SAE J1979-2. The value is only available, if OBD on UDS is supported (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation).

MONITOR_ACTIVITY_DATA: (2 Byte) The monitor activity numerator (MAN) and stored monitor activity ratio (SMAR) of a DTC are returned in the following format: [MAN SMAR].
This value can be used to configure extended data record number 0x93 according to SAE J1979-2. The value is only available, if OBD on UDS is supported (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation).

########## 4. DemDataElementUsePort

- **UUID**: `49751469-5009-4eef-abcb-c9d51e01efaf`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementUsePort`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `USE_DATA_CLIENT_SERVER_PORT`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `USE_DATA_CLIENT_SERVER_PORT`
  - `USE_DATA_CLIENT_SERVER_PORT_WITH_EVENTID`
  - `USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL`
  - `USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_EVENTID`
  - `USE_DATA_SENDER_RECEIVER`
  - `USE_DATA_INTERNAL`
  - `USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_EVENTID_BUFFERLENGTH`
  - `USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_PROXY`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines which interface shall be used to access the data.

For data calculated internally by the Dem, use
USE_DATA_INTERNAL

For data provided by the application through a client/server port, use
USE_DATA_CLIENT_SERVER_PORT
USE_DATA_CLIENT_SERVER_PORT_WITH_EVENTID

For data provided by the application through a C function-callback, use
USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL
USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_EVENTID
USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_EVENTID_BUFFERLENGTH
USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_PROXY

For data provided by the application through a sender/receiver port, use
USE_DATA_SENDER_RECEIVER

######### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

########## 1. DemDataElementReadFnc

- **UUID**: `bd1edc58-fe39-4b68-a5fe-36df2439b678`

- **描述**: In case of DemDataElementUsePort is USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL, USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_EVENTID, USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_EVENTID_BUFFERLENGTH or USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_PROXY,  this parameter defines the prototype of the C function "ReadDataElement" used to get the according value.

####### 15. DemEnableCondition

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `2128e56a-252f-417e-a437-9069960235fb`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemEnableCondition`
- **多重性**: [0..254]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents a single enable condition.

Please note: enable conditions can not be used directly but must be grouped (see DemEnableConditionGroup).

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemEnableConditionId

- **UUID**: `35a21b4d-8001-4fe6-954f-61cb5021c375`
- **参数名**: `DemEnableConditionId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `255`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Internal identifier of the enable condition.

This number is calculated internally and shall not be configured manually. This parameter is only available to publish the result of this calculation.
Applications using Dem APIs must not rely on the value of this parameter. Instead, they must use the symbolic constant (SymbolicNameValue) DemConf_DemEnableCondition_{SHORT-NAME}.

The ID value 0 is reserved for internal purpose.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. DemEnableConditionStatus

- **UUID**: `da12f486-9f80-40ae-85ad-3c4f0e8d11f0`
- **参数名**: `DemEnableConditionStatus`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the status of this enable condition after full initialization, i.e. when Dem_MasterInit() or Dem_Init() finished.
After pre-initialization, but before the full intialization (between Dem_(Master)PreInit() and Dem_(Master)Init()) the status is always 'true'.
Changes to the enable condition status during runtime are not persisted non-volatile. The status of this enable condition will always be reset to this configuration value during Dem_MasterInit() or Dem_Init().

######### 2. DemPrimaryMemory

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `163fd33f-f576-42b9-a3ca-595772b034f7`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemPrimaryMemory`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the primary event memory specific parameters
of the Dem module.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. DemMaxNumberEventEntryPrimary

- **UUID**: `8013129e-5b27-4b65-8850-9ec24040245e`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberEventEntryPrimary`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Maximum number of events which can be stored in the primary memory

############ 2. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots

- **UUID**: `ff35b89e-bca3-4a49-a0dc-841f5740944a`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which time series snapshot records (also known as multi-sample development snapshot records) can be stored at the same time for this event memory.

############ 3. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets

- **UUID**: `14329618-ad64-4ca9-9d85-6f5aba197932`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..16]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The maximum number of time series snapshot record sets that can be stored for an event assigned to this memory. If the parameter is missing, only one set will be stored.

A set of time series snapshot records consists of all past and future samples that are stored for an event after the freeze frame storage trigger has occurred. With each re-occurrence of the trigger, a new set can be stored. 
When the maximum number of time series snapshot record sets has been stored, the new set may overwrite the oldest set depending on the value of DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport.

For more information on how the time series snapshot records of the different sets are numbered, refer to DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset.

############ 4. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset

- **UUID**: `4836c4be-dace-47f8-903a-fd99c667c38d`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [1..32]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This offset is used to calculate the first record number of all subsequent sets of time series snapshots that are stored for an event (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets).

This parameter only has an effect if DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1. 

The first set of time series snapshot record numbers starts at the freeze frame recod number (see DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber) configured for the time series snapshot record (see DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries).
If DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1, the records numbers of the subsequent sets will start at:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (1 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (2 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
...
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (n* DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset), where n equals (DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets -1).

Example:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber is set to 0x80,
- DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset is set to 0x08 and
- DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets equals 3,
the different sets of time series snapshot records stored for an event will start at record number 
- 0x80,
- 0x88 and
- 0x90.

############ 5. DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots

- **UUID**: `c61a6001-1066-4435-8e95-edcbe5905593`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which custom triggered snapshot records and/or custom triggered time series snapshot records can be stored at the same time for this event memory.
A snapshot record or time series snapshot record is custom triggered if the corresponding DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger is configured to DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CUSTOM.

########### BOOLEAN 类型参数

############ 1. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport

- **UUID**: `21b080ce-de0d-4f00-9f67-eb0d25c137bc`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines if the time series snapshot record sets of events assigned to this memory are stored in a circular buffer.

If the switch is enabled and the configured maximum number of time series snapshot record sets (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets) is stored for an event, the oldest set of time series snapshot records will be overwritten with each new storage trigger occurrence. Correspondingly, all time series snapshot records of the event will be renumbered in ascending order from oldest to newest.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. DemTypeOfFreezeFrameRecordNumeration

- **UUID**: `53cd6f3a-158d-405e-8cfc-42eb1255c057`
- **参数名**: `DemTypeOfFreezeFrameRecordNumeration`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CONFIGURED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED_FIFO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the type of assigning snapshot record numbers.

CALCULATED: The snapshot records are enumerated from one (1) up to DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords for each event. Once an event memory entry is created a snapshot is stored at each FAILED transition. After DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords have been stored for the event, the last snapshot is updated with each FAILED transition.

CALCULATED_FIFO: The snapshot records are enumerated from one (1) up to DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords for each event. Once an event memory entry is created, a snapshot is stored at each FAILED transition of the event. After DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords have been stored for the event, the oldest snapshot is overwritten during the next trigger. When a snapshot is overwritten, all snapshot records are renumbered in ascending order from oldest to newest snapshot record. 

CONFIGURED: The snapshot record numbers are taken from DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemFreezeFrameRecNumClassRef. Storage triggers and update conditions can be configured.

############ 2. DemEventMemoryEntryStorageTrigger

- **UUID**: `173382ee-e393-4cba-9c88-a6b7d7f7b570`
- **参数名**: `DemEventMemoryEntryStorageTrigger`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_STORAGE_ON_TEST_FAILED`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_CONFIRMED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_FDC_THRESHOLD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_PENDING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_TEST_FAILED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter describes the trigger for the allocation of an event memory entry, i.e. the point of time when the corresponding DTC is initially stored in the referenced event memory.

CONFIRMED: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC confirms (bit3 transitions 0->1).

FDC_THRESHOLD: An event entry is allocated once the fault detection counter of its corresponding DTC exceeds its storage threshold.

PENDING: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC gets pending (bit2 transitions 0->1).

TEST_FAILED: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC fails (bit0 transitions 0->1).

Restriction: If FDC_THRESHOLD is selected, monitor internal debouncing cannot be used to store event data at pre-failed.

- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberEventEntryUserDefined`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE

############ 2. DemUserDefinedMemoryIdentifier

- **UUID**: `ed05c932-9808-4eca-9112-746c702ca3b6`
- **参数名**: `DemUserDefinedMemoryIdentifier`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Identifier used by external tester to identify the User defined event memory

############ 3. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots

- **UUID**: `72093ee5-8ea8-4393-aeb4-c5c43449899f`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which time series snapshot records (also known as multi-sample development snapshot records) can be stored at the same time for this event memory.

############ 4. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets

- **UUID**: `f07755d4-d56d-4249-ba6f-8b1ff4af77e3`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..16]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The maximum number of time series snapshot record sets that can be stored for an event assigned to this memory. If the parameter is missing, only one set will be stored.

A set of time series snapshot records consists of all past and future samples that are stored for an event after the freeze frame storage trigger has occurred. With each re-occurrence of the trigger, a new set can be stored. 
When the maximum number of time series snapshot record sets has been stored, the new set may overwrite the oldest set depending on the value of DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport.

For more information on how the time series snapshot records of the different sets are numbered, refer to DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset.

############ 5. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset

- **UUID**: `975b5994-8965-42d8-8041-539e15c8f8c4`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [1..32]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This offset is used to calculate the first record number of all subsequent sets of time series snapshots that are stored for an event (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets).

This parameter only has an effect if DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1. 

The first set of time series snapshot record numbers starts at the freeze frame recod number (see DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber) configured for the time series snapshot record (see DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries).
If DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1, the records numbers of the subsequent sets will start at:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (1 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (2 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
...
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (n* DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset), where n equals (DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets -1).

Example:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber is set to 0x80,
- DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset is set to 0x08 and
- DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets equals 3,
the different sets of time series snapshot records stored for an event will start at record number 
- 0x80,
- 0x88 and
- 0x90.

############ 6. DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots

- **UUID**: `10ec5f02-9f90-460d-8b29-4796621bcf12`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which custom triggered snapshot records and/or custom triggered time series snapshot records can be stored at the same time for this event memory.
A snapshot record or time series snapshot record is custom triggered if the corresponding DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger is configured to DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CUSTOM.

############ 1. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines if the time series snapshot record sets of events assigned to this memory are stored in a circular buffer.

If the switch is enabled and the configured maximum number of time series snapshot record sets (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets) is stored for an event, the oldest set of time series snapshot records will be overwritten with each new storage trigger occurrence. Correspondingly, all time series snapshot records of the event will be renumbered in ascending order from oldest to newest.

- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CONFIGURED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED_FIFO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the type of assigning snapshot record numbers.

CALCULATED: The snapshot records are enumerated from one (1) up to DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords for each event. Once an event memory entry is created a snapshot is stored at each FAILED transition. After DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords have been stored for the event, the last snapshot is updated with each FAILED transition.

CALCULATED_FIFO: The snapshot records are enumerated from one (1) up to DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords for each event. Once an event memory entry is created, a snapshot is stored at each FAILED transition of the event. After DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords have been stored for the event, the oldest snapshot is overwritten during the next trigger. When a snapshot is overwritten, all snapshot records are renumbered in ascending order from oldest to newest snapshot record. 

CONFIGURED: The snapshot record numbers are taken from DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemFreezeFrameRecNumClassRef. Storage triggers and update conditions can be configured.

########## 2. DemEventMemoryEntryStorageTrigger

- **UUID**: `173382ee-e393-4cba-9c88-a6b7d7f7b570`
- **参数名**: `DemEventMemoryEntryStorageTrigger`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_STORAGE_ON_TEST_FAILED`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_CONFIRMED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_FDC_THRESHOLD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_PENDING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_TEST_FAILED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter describes the trigger for the allocation of an event memory entry, i.e. the point of time when the corresponding DTC is initially stored in the referenced event memory.

CONFIRMED: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC confirms (bit3 transitions 0->1).

FDC_THRESHOLD: An event entry is allocated once the fault detection counter of its corresponding DTC exceeds its storage threshold.

PENDING: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC gets pending (bit2 transitions 0->1).

TEST_FAILED: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC fails (bit0 transitions 0->1).

Restriction: If FDC_THRESHOLD is selected, monitor internal debouncing cannot be used to store event data at pre-failed.

########## 3. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots

- **UUID**: `72093ee5-8ea8-4393-aeb4-c5c43449899f`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which time series snapshot records (also known as multi-sample development snapshot records) can be stored at the same time for this event memory.

########## 4. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets

- **UUID**: `f07755d4-d56d-4249-ba6f-8b1ff4af77e3`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..16]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The maximum number of time series snapshot record sets that can be stored for an event assigned to this memory. If the parameter is missing, only one set will be stored.

A set of time series snapshot records consists of all past and future samples that are stored for an event after the freeze frame storage trigger has occurred. With each re-occurrence of the trigger, a new set can be stored. 
When the maximum number of time series snapshot record sets has been stored, the new set may overwrite the oldest set depending on the value of DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport.

For more information on how the time series snapshot records of the different sets are numbered, refer to DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset.

########## 5. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset

- **UUID**: `975b5994-8965-42d8-8041-539e15c8f8c4`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [1..32]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This offset is used to calculate the first record number of all subsequent sets of time series snapshots that are stored for an event (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets).

This parameter only has an effect if DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1. 

The first set of time series snapshot record numbers starts at the freeze frame recod number (see DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber) configured for the time series snapshot record (see DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries).
If DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1, the records numbers of the subsequent sets will start at:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (1 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (2 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
...
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (n* DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset), where n equals (DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets -1).

Example:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber is set to 0x80,
- DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset is set to 0x08 and
- DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets equals 3,
the different sets of time series snapshot records stored for an event will start at record number 
- 0x80,
- 0x88 and
- 0x90.

########## 6. DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots

- **UUID**: `10ec5f02-9f90-460d-8b29-4796621bcf12`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which custom triggered snapshot records and/or custom triggered time series snapshot records can be stored at the same time for this event memory.
A snapshot record or time series snapshot record is custom triggered if the corresponding DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger is configured to DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CUSTOM.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport

- **UUID**: `4fdd3812-b95c-456b-8291-8200631b6451`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines if the time series snapshot record sets of events assigned to this memory are stored in a circular buffer.

If the switch is enabled and the configured maximum number of time series snapshot record sets (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets) is stored for an event, the oldest set of time series snapshot records will be overwritten with each new storage trigger occurrence. Correspondingly, all time series snapshot records of the event will be renumbered in ascending order from oldest to newest.

Once an event memory entry is created a snapshot is stored at each FAILED transition. After DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords have been stored for the event, the last snapshot is updated with each FAILED transition.

- **描述**: The unique identifier of the extended data record as requested and reported by $19 06.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. DemExtendedDataRecordTrigger

- **UUID**: `b831b4e5-a28c-4380-940f-e4230acef120`
- **参数名**: `DemExtendedDataRecordTrigger`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_TEST_FAILED`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_TEST_FAILED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_FDC_FIRST_IN_CYCLE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CONFIRMED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_PASSED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the trigger to store and update ExtendedDataRecords, if configured.

FDC_FIRST_IN_CYCLE: The extended data record capture/ update is triggered by the event's fault detection counter exceeding the configured storage threshold. This trigger is only processed once per operationcycle.

TEST_FAILED: The  extended data record capture/ update is triggered by the TestFailed bit transition from 0 to 1.

CONFIRMED: The  extended data record capture is triggered by the Confirmed bit transition from 0 to 1.

PASSED: The  extended data record capture/ update is triggered by a qualified 'Passed' report that resets the TestFailed bit from 1 to 0.

########## 2. DemExtendedDataRecordUpdate

- **UUID**: `0ff32bc7-6f9d-401f-a73e-433edbd7910e`
- **参数名**: `DemExtendedDataRecordUpdate`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_NO` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if this record is captured only once (UPDATE_RECORD_NO) or updated with each following trigger ((UPDATE_RECORD_YES).

Please note that internal data elements are updated continuously and can not be frozen to their value at the time of confirmation.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines a record number for a freeze frame record. 
This record number is unique per freeze frame record number class.

In case that DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries is enabled, this parameter represents the first record number of the time series snapshot records.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries

- **UUID**: `9b122b9d-0e08-4112-b49e-6ad5591b0ccb`
- **参数名**: `DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines whether the record is used as a time series snapshot record.

If this parameter is enabled, DemTimeSeriesSnapshotClassRef must contain a valid reference.

If this parameter is disabled or not configured, the record is used as a configured freeze frame record and DemTimeSeriesSnapshotClassRef is ignored.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger

- **UUID**: `5cd824ea-efbe-458f-bcd9-ccbaff09abfe`
- **参数名**: `DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_FDC_THRESHOLD` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CONFIRMED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_PENDING` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_TEST_FAILED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_TEST_FAILED_FIRST_IN_CYCLE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_FDC_FIRST_IN_CYCLE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_PASSED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CUSTOM` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the trigger to store the FreezeFrameRecord.

FDC_THRESHOLD: The snapshot data capture is triggered by a fault detection counter passing a storage threshold.

CONFIRMED: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the ConfirmedDTC transition 0 -> 1.

PENDING: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the PendingDTC transition 0 -> 1.

TEST_FAILED: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the TestFailed transition 0 -> 1.

TEST_FAILED_FIRST_IN_CYCLE: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the first TestFailed transition 0 -> 1 in an operation cycle (i.e TestFailedThisOperationCycle transition 0 -> 1).

FDC_FIRST_IN_CYCLE: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the first DTC fault detection counter reaching the storage threshold in an operation cycle.

PASSED: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the TestFailed transition 1 -> 0.

CUSTOM: The snapshot data capture is triggered by API Dem_StoreCustomTriggeredFreezeFrame(). An event can only use this snapshot trigger, when the corresponding event memory has DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots > 0


Please note:
For all triggers except CUSTOM, snapshot trigger occuring before an event memory entry is allocated are postponed until the event memory entry gets allocated.
Snapshot trigger CUSTOM can capture snapshot data even before an event memory entry is allocated to the event.

########## 2. DemFreezeFrameRecordUpdate

- **UUID**: `7df03bce-c9ad-44a7-ae40-478bcd85c0a0`
- **参数名**: `DemFreezeFrameRecordUpdate`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_NO` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the case, when the freeze frame record is stored/updated.

DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_NO - The snapshot record is captured only once.

DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES - The snapshot is captured with each trigger, overwriting a previously captured snapshot.

If option DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES is chosen for a snapshot record with DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger CONFIRMED the snapshot record is initally stored on ConfirmedDTC transition 0 -> 1 and updated with each following TestFailed transition 0 -> 1.

If option DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES is chosen for a time series snapshot record (see DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries) all past samples are overwritten and future samples are collected anew with each trigger.

- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the combinations of SPNs s for a J1939 relevant freeze frame.

Your configuration must use one (1) common definition for all J1939 freeze frames and one (1) common definition for all J1939 expanded freeze frames (use the event parameters DemJ1939FreezeFrameClassRef and DemJ1939ExpandedFreezeFrameClassRef to use this definitions). Therefore the multiplicity of this parameter is limited to 2.

Therefore the multiplicity of this parameter is limited to 2.

This container will be used only, if the OBD support (for either OBD II or WWH-OBD or OBD on UDS or ZEV on UDS) is activated (see DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport and DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation).

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **30** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemOBDCompliancy

- **UUID**: `10ab62bd-ca23-44b3-bbc9-6b8268f6e2dd`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDCompliancy`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Configuration value to define the appropriate value to PID$1C "OBD requirements to which vehicle or engine is certified." according to the respective standards, e.g. OBD I, OBD II, JOBD etc.

########## 2. DemOBDPermanentDtcActivationDistance

- **UUID**: `025b6420-804d-4557-913f-9914fb9aac23`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDPermanentDtcActivationDistance`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
Distance after first valid Odometer value was provided after which permanent DTC storage is activated.

The resolution is the same as for PID$21 and PID$31 (see parameter DemOBDInputDistanceInformation).

Providing this parameter will also enable an API to activate permanent DTC storage before the configured distance is reached.

If this parameter is missing, permanent DTC storage is always active and can't be suppressed.

########## 3. DemOBDPermanentChronologyMaxNumberEvents

- **UUID**: `ca228b3b-0d8a-40b0-9c29-94af5d2b1838`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDPermanentChronologyMaxNumberEvents`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Maximum number of events which can be stored in the chronology of confirmed events. 

This chronology is used to identify the next DTC to be stored in the permanent memory, if the permanent memory was full and an entry gets freed.
Each event in the chronology adds two more bytes to the Dem's status NvRAM block.

It is recommended to configure a value that corresponds to the maximum number of DTCs fulfilling the criteria for permanent storage at the same time in a (realistic) worst case scenario. 
To ensure that always the oldest confirmed DTC is stored in the permanent memory or if NvRam consumption is not a critical restriction, set the value to the maximum number of DTCs having the MIL attached (OBDII or OBDonUDS) or the maximum number of class A and B1 DTCs (WWHOBD).

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 4. DemDelayWarmUpCycleToDcyEnd

- **UUID**: `d5c8fca2-d718-4822-beef-a6dd55bb448c`
- **参数名**: `DemDelayWarmUpCycleToDcyEnd`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If enabled the DEM can only be triggered for the start of warm-Up cycle and it will ignore the trigger for the end of warm-up cycle. 
It will automatically restart the warm-up cycle at the end of a (qualified) driving cycle. 

Therefore the DTCs that have configured the warm-up cycle as their aging cycle will age at the end of the driving cycle.

########## 5. DemOBDFreezeFrameInService19

- **UUID**: `7b2af849-0cac-49ec-ba44-80c210aedfed`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDFreezeFrameInService19`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II legislation.
Report the OBD II freeze frame in UDS Service $19 04 (with record number 0x00 or wildcard 0xFF).

If enabled the OBD II Freeze Frame can be accessed in diagnostic service $19 04 and in Mode 02
otherwise only in Mode 02.

########## 6. DemOBDHideOccurrences

- **UUID**: `74b28e70-a283-4e95-9c46-9b9479259d47`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDHideOccurrences`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
Dependent OBD DTCs shall not appear in some UDS $19 sub-services.

If enabled, the Dem hides dependent OBD DTCs from reporting.
Also, operation 'HideOccurrences' in service 'DemOBDServices' is provided to change this behavior at run-time.
Dependent OBD DTCs are identified via the parameter 'DemEventSignificance'. A setting of 'OCCURRENCE' marks a dependent DTC.

Note: Non-OBD DTCs are not affected by this feature.

########## 7. DemOBDPermanentEraseConditionDcy

- **UUID**: `4a3211e5-041f-4c92-aeb5-924493ee2944`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDPermanentEraseConditionDcy`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
This parameter controls the behavior of a permanent fault after a clear DTC command issued while the OBD driving cycle (DCY) is not yet qualified.

If enabled, the Dem checks the DCY qualification state during a Clear DTC request.
- DCY not qualified: The permanent fault code can be removed from permanent memory in the same DCY as the clear DTC request was issued.
- DCY qualified: The permanent fault code cannot be removed from permanent memory in the same DCY as the clear DTC request was issued.

If disabled, the DCY qualification state has no effect.

Note: To remove a DTC from permanent memory the PFC condition must be fulfilled and the DTC must be tested as passed and not failed.

########## 8. DemOBDPermanentMandatoryPfcAfterClear

- **UUID**: `0c99d3d4-6860-41be-aeff-d72ce66d38ea`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDPermanentMandatoryPfcAfterClear`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
This configuration switch specifies, when a DTC will be removed from the permanent memory after the fault memory was cleared (e.g. Mode $04) and the DTC illuminates the MIL again.

If this option is enabled, the DTC will be removed from permanent memory only if PFC cycle was active and MIL becomes inactive for this DTC.

If this option is disabled, the DTC will be removed from permanent memory after the MIL becomes inactive for this event.

########## 9. DemOBDRestartDcyOnClearDTC

- **UUID**: `fd16d8f9-8e06-491d-8ff5-21b442ae18a2`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDRestartDcyOnClearDTC`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: With ClearDTC (Mode $04 or Service $14FFFFFF) the DEM may restart the OBD driving cycle independent of its current qualification state.

If enabled, the DEM restarts the OBD driving cycle (configured as DemOperationCycleType==DEM_OPCYC_OBD_DCY) with Mode $04 or Service $14 FFFFFF requests.

If disabled, no cycle is restarted with Mode $04 or Service $14 FFFFFF requests.

########## 13. DemReportBlinkingMilAsActive

- **UUID**: `5e810727-ccac-416a-9297-e4441885be90`
- **参数名**: `DemReportBlinkingMilAsActive`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If enabled, the DEM reports the MIL as 'activated' if its current state is BLINKING and/or CONTINUOUS.
If disabled, the DEM reports the MIL as 'activated' only if its current state is CONTINUOUS (see parameter Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemIndicatorAttribute/DemIndicatorBehaviour).

This influences the behavior of PID $01, PID $F501, PID $21 and PID $4D.

- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This switch describes whether the completeness status for fuel system monitors is calculated or always indicated as complete in:
- PID $01,
- PID $41,
- PID $F501.
- J1939 diagnostic readiness 1 and
- J1939 diagnostic readiness 3.

True: Fuel system monitoring is always reported as complete.
False: Completeness of fuel system monitoring is calculated based on non-continuous fuel system monitor status.

########## 15. DemOBDCompressionMisfireAlwaysComplete

- **UUID**: `0d3b0c2a-5e83-410d-8e67-460eda73eb2b`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDCompressionMisfireAlwaysComplete`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This switch describes whether the completeness status for misfire monitors is calculated or always indicated as complete for compression ignition engines (DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDEngineType == DEM_IGNITION_COMPRESSION) in
- PID $01,
- PID $41,
- J1939 diagnostic readiness 1 and
- J1939 diagnostic readiness 3.

In the data contexts above, misfire monitors will always indicate completed for spark ignition engines independently of this parameter value.

For PID $F501, the completion of readiness group Misfire is always calculated.

True: Misfire monitoring is always reported as complete.
False: Completeness of misfire monitors is calculated.

########## 16. DemOBDPermanentDtcImmediateReport

- **UUID**: `caad3e6a-318f-4c93-923e-c2d0f1a3c83e`

- **来源**: Vector Informatik

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
TRUE: All Permanent Dtcs are available via OBD  mode 0A immediately after WIR bit is visible
FALSE: Only DTCs for which /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification is disabled and the /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold is configured to 0 are visible in mode 0A immediately, all other DTCs only after OBD driving cycle is restarted.

########## 19. DemSupportedObdUdsDtcSeparation

- **UUID**: `d6a63781-a28e-413b-bf17-15a7f005839a`
- **参数名**: `DemSupportedObdUdsDtcSeparation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBDonUDS legislation.

When this parameter is set to true, the OBD UDS DTC separation feature is enabled, i.e. the configured 3 byte OBD DTC value (DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemObdDTC3Byte) is reported in all OBDonUDS related services (see SAE J1979-2).
If this parameter is set to false, the OBD UDS DTC separation is disabled, i.e. the UDS DTC value (DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemUdsDTC) is reported instead. This is the default behavior.

- **描述**: This switch allows the user to disable event monitoring in current driving cycle. 
Usage of the API Dem_DisableEventMonitoringInCurrentDcy() will make the event be considered as tested completed during the readiness calculation of the following readiness data:

- PID $01,
- PID $F501.
- DM05.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. DemEngineRuntimePolling

- **UUID**: `fa17ad0f-050e-4f66-ae24-4b517675627e`
- **参数名**: `DemEngineRuntimePolling`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `60`
- **取值范围**: [0.001..2678400]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Allow to configure the time for the periodic polling of the engine runtime in minutes.

The value must be a multiple of the DemGeneral/DemTaskTime. Otherwise it is rounded down to the next multiple value of DemTaskTime.

This value is mandatory, when parameter DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStart is required. If missing, it defaults to the DemTaskTime.

The AUTOSAR configuration standard is to use SI units, so in the EcuC file this parameter is stored as float value in seconds - the DaVinci Configurator 5 GUI will convert the value when using a diffenent unit.

########## 2. DemEngineRuntimeSecondsPolling

- **UUID**: `8f873a6d-21c4-44ef-b809-ed7273208466`
- **参数名**: `DemEngineRuntimeSecondsPolling`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `60`
- **取值范围**: [0.001..2678400]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Allow to configure the time for the periodic polling of the engine runtime in seconds.

The value must be a multiple of the DemGeneral/DemTaskTime. Otherwise it is rounded down to the next multiple value of DemTaskTime.

This value is mandatory, when parameter DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStartSeconds is required. If missing, it defaults to the DemTaskTime.

The AUTOSAR configuration standard is to use SI units, so in the EcuC file this parameter is stored as float value in seconds - the DaVinci Configurator 5 GUI will convert the value when using a diffenent unit.

########## 3. DemOBDFreezeFrameBehavior

- **UUID**: `de28e936-2584-4c24-b250-7ebbc14a01fc`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDFreezeFrameBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_OBDII_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDII_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDII_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE3` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDII_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE4` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDII_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE5` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_WWHOBD_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_WWHOBD_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_COMBINED_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_COMBINED_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This configuration value defines, when the OBD II resp. WWH-OBD Freeze Frame is taken, stored and updated and when/how it is visible for diagnostic requests.

DemOBDFreezeFrameBehavior has no effect on OBDonUDS Freeze Frame storage.The OBDonUDS FF is always stored when trigger conditions are met.

OBD II, TYPE 1: OBD II Freeze Frame data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
If PendingDTC is healed without being ConfirmedDTC, the Freeze Frame will be cleared.
The Freeze Frame is immediately visible in Mode02.

OBD II, TYPE 2: OBD II Freeze Frame data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
If PendingDTC is healed without being ConfirmedDTC, the Freeze Frame will be cleared.
The Freeze Frame is visible as soon as the ConfirmedDTC status bit of the DTC is visible. The visibility depends on parameter /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification. If this parameter is enabled, visibility is delayed until the OBD driving cycle is qualified.
This option allows multiple Freeze Frames (see parameter /DemGeneral/DemMaxNumberEventEntryOBDFreezeFrame).

OBD II, TYPE 3: OBD II Freeze Frame data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
The Pending Freeze Frame will be displaced if another DTC becomes a ConfirmedDTC.
If PendingDTC is healed without being ConfirmedDTC the Freeze Frame will be cleared.
The Freeze Frame is immediately visible in Mode02.

OBD II, TYPE 4: OBD II Freeze Frame  data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
The Pending Freeze Frame will be updated if the DTC becomes a ConfirmedDTC.
If PendingDTC is healed without being ConfirmedDTC the Freeze Frame will be cleared.
The Freeze Frame is immediately visible in Mode02.

OBD II, TYPE 5: OBD II Freeze Frame data capture is triggered when the trip condition is fulfilled.
The Freeze Frame is visible as soon as the ConfirmedDTC status bit of the DTC is visible. The visibility depends on parameter /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification. If this parameter is enabled, visibility is delayed until the OBD driving cycle is qualified.
For DTCs related to misfire or fuel system monitoring
(DemEventOBDReadinessGroup = DEM_OBD_RDY_MISF or DEM_OBD_RDY_FLSYS or DEM_OBD_RDY_FLSYS_NONCONT) the Freeze Frame can also become visible without the need for a qualified driving cycle in some edge cases. 
A stored but not yet visible freeze frame will be displaced by the freeze frame of an event with parameter DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification disabled, if the invisible freeze frame is not related to a misfire and fuel system monitor.
For more information refer to chapter "Reporting of Freeze Frame with Mode $02"  in the OBD Technical Reference.

WWH-OBD, TYPE 1: WWH-OBD Freeze Frame data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
The Freeze Frame is immediately visible

WWH-OBD, TYPE 2: WWH-OBD  Freeze Frame  data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
If DTC becomes a ConfirmedDTC the Freeze Frame data will be updated.
The Freeze Frame is immediately visible.

COMBINED, TYPE1: This behavior corresponds to 'Type 1' for OBDII and 'Type 1' for WWH-OBD.

COMBINED, TYPE2: This behavior corresponds to 'Type 4' for OBDII and 'Type 2' for WWH-OBD.

Use COMBINED values if and only if using OBDII and WWH-OBD in different PBS variants within one configuration.

########## 4. DemOBDLegislation

- **UUID**: `5b05ab0b-5fd6-46ba-82fd-2bc63673fdc8`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDLegislation`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_OBDII`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_OBDII`
  - `DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_WWHOBD`
  - `DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_OBD_ON_UDS`
  - `DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_ZEV_ON_UDS`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The OBD legislation that shall be supported.

OBDII: Support of OBD II.

WWHOBD: Support of WWH-OBD.

OBD_ON_UDS: Support of OBDonUDS.

ZEV_ON_UDS: Support of ZEVonUDS.

If OBD support is disabled (Dem/DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport == DEM_OBD_NO_OBD_SUPPORT), this parameter is ignored.

########## 5. DemClearPermanentDtcBehavior

- **UUID**: `25ac03db-499b-45b0-806e-d66ec9e2ea95`
- **参数名**: `DemClearPermanentDtcBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_CLR_PERM_DTC_TYPE_1`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_CLR_PERM_DTC_TYPE_1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_CLR_PERM_DTC_TYPE_2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
DEM_CLR_PERM_DTC_TYPE_1 (default):
    Permanent DTC is erased if only tested passed (and not tested failed) during the driving cycle and if permanent fault code condition is fulfilled.
DEM_CLR_PERM_DTC_TYPE_2:
   Permanent DTC with IUMPR monitoring is erased if only tested passed (and not tested failed) during the driving cycle.
   Permanent DTC without IUMPR monitoring is erased if only tested passed (and not tested failed) during the driving cycle and if permanent fault code condition is fulfilled.

- **描述**: This reference requests the current odometer for PID21, PID31, J1939 diagnostic readiness 2,  and permanent DTC storage activation distance calculation, therefore this reference is mandatory if one those features is supported.

The returned odometer value (4 Byte) must have a resolution of 1 kilometer or 1 mile.
Furthermore, the odometer value must be provided in big endian format.

If no valid odometer is available the return value must be E_NOT_OK.
Furthermore the permanent DTC storage activation distance calculation is not performed until a valid odometer value is received.

- **描述**: Input variable for the vehicle speed, which is assigned to a specific data element used as interface for the Dem-internal PID calculations.
(not supported)

########## 9. DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStart

- **UUID**: `478c86e5-c1e3-4330-96dd-af24ed4db9aa`
- **引用名**: `DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStart`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `DemDataClass`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Dem/DemGeneral/DemDataClass`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This reference requests the current engine runtime in minutes for PID4D, PID4E, J1939 diagnostic readiness 2, WWH-OBD aging and B1 counter calculation. This reference is also used by the Dem to track the cumulative engine runtime with PTO active if any event is affected by the Dem PTO handling (see /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemConsiderPtoStatus) and the Dem calculates the monitor status since DTCs cleared for PID $01, PID $F501 or J1939 diagnostic readiness 1.
Therefore, this reference is mandatory if one of those features is supported.

The returned engine runtime value (4 Byte) must have a resolution of 1 minute.
Furthermore, the engine runtime value must be provided in big endian format.
If no valid engine runtime is available the return value must be E_NOT_OK.

########## 10. DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStartSeconds

- **UUID**: `6b69edaa-43ae-4fd0-92eb-6044032602a4`
- **引用名**: `DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStartSeconds`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `DemDataClass`

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This reference requests the current engine runtime in seconds for J1939 diagnostic readiness 3. Therefore, this reference is mandatory if this feature is supported.

The returned engine runtime value (4 Byte) must have a resolution of 1 second.
Furthermore, the engine runtime value must be provided in big endian format.
If no valid engine runtime is available the return value must be E_NOT_OK.

####### 33. DemGroupOfDTC

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `19afb70b-2598-4db7-a9f5-93af9eac05ef`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemGroupOfDTC`
- **多重性**: [0..4]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents a DTC group.

DTC groups can be cleared by service $14, if allowed by the OEM specification.
In case only $14 FFFFFF shall be offered, it is recommended to not configure DTC groups.

Defined groups are sorted according their 'DemGroupDTCs' value in increasing sequence and get assigned these semantics: (1) PowertrainGroup, (2) ChassisGroup, (3) BodyGroup, (4) NetworkGroup.
For example when you define only 2 groups, the lower GroupDTC value represents the PowertrainGroup, the higher GroupDTC value represents the ChassisGroup. Undefined are Body- and NetworkGroup.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemGroupDTCs

- **UUID**: `d2def932-ef99-44f4-aa12-d536fc26c003`
- **参数名**: `DemGroupDTCs`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..16777214]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: DTC value of the selected group of DTC.

A clear DTC request for this number will clear all DTCs that belong to this group.
