####### 35. DemNvRamBlockId

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `8fa84fe4-ca3d-4395-b3b1-343b598e8e44`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemNvRamBlockId`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represent a non-volatile memory block used by the Dem.

If no permanent storage of event memory entries is required, no block needs to be configured.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemNvRamBlockIdIndex

- **UUID**: `eaf25db9-dccd-4fea-ab19-a3871265b0c2`
- **参数名**: `DemNvRamBlockIdIndex`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: For non-unique block types, this index identifies the exact block (see DemNvRamBlockIdType).

This parameter must exist and be able to identify all NvBlocks of following kind:
PRIMARY: each index must have an unique value and the index values must complete the range 0 - (DemMaxNumberEventEntryPrimary -1),
USERDEFINED: for all blocks with the same DemNvRamBlockIdEventMemoryRef reference, each index must have an unique value and all index values must complete the range 0 - (DemMaxNumberEventEntryUserDefined -1), 
TIMESERIES: for all blocks with the same DemNvRamBlockIdEventMemoryRef reference, each index must have an unique value and all index values must complete the range 0 - (DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots -1), 

The value is necessary to store the correct memory entry in case it was changed.

For all NvBlocks of other kind (e.g. ADMIN, STATUS,...) the parameter value will be ignored.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. DemNvRamBlockIdType

- **UUID**: `dc3444c5-8096-4fdc-bf0c-a2357b5a16cf`
- **参数名**: `DemNvRamBlockIdType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_ADMIN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_STATUS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_PRIMARY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_PERMANENT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_MIRROR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_FREEZEFRAME` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_OBDIUMPR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_DEBOUNCE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_AVAILABILITY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_TIMESERIES` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_DTR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_AGING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_CYCLECOUNTER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_USERDEFINED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_CUSTOMTRIGGER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Usage kind of this NvBlock.

This parameter is used to identify which block data needs to be stored when event data is modified.

Following block types exist at most once:
ADMIN: Always required.

STATUS: Always required.

MIRROR: Only required if DemGeneral/DemMaxNumberEventEntryMirror > 0.

PERMANENT: Only required if DemGeneral/DemMaxNumberEventEntryPermanent > 0.

FREEZEFRAME: Only required if DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport is enabled and DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation == OBDII or OBD_ON_UDS.

OBDIUMPR: Only required if DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport is enabled and DemGeneral/DemRatioId containers are configured.

DEBOUNCE: Only required if any event uses CounterBased Debouncing with DemDebounceCounterStorage==TRUE.

AVAILABILITY: Only required if event availability status is persisted in non-volatile memory due DemAvailabilityStorage==TRUE.

DTR: Only required if DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport is enabled and
if DemConfigSet/DemDtrs/DemDtr container exist or DemGeneral/DemPostBuild/DemMaxNumberEventEntryPermanent > 0

AGING: Only required if aging data is persisted in non-volatile memory due to DemGeneral/DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs==TRUE or if DemGeneral/DemDataClass/DemDataElementInternalData == AGED_COUNTER is used.

CYCLECOUNTER: Only required if cycle counter data is persisted in non-volatile memory due to DemGeneral/DemSupportStorageIndependentCycleCounters ==TRUE.

Following block types can exist several times, depending on the configuration:
PRIMARY: The number of containers of this type must match DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemPrimaryMemory/DemMaxNumberEventEntryPrimary.

USERDEFINED: The number of containers of this type must match the summarized number of all DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemUserDefinedMemory/DemMaxNumberEventEntryUserDefined.

TIMESERIES: The number of containers of this type must match the summarized number of DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots in DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemPrimaryMemory and all DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemUserDefinedMemory containers.

CUSTOMTRIGGER: The number of containers of this type must match the summarized number of DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots in DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemPrimaryMemory and all DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemUserDefinedMemory containers.

- **引用类型**: CHOICE-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]

####### 36. DemOperationCycle

Otherwise the application has to end the cycle by calling Dem_SetOperationCycleState with state DEM_CYCLE_STATE_END.
This option is only relevant when DemOperationCycleStatusStorage is TRUE.

(Not supported, please use DemGeneral/DemRestartCycleOnInitRef instead)

####### 37. DemRatioId

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `73ce34d3-896d-489a-a44a-f8f3b612bb4d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemRatioId`
- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the OBD specific ratio Id configuration.

It is related to a specific event, a FID, and an IUMPR group.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. DemIUMPRDenGroup

- **UUID**: `68dc8bca-d17d-4cdf-b71a-0c257975cee4`
- **参数名**: `DemIUMPRDenGroup`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_NONE`
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_PHYS_API`
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_COLDSTART`
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_EVAP`
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_500MI`
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_INDEPENDENT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the assigned denominator type which is applied in addition to the General Denominator conditions.

NONE: The denominator is calculated based on the general individual denominator conditions.

PHYS_API: The denominator is calculated based on the general individual denominator conditions. The denominator can be locked via dedicated APIs.

COLDSTART, EVAP, 500MI: The denominator is calculated based on the general individual denominator conditions and additional conditions that must be fulfilled before the denominator is incremented.

INDEPENDENT: Denominator may be incremented independently of the general individual denominator. The denominator may be locked via dedicated APIs.

Defines a unique ratio Id.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. DemIUMPRDenGroup

- **UUID**: `5eedbc07-1b95-41c2-8270-ac4fc5d85871`
- **参数名**: `DemIUMPRDenGroup`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_500MILL` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_COLDSTART` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_EVAP` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_NONE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_PHYS_API` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: (Not supported yet, please use the AUTOSAR 4.0.3 compliant definition in /Dem/DemGeneral/DemRatioId)
This parameter specifies the assigned denominator type which is applied in addition to the General Denominator conditions.

NONE: The denominator is calculated based on the general denominator conditions.

PHYS_API: The denominator can be locked via dedicated APIs.

COLDSTART, EVAP, 500MILL: Additional conditions must be fulfilled before the denominator is incremented.

####### 39. DemStorageCondition

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `a432a244-2e29-4339-bf31-2c41a34c254f`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemStorageCondition`
- **多重性**: [0..255]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents a single storage condition.

Please note: storage conditions can not be used directly but must be grouped (see DemStorageConditionGroup).

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemStorageConditionId

- **UUID**: `3e651eec-8a41-49b0-8aa1-ec321c0fba8e`
- **参数名**: `DemStorageConditionId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `255`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Internal identifier of the storage condition.

This number is calculated internally and shall not be configured manually. This parameter is only available to publish the result of this calculation.
Applications using Dem APIs must not rely on the value of this parameter. Instead, they must use the symbolic constant (SymbolicNameValue) DemConf_DemStorageCondition_{SHORT-NAME}.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. DemStorageConditionStatus

- **UUID**: `6fd4afac-83e4-4efe-8b83-382797b8e1c6`
- **参数名**: `DemStorageConditionStatus`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the status of this storage condition after full initialization, i.e. when Dem_MasterInit() or Dem_Init() finished.
After pre-initialization, but before the full intialization (between Dem_(Master)PreInit() and Dem_(Master)Init() ) the status is always 'true'. 
Changes to the storage condition status during runtime are not persisted non-volatile. The status of this storage condition will always be reset to this configuration value during Dem_MasterInit() or Dem_Init().

####### 42. DemMILGroup

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `f39f273a-df0a-41bf-87cd-4e75daad88c5`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemMILGroup`
- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents a group of events that use a common trip counter to determine their MIL status.

Incrementing the common trip counter is triggered by any event of the MIL group.
Healing is performed individually for each event of the MIL group, but the common trip counter is only reset after all events of the group have been healed.

The reference to a MIL group is configured at the DTCClass container.

####### 43. DemPostbuild

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `3ff1ce95-0712-4425-9b33-16133c624ea3`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemPostbuild`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

The content of this container is only relevant for the implementation variant PostBuild (POST-BUILD-LOADABLE).

For implementation variant PreCompile (and therfore PostBuild-Selectable without -Loadable) all contained parameters are ignored.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemMaxSizeFreezeFrame

- **UUID**: `3ee0662a-6aa2-4f2c-8dd3-29e5b7e94dc4`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxSizeFreezeFrame`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) size of the largest FreezeFrame record.

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents the size of largest defined FreezeFrame record. The minimum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve additional space in the NvRAM blocks. Later in the post-build phase you can choose different DIDs (or a different number of DIDs) for a defined FreezeFrame - as long as the size of the new selected DIDs still fit into the reserved space.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value (when it is smaller than the configured, largest FreezeFrame's size. Changing the FreezeFrame's DIDs afterwards may shorten the largest FreezeFrame's size - but this will NOT decrease this value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum size.

"Size of a freeze frame" is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDidClasses in a DemGeneral/DemFreezeFrameClass. The size of a DidClass is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemNNDataElementClass elements by adding their DemDataElementDataSize values.

########## 2. DemMaxNumberDtr

- **UUID**: `0f0473c7-09c2-4954-8b6b-3f9eb77ab955`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberDtr`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) to store an upper limit of Dtr elements.

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents an upper limit to the maximum value (+1) of the DemDtrId, calculated over all variants. This maximum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve additional number of Dtr elements. Later in the post-build phase you can change Dtr configuration (also create and delete Dtrs) - as long as the maximum value (+1) of the DemDtrId calculated over all variants is smaller than this value.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value (when it is smaller than the configured). Deleting Dtrs afterwards may decrease the lowest possible value - but this will NOT decrease this configured value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum size.

########## 3. DemMaxSizeGlobalFreezeFrame

- **UUID**: `b09925ef-9e62-4381-b7dc-b4fe1e54a75f`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxSizeGlobalFreezeFrame`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) size of the global freeze frame record.

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents the size of the global freeze frame record. The minimum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve additional space in the NvRAM blocks. Later in the post-build phase you can choose different DIDs (or a different number of DIDs) for the global freeze frame - as long as the size of the new selected DIDs still fits into the reserved space.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value when it is smaller than the configured global freeze frame size. Changing the freeze frame's DIDs afterwards may shorten the global freeze frame size - but this will NOT decrease this value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum size.

"Size of the global freeze frame" is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDidClasses in the DemGeneral/DemGlobalFreezeFrame/DemGlobalFreezeFrameClassRef. The size of a DidClass is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDataClass elements by adding their DemDataElementDataSize values.

########## 4. DemMaxSizeTimeSeriesFreezeFrame

- **UUID**: `c0aadee5-9ec7-49c4-baa0-b103fe5f0d72`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxSizeTimeSeriesFreezeFrame`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) size of the time series snapshot records (also known as multi-sample development snapshot records).

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents the size of a time series snapshot record. The minimum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve additional space in the NvRAM blocks. Later in the post-build phase you can choose different DIDs (or a different number of DIDs) for the time series snapshot record - as long as the size of the new selected DIDs still fits into the reserved space.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value when it is smaller than the configured time series snapshot record size. Changing the freeze frame's DIDs afterwards may shorten the time series snapshot record size - but this will NOT decrease this value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum size.

"Size of the time series snapshot record" is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDidClasses in the DemGeneral/DemFreezeFrameClass used in DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemDevFreezeFrameBClassRef. The size of a DidClass is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDataClass elements by adding their DemDataElementDataSize values.

########## 5. DemMaxSizeOBDonUDSFreezeFrame

- **UUID**: `921d9ca5-5933-4f37-b5fa-8238e34654c6`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxSizeOBDonUDSFreezeFrame`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) size of the largest OBD on UDS FreezeFrame record.

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents the size of largest defined OBD on UDS FreezeFrame record. The minimum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve additional space in the NvRAM blocks. Later in the post-build phase you can choose different DIDs (or a different number of DIDs) for a defined FreezeFrame - as long as the size of the new selected DIDs still fit into the reserved space.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value (when it is smaller than the configured, largest FreezeFrame's size. Changing the FreezeFrame's DIDs afterwards may shorten the largest FreezeFrame's size - but this will NOT decrease this value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum size.

"Size of a freeze frame" is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDidClasses in a DemGeneral/DemFreezeFrameClass. The size of a DidClass is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDataElement Class elements by adding their DemDataElementDataSize values.

########## 6. DemMaxNumberEventsWithMonitorActivityData

- **UUID**: `ea71973e-388d-4ba4-9415-306164a65678`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberEventsWithMonitorActivityData`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is only relevant for OBD on UDS legislation.

Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) to store an upper limit of Monitor Activity Data (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemDataClass/DemDataElementInternalData MONITOR_ACTIVITY_DATA).

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents an upper limit for the number of events for which Monitor Activity Data can be stored. This maximum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve an additional number of events with Monitor Activity Data. Later in the post-build phase you can change the DemExtendedDataClassRef of an event and thereby the DemExtendedDataRecordClasses referenced by an event - as long as the maximum number of events with Monitor Activity Data is smaller or equal to this value.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value (when it is smaller than the configured). Changing DemExtendedDataClassRefs or DemExtendedDataClasses afterwards may decrease the lowest possible value - but this will NOT decrease this configured value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum number.

########## 7. DemMaxNumberEventsPtoTimer

- **UUID**: `ae05d2b6-c2fa-4f4e-b620-05817e6a8c99`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberEventsPtoTimer`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is only relevant if any event is affected by the Dem PTO handling (see /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemConsiderPtoStatus) and the Dem calculates the monitor status since DTCs cleared for PID $01, PID $F501 or J1939 diagnostic readiness 1.
For each event affected by the Dem PTO handling that has an OBD Readiness group (see /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEventOBDReadinessGroup), an event specific PTO timer is maintained to track the cumulative engine runtime with PTO active.

Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) to store an upper limit of event specific PTO timers.

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents an upper limit for the number of events for which a PTO timer can be maintained. This maximum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve an additional number of events with PTO timer. Later in the post-build phase you can change the DemEventOBDReadinessGroup of an event as long as the maximum number of events with PTO timer is smaller or equal to this value.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value (when it is smaller than the configured). Changing the DemEventOBDReadinessGroup of an event afterwards may decrease the lowest possible value - but this will NOT decrease this configured value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum number.

########## 1. DemEventAvailableDefaultSupport

- **UUID**: `a2c3b23c-057e-405f-8366-a150ae474dc0`
- **参数名**: `DemEventAvailableDefaultSupport`

- **描述**: Select whether the default availability value should be initialized with Dem_Init()

TRUE: Use the default values from configuration parameter DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventAvailable to set the initial value during Dem_Init()
FALSE: The availability information is not changed during Dem_Init()

If this setting is enabled, API Dem_SetEventAvailable cannot be used before Dem_Init() due to Dem_Init() overwriting the active setting.

####### 44. DemGlobalFreezeFrame

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `67f0449a-4ff9-4447-9248-714bc29dcd4f`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemGlobalFreezeFrame`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

In this container the attributes and content of the global freeze frame can be configured.
If this container is configured, global freeze frame data is stored for each event (In addition to Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemFreezeFrameClassRef).

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordNumber

- **UUID**: `4870318e-6a16-4d0c-85f2-da38ed904f12`
- **参数名**: `DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..254]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the record number of the global freeze frame.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordUpdate

- **UUID**: `c90b288a-8f22-4767-be6e-5705146c1c31`
- **参数名**: `DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordUpdate`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_NO`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines, whether the global freeze frame is updated with each trigger.

DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_NO: The global freeze frame is captured only once.

DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES: The global freeze frame is captured with each trigger, overwriting the previously captured data (currently not supported).

########## 2. DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordTrigger

- **UUID**: `bb056dea-a439-4c1f-b4bc-9a12e664bf5a`
- **参数名**: `DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordTrigger`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CONFIRMED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the trigger to store the global freeze frame. Currently only trigger CONFIRMED is supported.

FDC_THRESHOLD: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by a fault detection counter passing a storage threshold.

CONFIRMED: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by the ConfirmedDTC transition from 0 to 1.

PENDING: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by the PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.

TEST_FAILED: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by the TestFailed transition from 0 to 1.

TEST_FAILED_FIRST_IN_CYCLE: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by the first TestFailed transition from 0 to 1 in an operation cycle.

FDC_FIRST_IN_CYCLE: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by the first DTC fault detection counter reaching the storage threshold in an operation cycle.

Please note that triggers occuring before an event memory entry is allocated are discarded.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter references the list of DIDs to be stored in the global freeze frame.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration value defines the time interval for the periodic collection of the samples.

The value must be a multiple of the DemGeneral/DemTaskTime. Otherwise it is rounded down to the next multiple value of DemTaskTime.

The AUTOSAR configuration standard is to use SI units, so in the EcuC file this parameter is stored as float value in seconds - the DaVinci Configurator 5 GUI will convert the value when using a different unit.

####### 46. DemGeneralCalibration

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7ed5a7e6-0a6d-4f36-9ab1-e4375c087423`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemGeneralCalibration`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Contains configuration options for calibration.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemCalibrationAdditionalCounterBasedDebouncingClasses

- **UUID**: `755bc538-eea6-4be0-90f9-332817ebdb65`
- **参数名**: `DemCalibrationAdditionalCounterBasedDebouncingClasses`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is only relevant if DemGeneral/DemGeneralCalibration/DemCalibrationCounterBasedDebouncingSupport is set to true. 

This parameter specifies the number of DemCounterBasedDebouncingClasses that shall be calibratable in addition the configured and referenced DemConfigSet/DemDebounceCounterBasedClasses.
The values of these additional classes can be calibrated (see DemCalibrationCounterBasedDebouncingSupport) and can be referenced by any event using counter based debouncing via calibration.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. DemCalibrationCounterBasedDebouncingSupport

- **UUID**: `cb1dbdbc-d03d-47c5-a709-5f7f4a4ac0cd`
- **参数名**: `DemCalibrationCounterBasedDebouncingSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this parameter is set to true, the following parameters of a DemConfigSet/DemDebounceCounterBasedClass can be calibrated: 

- DemDebounceCounterIncrementStepSize
- DemDebounceCounterFailedThreshold
- DemDebounceCounterPassedThreshold
- DemDebounceCounterJumpUp
- DemDebounceCounterJumpUpValue
- DemDebounceCounterJumpDown
- DemDebounceCounterJumpDownValue
- DemCounterBasedFdcThresholdStorageValue
- DemDebounceContinuous
- DemDebounceBehavior
- DemDebounceCounterDecrementStepSize: In contrast to the initially configured value, when calibrating this parameter the modified value needs to be negative, i.e. it is equal to configured value * (-1).

For events with counter based debouncing it is also possible to calibrate which event references which counter based debouncing class. 

Note: To support calibration of counter based debounce classes, optimizations are disabled for the tables containing these classes. This can lead to increased memory consumption.

##### 26. DemCallbackDTCStatusChanged

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `484fdb01-c751-4442-b524-6f2b57489062`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemCallbackDTCStatusChanged`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

The presence of this container sets up a callout function 'DTCStatusChanged' for this DTC. The Dem will call this function each time a DTC status changes.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackDTCStatusChangedFnc'.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. DemCallbackDTCStatusChangedFnc

- **UUID**: `8b145100-b069-4dd3-aaab-d11194fabfb1`
- **参数名**: `DemCallbackDTCStatusChangedFnc`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Function name of a 'DTCStatusChanged' callout.

If this parameter exists, it must give a valid C-Function which is called instead of using an RTE port interface.
See DemGeneral/DemHeaderFileInclusion to specify a header file which declares this function.

##### 28. DemDataClass

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `64d96937-5282-4ac8-a980-deafe1b22dd3`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDataClass`
- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container groups the configuration (parameters) of a data element.

Data elements are used to build DIDs, PIDs and ExtendedDataRecords

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **7** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemDataElementDataSize

- **UUID**: `453659d9-d2d2-4432-a822-f81676e9f873`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementDataSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..4096]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the size of the data element in bytes.

For Dem-internal data elements, the size shall be predefined according to the selected internal data element (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemDataClass/DemDataElementInternalData).

For Dem-external data elements, the size depends on the data type and number of elements which needs to be stored.
For array data classes, this field must contain the total size of the array in bytes. An example is provided below.

BOOLEAN is a 8 bit data type and therefore needs 1 byte.
UINT8, SINT8, UINT8_N and SINT8_N are 8 bit data types and therefore need 1 bytes per element.
UINT16, SINT16, UINT16_N and SINT16_N are 16 bit data types and therefore need 2 bytes per element.
UINT32, SINT32, UINT32_N and SINT32_N are 32 bit data types and therefore need 4 bytes per element.
FLOAT and FLOAT_N entries are 32 bit data types and therefore need 4 bytes per element.

Example: For example if an UINT16_N array data type is selected, of which 4 entries need to be stored, than a data size of 8 bytes needs to be allocated.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemDataElementStoreNonVolatile

- **UUID**: `ae77d962-a3f3-4efd-be39-71e65a82f977`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementStoreNonVolatile`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Select if the data element needs to be stored in NVRam.

Ususally, snapshots of a system value (like temperature, voltage...) need to be stored in NVRam. If the data element is stored, the Dem will collect the current value into NVRam at the DTC storage time. Reading access will return the stored data (e.g. for diagnostic service $19)

Disabling NV storage for this data element will cause the Dem to fetch the data with each read access. This is useful if this data element is something that will not change over time, or is already stored outside the Dem.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemDataElementDataType

- **UUID**: `37013dce-9ebb-4e5e-8dc9-4f5746bb979d`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementDataType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BOOLEAN`
  - `SINT16`
  - `SINT32`
  - `SINT8`
  - `SINT8_N`
  - `UINT16`
  - `UINT32`
  - `UINT8`
  - `UINT8_N`
  - `UINT16_N`
  - `SINT16_N`
  - `UINT32_N`
  - `SINT32_N`
  - `FLOAT`
  - `FLOAT_N`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Provide the implementation data type of data belonging to a external data.

For DemDataElementDataSize > 1 the configured data type UINT8/SINT8 is treated as UINT8_N/SINT8_N to establish compatibility with the DCM.

######## 2. DemDataElementEndianness

- **UUID**: `e1ac5655-cd09-457a-91be-7b067a2aba1d`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementEndianness`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BIG_ENDIAN`
  - `LITTLE_ENDIAN`
  - `OPAQUE`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the endianness of the data belonging to an external data.

######## 3. DemDataElementInternalData

- **UUID**: `1452433a-887f-4d98-9622-6a95bab8d169`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementInternalData`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_AGINGCTR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGINGCTR_UPCNT`
  - `DEM_AGINGCTR_INVERTED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGINGCTR_DOWNCNT`
  - `DEM_OCCCTR`
  - `DEM_OCCCTR_2BYTE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OVFLIND`
  - `DEM_SIGNIFICANCE`
  - `DEM_PRIORITY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_CURRENT_FDC`
  - `DEM_MAX_FDC_SINCE_LAST_CLEAR`
  - `DEM_MAX_FDC_DURING_CURRENT_CYCLE`
  - `DEM_CYCLES_SINCE_FIRST_FAILED`
  - `DEM_CYCLES_SINCE_LAST_FAILED`
  - `DEM_FAILED_CYCLES`
  - `DEM_CONSECUTIVE_FAILED_CYCLES` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_CYCLES_TESTED_SINCE_FIRST_FAILED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_CYCLES_TESTED_SINCE_LAST_FAILED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_HEALINGCTR_DOWNCNT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_ROOTCAUSE_EVENTID` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDDTC` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDDTC_3BYTE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBD_RATIO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_FAULT_PENDING_COUNTER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_AGED_COUNTER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_WUC_SINCE_LAST_FAILED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_SI30_STATUS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_IUMPR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_DTR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_MONITOR_ACTIVITY_DATA` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Select the Dem-internal data value, which is mapped to the data element.

AGINGCTR / AGINGCTR_UPCNT: (1 Byte) Current value of the aging counter, counting from 1...DemAgingCycleCounterThreshold. The value latches at aging counter threshold (refer to DemGeneral/DemAgingCounterBehavior).
If DemGeneral/DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs is disabled, this value is processed and readable only for currently stored events.
If DemGeneral/DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs is enabled, depending on the configuration, this value is always readable (refer to Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility).

AGINGCTR_INVERTED / AGINGCTR_DOWNCNT: (1 Byte) Current value of the aging counter, counting backwards from the configured aging cycle counter threshold value down to 0. If DemGeneral/DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs is disabled, this value is processed and readable only for currently stored events.
If DemGeneral/DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs is enabled, depending on the configuration, this value is always readable (refer to Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility).

OCCCTR: (1 Byte) Current value of the occurrence counter, counting from 1...255. The value is incremented on each transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 and latches at 255. This value is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

OCCCTR_2BYTE: (2 Byte) Current value of the occurrence counter, counting from 1...65535. The value is incremented on each transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 and latches at 65535. This value is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

OVFLIND: (1 Byte) Value 1 if the primary memory has overflown, 0 otherwise. Will be reset on ClearAllDTC request. This value is always available.

SIGNIFICANCE: (1 Byte) Value 0 if the event is configured as OCCURRENCE, 1 if the event is configured as FAILURE. This value is always available.

PRIORITY: (1 Byte) The DTC priority which is mapped into the environmental data. This value is always available.

CURRENT_FDC: (1 Byte) Current value of the DTC fault detection counter. This value is always available.

MAX_FDC_SINCE_LAST_CLEAR: (1 Byte) Maximum value of the DTC fault detection counter reached since last clear. 0 is the minimum value reported. 
If Dem/DemGeneral/DemSupportStorageIndependentMaxFDCSinceLastClear is not enabled the data element is processed and readable only for currently stored events. Otherwise it is processed and readable at all times (see also Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility).

MAX_FDC_DURING_CURRENT_CYCLE: (1 Byte) Maximum value of the DTC fault detection counter reached in the current operation cycle. 0 is the minimum value reported. This value is always available.

CYCLES_SINCE_FIRST_FAILED: (1 Byte) Number of completed operation cycles, since the cycle of the first transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 of the DTC. The value latches at 255. The counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

CYCLES_SINCE_LAST_FAILED: (1 Byte) Number of completed operation cycles, since the last transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 of the DTC. The value is reset at the first failed result in the operation cycle, latches at 255. The counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

FAILED_CYCLES: (1 Byte) Number of operation cycles, since last DTC clear in which the DTC was tested 'failed', including the current cycle. The value is incremented at the first failed result in the operation cycle, latches at 255.
If Dem/DemGeneral/DemSupportStorageIndependentCycleCounters is not enabled the counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events. Otherwise the counter is processed and readable at all times (see also Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility).

CONSECUTIVE_FAILED_CYCLES: (1 Byte): Number of consecutive operation cycles in which the DTC was tested 'failed', including the current cycle. The value is incremented at the first failed result in the operation cycle and reset at the end of an operation cycle that was tested and not tested 'failed'.The value latches at 255. The counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

CYCLES_TESTED_SINCE_FIRST_FAILED: (1 Byte) Number of completed and tested operation cycles since the cycle of the first transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 of the DTC. The value latches at 255. The counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

CYCLES_TESTED_SINCE_LAST_FAILED: (1 Byte) Number of completed and tested operation cycles, since the last transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 of the DTC (Healing  Counter). The value is reset at the first failed result in the operation cycle, latches at 255. The value is always available.

HEALINGCTR_DOWNCNT: (1 Byte) Number of complete and tested operation cycles until WIR bit will be cleared (independently of whether Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemIndicatorAttribute/DemIndicatorRef is set or not). If healing has not started yet, Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemIndicatorAttribute/DemIndicatorHealingCycleCounterThreshold is reported. If healing is completed, 0 is reported. The value is always available.

ROOTCAUSE_EVENTID: (2 Byte) The event id that caused the storage/update of the environmental data. Can be used in context of the feature 'combined event' to store the root cause DemEventId value. The value is processed and readable only for currently stored events

OBDDTC: (2 Byte) The corresponding OBD II DTC for the requested UDS DTC. If no OBD II DTC is configured, the returned value will be 0. This value is always available.

OBDDTC_3BYTE: (3 Byte) 
In OBDII configurations: The corresponding OBD II DTC for the requested UDS DTC in format [0x00 LoByte HiByte].
In OBD on UDS configurations: The corresponding 3 byte OBD DTC for the requested UDS DTC, if DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemSupportedObdUdsDtcSeparation == true.
If no OBD II DTC resp. 3 byte OBD DTC is configured, the returned value will be 0. This value is always available.

OBD_RATIO:  (4 Byte) If the DTC has a ratio attached, the numerator and denominator are returned in the following format: [NumHiByte NumLoByte DenomHiByte DenomLoByte].
If the DTC does not have a ratio attached, the returned value is [0x00 0x00 0x00 0x00]. This value is always available.

FAULT_PENDING_COUNTER: (1 Byte) Number of consecutive operation cycles in which the DTC was tested 'failed', including the current cycle. The value is incremented at the first failed result in the operation cycle and reset at the end of an operation cycle that was tested and not tested 'failed'. In contrast to the 'Consecutive Failed Cycle Counter' the value latches at DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold + 1.
If Dem/DemGeneral/DemSupportStorageIndependentCycleCounters is not enabled the counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events. Otherwise the counter is processed and readable at all times (see also Dem/DemGeneral/DemExtendedDataVisibility).

AGED_COUNTER: (1 Byte) Current value of the aged counter. The value is incremented, if the DTC is aged and reset, if the DTC is cleared. The value latches at 255 and is always available.

WUC_SINCE_LAST_FAILED: (1 Byte) Number of completed warm up cycles, since the last transition of the TestFailed Bit from 0 to 1 of the DTC. The value latches at 255. The counter is processed and readable only for currently stored events.

SI30_STATUS: (1 Byte) Extended status byte. The extended status is calculated and is always available.

IUMPR: (4 Byte) The numerator and denominator of the ratio of a DTC are returned in the following format: [NumHiByte NumLoByte DenomHiByte DenomLoByte].
This value can be used to configure extended data record number 0x91 according to SAE J1979-2. The value is only available, if OBD on UDS is supported (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation).

DTR: (1 Byte for the number of test results, 8 Byte for each test result) The Diagnostic Test Results (DTRs) of a DTC are returned in the following format: [NumberOfTestResults] and for each test result: [UaSIdHiByte UaSIdLoByte TestValueHiByte TestValueLoByte MinTestLimitHiByte MinTestLimitLoByte MaxTestLimitHiByte MaxTestLimitLoByte].
The DTRs are returned in ascending order by Monitor Identifier (MID) and Test Identifier (TID).
The size of this internal data element varies for each DTC based on the number of DTRs referencing the corresponding event. Due to this variability, size must be configured to
the maximum supported value of 249 bytes (Dem supports a maximum of 31 DTRs with 8 byte each plus 1 byte for the number of test results, see Dem/DemGeneral/DemDataClass/DemDataElementDataSize). The actual size is calculated at runtime.
This value can be used to configure extended data record number 0x92 according to SAE J1979-2. The value is only available, if OBD on UDS is supported (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation).

MONITOR_ACTIVITY_DATA: (2 Byte) The monitor activity numerator (MAN) and stored monitor activity ratio (SMAR) of a DTC are returned in the following format: [MAN SMAR].
This value can be used to configure extended data record number 0x93 according to SAE J1979-2. The value is only available, if OBD on UDS is supported (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation).

######## 4. DemDataElementUsePort

- **UUID**: `49751469-5009-4eef-abcb-c9d51e01efaf`
- **参数名**: `DemDataElementUsePort`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `USE_DATA_CLIENT_SERVER_PORT`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `USE_DATA_CLIENT_SERVER_PORT`
  - `USE_DATA_CLIENT_SERVER_PORT_WITH_EVENTID`
  - `USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL`
  - `USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_EVENTID`
  - `USE_DATA_SENDER_RECEIVER`
  - `USE_DATA_INTERNAL`
  - `USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_EVENTID_BUFFERLENGTH`
  - `USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_PROXY`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines which interface shall be used to access the data.

For data calculated internally by the Dem, use
USE_DATA_INTERNAL

For data provided by the application through a client/server port, use
USE_DATA_CLIENT_SERVER_PORT
USE_DATA_CLIENT_SERVER_PORT_WITH_EVENTID

For data provided by the application through a C function-callback, use
USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL
USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_EVENTID
USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_EVENTID_BUFFERLENGTH
USE_DATA_FUNCTION_CALL_WITH_PROXY

For data provided by the application through a sender/receiver port, use
USE_DATA_SENDER_RECEIVER

##### 39. DemEnableCondition

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `2128e56a-252f-417e-a437-9069960235fb`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemEnableCondition`
- **多重性**: [0..254]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents a single enable condition.

Please note: enable conditions can not be used directly but must be grouped (see DemEnableConditionGroup).

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemEnableConditionId

- **UUID**: `35a21b4d-8001-4fe6-954f-61cb5021c375`
- **参数名**: `DemEnableConditionId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `255`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Internal identifier of the enable condition.

This number is calculated internally and shall not be configured manually. This parameter is only available to publish the result of this calculation.
Applications using Dem APIs must not rely on the value of this parameter. Instead, they must use the symbolic constant (SymbolicNameValue) DemConf_DemEnableCondition_{SHORT-NAME}.

The ID value 0 is reserved for internal purpose.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemEnableConditionStatus

- **UUID**: `da12f486-9f80-40ae-85ad-3c4f0e8d11f0`
- **参数名**: `DemEnableConditionStatus`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the status of this enable condition after full initialization, i.e. when Dem_MasterInit() or Dem_Init() finished.
After pre-initialization, but before the full intialization (between Dem_(Master)PreInit() and Dem_(Master)Init()) the status is always 'true'.
Changes to the enable condition status during runtime are not persisted non-volatile. The status of this enable condition will always be reset to this configuration value during Dem_MasterInit() or Dem_Init().

- **参数名**: `DemClearDtcNotificationFnc`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE

####### 2. DemPrimaryMemory

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `163fd33f-f576-42b9-a3ca-595772b034f7`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemPrimaryMemory`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the primary event memory specific parameters
of the Dem module.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. DemMaxNumberEventEntryPrimary

- **UUID**: `8013129e-5b27-4b65-8850-9ec24040245e`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberEventEntryPrimary`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [1..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Maximum number of events which can be stored in the primary memory

########## 2. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots

- **UUID**: `ff35b89e-bca3-4a49-a0dc-841f5740944a`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which time series snapshot records (also known as multi-sample development snapshot records) can be stored at the same time for this event memory.

########## 3. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets

- **UUID**: `14329618-ad64-4ca9-9d85-6f5aba197932`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..16]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The maximum number of time series snapshot record sets that can be stored for an event assigned to this memory. If the parameter is missing, only one set will be stored.

A set of time series snapshot records consists of all past and future samples that are stored for an event after the freeze frame storage trigger has occurred. With each re-occurrence of the trigger, a new set can be stored. 
When the maximum number of time series snapshot record sets has been stored, the new set may overwrite the oldest set depending on the value of DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport.

For more information on how the time series snapshot records of the different sets are numbered, refer to DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset.

########## 4. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset

- **UUID**: `4836c4be-dace-47f8-903a-fd99c667c38d`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [1..32]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This offset is used to calculate the first record number of all subsequent sets of time series snapshots that are stored for an event (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets).

This parameter only has an effect if DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1. 

The first set of time series snapshot record numbers starts at the freeze frame recod number (see DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber) configured for the time series snapshot record (see DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries).
If DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1, the records numbers of the subsequent sets will start at:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (1 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (2 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
...
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (n* DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset), where n equals (DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets -1).

Example:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber is set to 0x80,
- DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset is set to 0x08 and
- DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets equals 3,
the different sets of time series snapshot records stored for an event will start at record number 
- 0x80,
- 0x88 and
- 0x90.

########## 5. DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots

- **UUID**: `c61a6001-1066-4435-8e95-edcbe5905593`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which custom triggered snapshot records and/or custom triggered time series snapshot records can be stored at the same time for this event memory.
A snapshot record or time series snapshot record is custom triggered if the corresponding DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger is configured to DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CUSTOM.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport

- **UUID**: `21b080ce-de0d-4f00-9f67-eb0d25c137bc`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines if the time series snapshot record sets of events assigned to this memory are stored in a circular buffer.

If the switch is enabled and the configured maximum number of time series snapshot record sets (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets) is stored for an event, the oldest set of time series snapshot records will be overwritten with each new storage trigger occurrence. Correspondingly, all time series snapshot records of the event will be renumbered in ascending order from oldest to newest.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. DemTypeOfFreezeFrameRecordNumeration

- **UUID**: `53cd6f3a-158d-405e-8cfc-42eb1255c057`
- **参数名**: `DemTypeOfFreezeFrameRecordNumeration`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CONFIGURED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED_FIFO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the type of assigning snapshot record numbers.

CALCULATED: The snapshot records are enumerated from one (1) up to DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords for each event. Once an event memory entry is created a snapshot is stored at each FAILED transition. After DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords have been stored for the event, the last snapshot is updated with each FAILED transition.

CALCULATED_FIFO: The snapshot records are enumerated from one (1) up to DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords for each event. Once an event memory entry is created, a snapshot is stored at each FAILED transition of the event. After DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords have been stored for the event, the oldest snapshot is overwritten during the next trigger. When a snapshot is overwritten, all snapshot records are renumbered in ascending order from oldest to newest snapshot record. 

CONFIGURED: The snapshot record numbers are taken from DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemFreezeFrameRecNumClassRef. Storage triggers and update conditions can be configured.

########## 2. DemEventMemoryEntryStorageTrigger

- **UUID**: `173382ee-e393-4cba-9c88-a6b7d7f7b570`
- **参数名**: `DemEventMemoryEntryStorageTrigger`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_STORAGE_ON_TEST_FAILED`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_CONFIRMED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_FDC_THRESHOLD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_PENDING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_TEST_FAILED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter describes the trigger for the allocation of an event memory entry, i.e. the point of time when the corresponding DTC is initially stored in the referenced event memory.

CONFIRMED: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC confirms (bit3 transitions 0->1).

FDC_THRESHOLD: An event entry is allocated once the fault detection counter of its corresponding DTC exceeds its storage threshold.

PENDING: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC gets pending (bit2 transitions 0->1).

TEST_FAILED: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC fails (bit0 transitions 0->1).

Restriction: If FDC_THRESHOLD is selected, monitor internal debouncing cannot be used to store event data at pre-failed.

- **UUID**: `72093ee5-8ea8-4393-aeb4-c5c43449899f`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which time series snapshot records (also known as multi-sample development snapshot records) can be stored at the same time for this event memory.

########## 4. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets

- **UUID**: `f07755d4-d56d-4249-ba6f-8b1ff4af77e3`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..16]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The maximum number of time series snapshot record sets that can be stored for an event assigned to this memory. If the parameter is missing, only one set will be stored.

A set of time series snapshot records consists of all past and future samples that are stored for an event after the freeze frame storage trigger has occurred. With each re-occurrence of the trigger, a new set can be stored. 
When the maximum number of time series snapshot record sets has been stored, the new set may overwrite the oldest set depending on the value of DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport.

For more information on how the time series snapshot records of the different sets are numbered, refer to DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset.

########## 5. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset

- **UUID**: `975b5994-8965-42d8-8041-539e15c8f8c4`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [1..32]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This offset is used to calculate the first record number of all subsequent sets of time series snapshots that are stored for an event (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets).

This parameter only has an effect if DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1. 

The first set of time series snapshot record numbers starts at the freeze frame recod number (see DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber) configured for the time series snapshot record (see DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries).
If DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1, the records numbers of the subsequent sets will start at:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (1 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (2 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
...
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (n* DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset), where n equals (DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets -1).

Example:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber is set to 0x80,
- DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset is set to 0x08 and
- DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets equals 3,
the different sets of time series snapshot records stored for an event will start at record number 
- 0x80,
- 0x88 and
- 0x90.

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which custom triggered snapshot records and/or custom triggered time series snapshot records can be stored at the same time for this event memory.
A snapshot record or time series snapshot record is custom triggered if the corresponding DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger is configured to DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CUSTOM.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport

- **UUID**: `21b080ce-de0d-4f00-9f67-eb0d25c137bc`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration switch defines if the time series snapshot record sets of events assigned to this memory are stored in a circular buffer.

If the switch is enabled and the configured maximum number of time series snapshot record sets (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets) is stored for an event, the oldest set of time series snapshot records will be overwritten with each new storage trigger occurrence. Correspondingly, all time series snapshot records of the event will be renumbered in ascending order from oldest to newest.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemTypeOfFreezeFrameRecordNumeration

- **UUID**: `53cd6f3a-158d-405e-8cfc-42eb1255c057`
- **参数名**: `DemTypeOfFreezeFrameRecordNumeration`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED`
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CONFIGURED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_FF_RECNUM_CALCULATED_FIFO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the type of assigning snapshot record numbers.

CALCULATED: The snapshot records are enumerated from one (1) up to DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords for each event. Once an event memory entry is created a snapshot is stored at each FAILED transition. After DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords have been stored for the event, the last snapshot is updated with each FAILED transition.

CALCULATED_FIFO: The snapshot records are enumerated from one (1) up to DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords for each event. Once an event memory entry is created, a snapshot is stored at each FAILED transition of the event. After DemMaxNumberFreezeFrameRecords have been stored for the event, the oldest snapshot is overwritten during the next trigger. When a snapshot is overwritten, all snapshot records are renumbered in ascending order from oldest to newest snapshot record. 

CONFIGURED: The snapshot record numbers are taken from DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemFreezeFrameRecNumClassRef. Storage triggers and update conditions can be configured.

######## 2. DemEventMemoryEntryStorageTrigger

- **UUID**: `173382ee-e393-4cba-9c88-a6b7d7f7b570`
- **参数名**: `DemEventMemoryEntryStorageTrigger`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_STORAGE_ON_TEST_FAILED`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_CONFIRMED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_FDC_THRESHOLD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_PENDING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_STORAGE_ON_TEST_FAILED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter describes the trigger for the allocation of an event memory entry, i.e. the point of time when the corresponding DTC is initially stored in the referenced event memory.

CONFIRMED: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC confirms (bit3 transitions 0->1).

FDC_THRESHOLD: An event entry is allocated once the fault detection counter of its corresponding DTC exceeds its storage threshold.

PENDING: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC gets pending (bit2 transitions 0->1).

TEST_FAILED: An event entry is allocated once its corresponding DTC fails (bit0 transitions 0->1).

Restriction: If FDC_THRESHOLD is selected, monitor internal debouncing cannot be used to store event data at pre-failed.

######## 3. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots

- **UUID**: `72093ee5-8ea8-4393-aeb4-c5c43449899f`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the maximum number of events for which time series snapshot records (also known as multi-sample development snapshot records) can be stored at the same time for this event memory.

######## 4. DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets

- **UUID**: `f07755d4-d56d-4249-ba6f-8b1ff4af77e3`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [1..16]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The maximum number of time series snapshot record sets that can be stored for an event assigned to this memory. If the parameter is missing, only one set will be stored.

A set of time series snapshot records consists of all past and future samples that are stored for an event after the freeze frame storage trigger has occurred. With each re-occurrence of the trigger, a new set can be stored. 
When the maximum number of time series snapshot record sets has been stored, the new set may overwrite the oldest set depending on the value of DemTimeSeriesSnapshotFifoSupport.

For more information on how the time series snapshot records of the different sets are numbered, refer to DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset.

######## 5. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset

- **UUID**: `975b5994-8965-42d8-8041-539e15c8f8c4`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSnapshotRecordNumberOffset`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `8`
- **取值范围**: [1..32]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This offset is used to calculate the first record number of all subsequent sets of time series snapshots that are stored for an event (see DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets).

This parameter only has an effect if DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1. 

The first set of time series snapshot record numbers starts at the freeze frame recod number (see DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber) configured for the time series snapshot record (see DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries).
If DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets is greater than 1, the records numbers of the subsequent sets will start at:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (1 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (2 * DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset),
...
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber + (n* DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset), where n equals (DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets -1).

Example:
- DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber is set to 0x80,
- DemTimeSeriesSnapshotOffset is set to 0x08 and
- DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshotSets equals 3,
the different sets of time series snapshot records stored for an event will start at record number 
- 0x80,
- 0x88 and
- 0x90.

######## 6. DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots

- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots`

A snapshot record or time series snapshot record is custom triggered if the corresponding DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger is configured to DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CUSTOM.

##### 46. DemExtendedDataRecordClass

**容器属性**:

- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemExtendedDataRecordClass`
- **多重性**: [0..253]

- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents a single extended data record.

Each extended data record is assembled out of one or several data elements.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemExtendedDataRecordNumber

- **UUID**: `5b874227-548b-48a7-ba8f-dded41d91e0d`
- **参数名**: `DemExtendedDataRecordNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..253]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The unique identifier of the extended data record as requested and reported by $19 06.

Please note that extended data records are unique per ECU, e.g. the number references a defined set of data independent of the DTC it is related to.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemExtendedDataRecordTrigger

- **UUID**: `b831b4e5-a28c-4380-940f-e4230acef120`
- **参数名**: `DemExtendedDataRecordTrigger`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_TEST_FAILED`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_TEST_FAILED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_FDC_FIRST_IN_CYCLE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CONFIRMED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_PASSED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the trigger to store and update ExtendedDataRecords, if configured.

FDC_FIRST_IN_CYCLE: The extended data record capture/ update is triggered by the event's fault detection counter exceeding the configured storage threshold. This trigger is only processed once per operationcycle.

TEST_FAILED: The  extended data record capture/ update is triggered by the TestFailed bit transition from 0 to 1.

CONFIRMED: The  extended data record capture is triggered by the Confirmed bit transition from 0 to 1.

PASSED: The  extended data record capture/ update is triggered by a qualified 'Passed' report that resets the TestFailed bit from 1 to 0.

######## 2. DemExtendedDataRecordUpdate

- **UUID**: `0ff32bc7-6f9d-401f-a73e-433edbd7910e`
- **参数名**: `DemExtendedDataRecordUpdate`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_NO` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines if this record is captured only once (UPDATE_RECORD_NO) or updated with each following trigger ((UPDATE_RECORD_YES).

Please note that internal data elements are updated continuously and can not be frozen to their value at the time of confirmation.
