**容器描述**:

This container contains a list of dedicated, different freeze frame record numbers.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber

- **UUID**: `00da3aa7-6957-4bcf-b281-69643ac658f5`
- **参数名**: `DemFreezeFrameRecordNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..254]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines a record number for a freeze frame record. 
This record number is unique per freeze frame record number class.

In case that DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries is enabled, this parameter represents the first record number of the time series snapshot records.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries

- **UUID**: `9b122b9d-0e08-4112-b49e-6ad5591b0ccb`
- **参数名**: `DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines whether the record is used as a time series snapshot record.

If this parameter is enabled, DemTimeSeriesSnapshotClassRef must contain a valid reference.

If this parameter is disabled or not configured, the record is used as a configured freeze frame record and DemTimeSeriesSnapshotClassRef is ignored.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger

- **UUID**: `5cd824ea-efbe-458f-bcd9-ccbaff09abfe`
- **参数名**: `DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_FDC_THRESHOLD` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CONFIRMED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_PENDING` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_TEST_FAILED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_TEST_FAILED_FIRST_IN_CYCLE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_FDC_FIRST_IN_CYCLE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_PASSED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CUSTOM` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the trigger to store the FreezeFrameRecord.

FDC_THRESHOLD: The snapshot data capture is triggered by a fault detection counter passing a storage threshold.

CONFIRMED: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the ConfirmedDTC transition 0 -> 1.

PENDING: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the PendingDTC transition 0 -> 1.

TEST_FAILED: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the TestFailed transition 0 -> 1.

TEST_FAILED_FIRST_IN_CYCLE: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the first TestFailed transition 0 -> 1 in an operation cycle (i.e TestFailedThisOperationCycle transition 0 -> 1).

FDC_FIRST_IN_CYCLE: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the first DTC fault detection counter reaching the storage threshold in an operation cycle.

PASSED: The snapshot data capture is triggered by the TestFailed transition 1 -> 0.

CUSTOM: The snapshot data capture is triggered by API Dem_StoreCustomTriggeredFreezeFrame(). An event can only use this snapshot trigger, when the corresponding event memory has DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots > 0


Please note:
For all triggers except CUSTOM, snapshot trigger occuring before an event memory entry is allocated are postponed until the event memory entry gets allocated.
Snapshot trigger CUSTOM can capture snapshot data even before an event memory entry is allocated to the event.

######## 2. DemFreezeFrameRecordUpdate

- **UUID**: `7df03bce-c9ad-44a7-ae40-478bcd85c0a0`
- **参数名**: `DemFreezeFrameRecordUpdate`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_NO` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the case, when the freeze frame record is stored/updated.

DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_NO - The snapshot record is captured only once.

DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES - The snapshot is captured with each trigger, overwriting a previously captured snapshot.

If option DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES is chosen for a snapshot record with DemFreezeFrameRecordTrigger CONFIRMED the snapshot record is initally stored on ConfirmedDTC transition 0 -> 1 and updated with each following TestFailed transition 0 -> 1.

If option DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES is chosen for a time series snapshot record (see DemFreezeFrameRecordIsTimeSeries) all past samples are overwritten and future samples are collected anew with each trigger.

Therefore the multiplicity of this parameter is limited to 2.

##### 52. DemJ1939FreezeFrameClass

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `0b7d2115-c661-4032-8a58-06f82f50e121`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemJ1939FreezeFrameClass`
- **多重性**: [0..2]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the combinations of SPNs s for a J1939 relevant freeze frame.

Your configuration must use one (1) common definition for all J1939 freeze frames and one (1) common definition for all J1939 expanded freeze frames (use the event parameters DemJ1939FreezeFrameClassRef and DemJ1939ExpandedFreezeFrameClassRef to use this definitions). Therefore the multiplicity of this parameter is limited to 2.

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

##### 55. DemGeneralOBD

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `60b17873-7fa3-473e-9bd1-93daeb106df2`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemGeneralOBD`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container will be used only, if the OBD support (for either OBD II or WWH-OBD or OBD on UDS or ZEV on UDS) is activated (see DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport and DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation).

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **30** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemOBDCompliancy

- **UUID**: `10ab62bd-ca23-44b3-bbc9-6b8268f6e2dd`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDCompliancy`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Configuration value to define the appropriate value to PID$1C "OBD requirements to which vehicle or engine is certified." according to the respective standards, e.g. OBD I, OBD II, JOBD etc.

######## 2. DemOBDPermanentDtcActivationDistance

- **UUID**: `025b6420-804d-4557-913f-9914fb9aac23`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDPermanentDtcActivationDistance`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0..4294967295]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
Distance after first valid Odometer value was provided after which permanent DTC storage is activated.

The resolution is the same as for PID$21 and PID$31 (see parameter DemOBDInputDistanceInformation).

Providing this parameter will also enable an API to activate permanent DTC storage before the configured distance is reached.

If this parameter is missing, permanent DTC storage is always active and can't be suppressed.

######## 3. DemOBDPermanentChronologyMaxNumberEvents

- **UUID**: `ca228b3b-0d8a-40b0-9c29-94af5d2b1838`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDPermanentChronologyMaxNumberEvents`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Maximum number of events which can be stored in the chronology of confirmed events. 

This chronology is used to identify the next DTC to be stored in the permanent memory, if the permanent memory was full and an entry gets freed.
Each event in the chronology adds two more bytes to the Dem's status NvRAM block.

It is recommended to configure a value that corresponds to the maximum number of DTCs fulfilling the criteria for permanent storage at the same time in a (realistic) worst case scenario. 
To ensure that always the oldest confirmed DTC is stored in the permanent memory or if NvRam consumption is not a critical restriction, set the value to the maximum number of DTCs having the MIL attached (OBDII or OBDonUDS) or the maximum number of class A and B1 DTCs (WWHOBD).

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemOBDCentralizedPID21Handling

######## 4. DemDelayWarmUpCycleToDcyEnd

- **UUID**: `d5c8fca2-d718-4822-beef-a6dd55bb448c`
- **参数名**: `DemDelayWarmUpCycleToDcyEnd`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If enabled the DEM can only be triggered for the start of warm-Up cycle and it will ignore the trigger for the end of warm-up cycle. 
It will automatically restart the warm-up cycle at the end of a (qualified) driving cycle. 

Therefore the DTCs that have configured the warm-up cycle as their aging cycle will age at the end of the driving cycle.

######## 5. DemOBDFreezeFrameInService19

- **UUID**: `7b2af849-0cac-49ec-ba44-80c210aedfed`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDFreezeFrameInService19`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II legislation.
Report the OBD II freeze frame in UDS Service $19 04 (with record number 0x00 or wildcard 0xFF).

If enabled the OBD II Freeze Frame can be accessed in diagnostic service $19 04 and in Mode 02
otherwise only in Mode 02.

######## 6. DemOBDHideOccurrences

- **UUID**: `74b28e70-a283-4e95-9c46-9b9479259d47`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDHideOccurrences`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
Dependent OBD DTCs shall not appear in some UDS $19 sub-services.

If enabled, the Dem hides dependent OBD DTCs from reporting.
Also, operation 'HideOccurrences' in service 'DemOBDServices' is provided to change this behavior at run-time.
Dependent OBD DTCs are identified via the parameter 'DemEventSignificance'. A setting of 'OCCURRENCE' marks a dependent DTC.

Note: Non-OBD DTCs are not affected by this feature.

######## 7. DemOBDPermanentEraseConditionDcy

- **UUID**: `4a3211e5-041f-4c92-aeb5-924493ee2944`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDPermanentEraseConditionDcy`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
This parameter controls the behavior of a permanent fault after a clear DTC command issued while the OBD driving cycle (DCY) is not yet qualified.

If enabled, the Dem checks the DCY qualification state during a Clear DTC request.
- DCY not qualified: The permanent fault code can be removed from permanent memory in the same DCY as the clear DTC request was issued.
- DCY qualified: The permanent fault code cannot be removed from permanent memory in the same DCY as the clear DTC request was issued.

If disabled, the DCY qualification state has no effect.

Note: To remove a DTC from permanent memory the PFC condition must be fulfilled and the DTC must be tested as passed and not failed.

######## 8. DemOBDPermanentMandatoryPfcAfterClear

- **UUID**: `0c99d3d4-6860-41be-aeff-d72ce66d38ea`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDPermanentMandatoryPfcAfterClear`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
This configuration switch specifies, when a DTC will be removed from the permanent memory after the fault memory was cleared (e.g. Mode $04) and the DTC illuminates the MIL again.

If this option is enabled, the DTC will be removed from permanent memory only if PFC cycle was active and MIL becomes inactive for this DTC.

If this option is disabled, the DTC will be removed from permanent memory after the MIL becomes inactive for this event.

######## 9. DemOBDRestartDcyOnClearDTC

- **UUID**: `fd16d8f9-8e06-491d-8ff5-21b442ae18a2`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDRestartDcyOnClearDTC`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: With ClearDTC (Mode $04 or Service $14FFFFFF) the DEM may restart the OBD driving cycle independent of its current qualification state.

If enabled, the DEM restarts the OBD driving cycle (configured as DemOperationCycleType==DEM_OPCYC_OBD_DCY) with Mode $04 or Service $14 FFFFFF requests.

If disabled, no cycle is restarted with Mode $04 or Service $14 FFFFFF requests.

######## 13. DemReportBlinkingMilAsActive

- **UUID**: `5e810727-ccac-416a-9297-e4441885be90`
- **参数名**: `DemReportBlinkingMilAsActive`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If enabled, the DEM reports the MIL as 'activated' if its current state is BLINKING and/or CONTINUOUS.
If disabled, the DEM reports the MIL as 'activated' only if its current state is CONTINUOUS (see parameter Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemIndicatorAttribute/DemIndicatorBehaviour).

This influences the behavior of PID $01, PID $F501, PID $21 and PID $4D.

######## 14. DemOBDFuelSystemAlwaysComplete

- **UUID**: `05650250-acae-4891-9e1a-4f584cb94497`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDFuelSystemAlwaysComplete`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This switch describes whether the completeness status for fuel system monitors is calculated or always indicated as complete in:
- PID $01,
- PID $41,
- PID $F501.
- J1939 diagnostic readiness 1 and
- J1939 diagnostic readiness 3.

True: Fuel system monitoring is always reported as complete.
False: Completeness of fuel system monitoring is calculated based on non-continuous fuel system monitor status.

######## 15. DemOBDCompressionMisfireAlwaysComplete

- **UUID**: `0d3b0c2a-5e83-410d-8e67-460eda73eb2b`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDCompressionMisfireAlwaysComplete`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This switch describes whether the completeness status for misfire monitors is calculated or always indicated as complete for compression ignition engines (DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDEngineType == DEM_IGNITION_COMPRESSION) in
- PID $01,
- PID $41,
- J1939 diagnostic readiness 1 and
- J1939 diagnostic readiness 3.

In the data contexts above, misfire monitors will always indicate completed for spark ignition engines independently of this parameter value.

For PID $F501, the completion of readiness group Misfire is always calculated.

True: Misfire monitoring is always reported as complete.
False: Completeness of misfire monitors is calculated.

######## 16. DemOBDPermanentDtcImmediateReport

- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
TRUE: All Permanent Dtcs are available via OBD  mode 0A immediately after WIR bit is visible
FALSE: Only DTCs for which /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification is disabled and the /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEventFailureCycleCounterThreshold is configured to 0 are visible in mode 0A immediately, all other DTCs only after OBD driving cycle is restarted.

######## 19. DemSupportedObdUdsDtcSeparation

- **UUID**: `d6a63781-a28e-413b-bf17-15a7f005839a`
- **参数名**: `DemSupportedObdUdsDtcSeparation`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBDonUDS legislation.

When this parameter is set to true, the OBD UDS DTC separation feature is enabled, i.e. the configured 3 byte OBD DTC value (DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemObdDTC3Byte) is reported in all OBDonUDS related services (see SAE J1979-2).
If this parameter is set to false, the OBD UDS DTC separation is disabled, i.e. the UDS DTC value (DemConfigSet/DemDTCClass/DemUdsDTC) is reported instead. This is the default behavior.

######## 20. DemDisableEventMonitoringInCurrentDcy

- **描述**: This switch allows the user to disable event monitoring in current driving cycle. 
Usage of the API Dem_DisableEventMonitoringInCurrentDcy() will make the event be considered as tested completed during the readiness calculation of the following readiness data:

- PID $F501.
- DM05.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. DemEngineRuntimePolling

- **UUID**: `fa17ad0f-050e-4f66-ae24-4b517675627e`
- **参数名**: `DemEngineRuntimePolling`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `60`
- **取值范围**: [0.001..2678400]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Allow to configure the time for the periodic polling of the engine runtime in minutes.

The value must be a multiple of the DemGeneral/DemTaskTime. Otherwise it is rounded down to the next multiple value of DemTaskTime.

This value is mandatory, when parameter DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStart is required. If missing, it defaults to the DemTaskTime.

The AUTOSAR configuration standard is to use SI units, so in the EcuC file this parameter is stored as float value in seconds - the DaVinci Configurator 5 GUI will convert the value when using a diffenent unit.

######## 2. DemEngineRuntimeSecondsPolling

- **UUID**: `8f873a6d-21c4-44ef-b809-ed7273208466`
- **参数名**: `DemEngineRuntimeSecondsPolling`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `60`
- **取值范围**: [0.001..2678400]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Allow to configure the time for the periodic polling of the engine runtime in seconds.

The value must be a multiple of the DemGeneral/DemTaskTime. Otherwise it is rounded down to the next multiple value of DemTaskTime.

This value is mandatory, when parameter DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStartSeconds is required. If missing, it defaults to the DemTaskTime.

The AUTOSAR configuration standard is to use SI units, so in the EcuC file this parameter is stored as float value in seconds - the DaVinci Configurator 5 GUI will convert the value when using a diffenent unit.

######## 3. DemOBDFreezeFrameBehavior

- **UUID**: `de28e936-2584-4c24-b250-7ebbc14a01fc`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDFreezeFrameBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_OBDII_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDII_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDII_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE3` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDII_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE4` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_OBDII_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE5` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_WWHOBD_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_WWHOBD_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_COMBINED_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_COMBINED_FREEZE_FRAME_BEHAVIOR_TYPE2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This configuration value defines, when the OBD II resp. WWH-OBD Freeze Frame is taken, stored and updated and when/how it is visible for diagnostic requests.

DemOBDFreezeFrameBehavior has no effect on OBDonUDS Freeze Frame storage.The OBDonUDS FF is always stored when trigger conditions are met.

OBD II, TYPE 1: OBD II Freeze Frame data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
If PendingDTC is healed without being ConfirmedDTC, the Freeze Frame will be cleared.
The Freeze Frame is immediately visible in Mode02.

OBD II, TYPE 2: OBD II Freeze Frame data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
If PendingDTC is healed without being ConfirmedDTC, the Freeze Frame will be cleared.
The Freeze Frame is visible as soon as the ConfirmedDTC status bit of the DTC is visible. The visibility depends on parameter /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification. If this parameter is enabled, visibility is delayed until the OBD driving cycle is qualified.
This option allows multiple Freeze Frames (see parameter /DemGeneral/DemMaxNumberEventEntryOBDFreezeFrame).

OBD II, TYPE 3: OBD II Freeze Frame data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
The Pending Freeze Frame will be displaced if another DTC becomes a ConfirmedDTC.
If PendingDTC is healed without being ConfirmedDTC the Freeze Frame will be cleared.
The Freeze Frame is immediately visible in Mode02.

OBD II, TYPE 4: OBD II Freeze Frame  data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
The Pending Freeze Frame will be updated if the DTC becomes a ConfirmedDTC.
If PendingDTC is healed without being ConfirmedDTC the Freeze Frame will be cleared.
The Freeze Frame is immediately visible in Mode02.

OBD II, TYPE 5: OBD II Freeze Frame data capture is triggered when the trip condition is fulfilled.
The Freeze Frame is visible as soon as the ConfirmedDTC status bit of the DTC is visible. The visibility depends on parameter /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification. If this parameter is enabled, visibility is delayed until the OBD driving cycle is qualified.
For DTCs related to misfire or fuel system monitoring
(DemEventOBDReadinessGroup = DEM_OBD_RDY_MISF or DEM_OBD_RDY_FLSYS or DEM_OBD_RDY_FLSYS_NONCONT) the Freeze Frame can also become visible without the need for a qualified driving cycle in some edge cases. 
A stored but not yet visible freeze frame will be displaced by the freeze frame of an event with parameter DemOBDVisibilityDelayedUntilDcyQualification disabled, if the invisible freeze frame is not related to a misfire and fuel system monitor.
For more information refer to chapter "Reporting of Freeze Frame with Mode $02"  in the OBD Technical Reference.

WWH-OBD, TYPE 1: WWH-OBD Freeze Frame data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
The Freeze Frame is immediately visible

WWH-OBD, TYPE 2: WWH-OBD  Freeze Frame  data capture is triggered by the initial PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.
If DTC becomes a ConfirmedDTC the Freeze Frame data will be updated.
The Freeze Frame is immediately visible.

COMBINED, TYPE1: This behavior corresponds to 'Type 1' for OBDII and 'Type 1' for WWH-OBD.

COMBINED, TYPE2: This behavior corresponds to 'Type 4' for OBDII and 'Type 2' for WWH-OBD.

Use COMBINED values if and only if using OBDII and WWH-OBD in different PBS variants within one configuration.

######## 4. DemOBDLegislation

- **UUID**: `5b05ab0b-5fd6-46ba-82fd-2bc63673fdc8`
- **参数名**: `DemOBDLegislation`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_OBDII`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_OBDII`
  - `DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_WWHOBD`
  - `DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_OBD_ON_UDS`
  - `DEM_OBD_LEGISLATION_ZEV_ON_UDS`

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The OBD legislation that shall be supported.

OBDII: Support of OBD II.

WWHOBD: Support of WWH-OBD.

OBD_ON_UDS: Support of OBDonUDS.

ZEV_ON_UDS: Support of ZEVonUDS.

If OBD support is disabled (Dem/DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport == DEM_OBD_NO_OBD_SUPPORT), this parameter is ignored.

######## 5. DemClearPermanentDtcBehavior

- **UUID**: `25ac03db-499b-45b0-806e-d66ec9e2ea95`
- **参数名**: `DemClearPermanentDtcBehavior`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEM_CLR_PERM_DTC_TYPE_1`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_CLR_PERM_DTC_TYPE_1` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_CLR_PERM_DTC_TYPE_2` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Only relevant for OBD II and OBD on UDS legislation.
DEM_CLR_PERM_DTC_TYPE_1 (default):
    Permanent DTC is erased if only tested passed (and not tested failed) during the driving cycle and if permanent fault code condition is fulfilled.
DEM_CLR_PERM_DTC_TYPE_2:
   Permanent DTC with IUMPR monitoring is erased if only tested passed (and not tested failed) during the driving cycle.
   Permanent DTC without IUMPR monitoring is erased if only tested passed (and not tested failed) during the driving cycle and if permanent fault code condition is fulfilled.

######## 4. DemOBDInputDistanceInformation

- **引用名**: `DemOBDInputDistanceInformation`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `DemDataClass`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Dem/DemGeneral/DemDataClass`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This reference requests the current odometer for PID21, PID31, J1939 diagnostic readiness 2,  and permanent DTC storage activation distance calculation, therefore this reference is mandatory if one those features is supported.

The returned odometer value (4 Byte) must have a resolution of 1 kilometer or 1 mile.
Furthermore, the odometer value must be provided in big endian format.

If no valid odometer is available the return value must be E_NOT_OK.
Furthermore the permanent DTC storage activation distance calculation is not performed until a valid odometer value is received.

######## 9. DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStart

- **UUID**: `478c86e5-c1e3-4330-96dd-af24ed4db9aa`
- **引用名**: `DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStart`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **目标**: `DemDataClass`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Dem/DemGeneral/DemDataClass`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This reference requests the current engine runtime in minutes for PID4D, PID4E, J1939 diagnostic readiness 2, WWH-OBD aging and B1 counter calculation. This reference is also used by the Dem to track the cumulative engine runtime with PTO active if any event is affected by the Dem PTO handling (see /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemConsiderPtoStatus) and the Dem calculates the monitor status since DTCs cleared for PID $01, PID $F501 or J1939 diagnostic readiness 1.
Therefore, this reference is mandatory if one of those features is supported.

The returned engine runtime value (4 Byte) must have a resolution of 1 minute.
Furthermore, the engine runtime value must be provided in big endian format.
If no valid engine runtime is available the return value must be E_NOT_OK.

######## 10. DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStartSeconds

- **UUID**: `6b69edaa-43ae-4fd0-92eb-6044032602a4`
- **引用名**: `DemOBDTimeSinceEngineStartSeconds`

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This reference requests the current engine runtime in seconds for J1939 diagnostic readiness 3. Therefore, this reference is mandatory if this feature is supported.

The returned engine runtime value (4 Byte) must have a resolution of 1 second.
Furthermore, the engine runtime value must be provided in big endian format.
If no valid engine runtime is available the return value must be E_NOT_OK.

##### 57. DemGroupOfDTC

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `19afb70b-2598-4db7-a9f5-93af9eac05ef`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemGroupOfDTC`
- **多重性**: [0..4]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents a DTC group.

DTC groups can be cleared by service $14, if allowed by the OEM specification.
In case only $14 FFFFFF shall be offered, it is recommended to not configure DTC groups.

Defined groups are sorted according their 'DemGroupDTCs' value in increasing sequence and get assigned these semantics: (1) PowertrainGroup, (2) ChassisGroup, (3) BodyGroup, (4) NetworkGroup.
For example when you define only 2 groups, the lower GroupDTC value represents the PowertrainGroup, the higher GroupDTC value represents the ChassisGroup. Undefined are Body- and NetworkGroup.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **1** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemGroupDTCs

- **UUID**: `d2def932-ef99-44f4-aa12-d536fc26c003`
- **参数名**: `DemGroupDTCs`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..16777214]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: DTC value of the selected group of DTC.

A clear DTC request for this number will clear all DTCs that belong to this group.

##### 59. DemNvRamBlockId

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `8fa84fe4-ca3d-4395-b3b1-343b598e8e44`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemNvRamBlockId`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represent a non-volatile memory block used by the Dem.

If no permanent storage of event memory entries is required, no block needs to be configured.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemNvRamBlockIdIndex

- **UUID**: `eaf25db9-dccd-4fea-ab19-a3871265b0c2`
- **参数名**: `DemNvRamBlockIdIndex`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: For non-unique block types, this index identifies the exact block (see DemNvRamBlockIdType).

This parameter must exist and be able to identify all NvBlocks of following kind:
PRIMARY: each index must have an unique value and the index values must complete the range 0 - (DemMaxNumberEventEntryPrimary -1),
USERDEFINED: for all blocks with the same DemNvRamBlockIdEventMemoryRef reference, each index must have an unique value and all index values must complete the range 0 - (DemMaxNumberEventEntryUserDefined -1), 
TIMESERIES: for all blocks with the same DemNvRamBlockIdEventMemoryRef reference, each index must have an unique value and all index values must complete the range 0 - (DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots -1), 

The value is necessary to store the correct memory entry in case it was changed.

For all NvBlocks of other kind (e.g. ADMIN, STATUS,...) the parameter value will be ignored.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemNvRamBlockIdType

- **UUID**: `dc3444c5-8096-4fdc-bf0c-a2357b5a16cf`
- **参数名**: `DemNvRamBlockIdType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_ADMIN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_STATUS` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_PRIMARY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_PERMANENT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_MIRROR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_FREEZEFRAME` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_OBDIUMPR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_DEBOUNCE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_AVAILABILITY` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_TIMESERIES` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_DTR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_AGING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_CYCLECOUNTER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_USERDEFINED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEM_NVBLOCK_CUSTOMTRIGGER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Usage kind of this NvBlock.

This parameter is used to identify which block data needs to be stored when event data is modified.

Following block types exist at most once:
ADMIN: Always required.

STATUS: Always required.

MIRROR: Only required if DemGeneral/DemMaxNumberEventEntryMirror > 0.

PERMANENT: Only required if DemGeneral/DemMaxNumberEventEntryPermanent > 0.

FREEZEFRAME: Only required if DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport is enabled and DemGeneral/DemGeneralOBD/DemOBDLegislation == OBDII or OBD_ON_UDS.

OBDIUMPR: Only required if DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport is enabled and DemGeneral/DemRatioId containers are configured.

DEBOUNCE: Only required if any event uses CounterBased Debouncing with DemDebounceCounterStorage==TRUE.

AVAILABILITY: Only required if event availability status is persisted in non-volatile memory due DemAvailabilityStorage==TRUE.

DTR: Only required if DemGeneral/DemOBDSupport is enabled and
if DemConfigSet/DemDtrs/DemDtr container exist or DemGeneral/DemPostBuild/DemMaxNumberEventEntryPermanent > 0

AGING: Only required if aging data is persisted in non-volatile memory due to DemGeneral/DemSupportAgingForAllDTCs==TRUE or if DemGeneral/DemDataClass/DemDataElementInternalData == AGED_COUNTER is used.

CYCLECOUNTER: Only required if cycle counter data is persisted in non-volatile memory due to DemGeneral/DemSupportStorageIndependentCycleCounters ==TRUE.

Following block types can exist several times, depending on the configuration:
PRIMARY: The number of containers of this type must match DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemPrimaryMemory/DemMaxNumberEventEntryPrimary.

USERDEFINED: The number of containers of this type must match the summarized number of all DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemUserDefinedMemory/DemMaxNumberEventEntryUserDefined.

TIMESERIES: The number of containers of this type must match the summarized number of DemMaxNumberTimeSeriesSnapshots in DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemPrimaryMemory and all DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemUserDefinedMemory containers.

CUSTOMTRIGGER: The number of containers of this type must match the summarized number of DemMaxNumberCustomTriggeredSnapshots in DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemPrimaryMemory and all DemGeneral/DemEventMemorySet/DemUserDefinedMemory containers.

此容器包含 **2** 个引用：

- **UUID**: `d39e7578-eff3-4356-944b-5b32c0dc1516`
- **引用名**: `DemNvRamBlockIdEventMemoryRef`
- **引用类型**: CHOICE-REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL

- **目标引用列表**:

- **描述**: This parameter must ONLY exist and contain a reference to the event memory for the following DemNvRamBlockIdType values

- DEM_NVBLOCK_USERDEFINED: a configured user defined memory must be referenced
- DEM_NVBLOCK_TIMESERIES: either the primary memory or a configured user defined memory must be referenced

##### 60. DemOperationCycle

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `c83e8feb-949f-4f68-a66a-994925cfcf3d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER

**容器描述**:

This container represents a monitoring cycle.

They are used to control event monitoring and aging.
Although this container does not define a symbolic name value, one is generated nonetheless.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemOperationCycleAutomaticEnd

- **UUID**: `ef4c8580-cc34-49fc-b47b-e324bd52f96e`
- **参数名**: `DemOperationCycleAutomaticEnd`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If configured to true the DEM automatically ends the cycle during ECU restart (e.g. in Dem_Shutdown or Dem_Init).
Otherwise the application has to end the cycle by calling Dem_SetOperationCycleState with state DEM_CYCLE_STATE_END.
This option is only relevant when DemOperationCycleStatusStorage is TRUE.

- **描述**: The autostart property defines if the operation cycles is automatically (re-)started during Dem_PreInit.
(Not supported, please use DemGeneral/DemRestartCycleOnInitRef instead)

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemOperationCycleType

##### 61. DemRatioId

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `73ce34d3-896d-489a-a44a-f8f3b612bb4d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemRatioId`
- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the OBD specific ratio Id configuration.

It is related to a specific event, a FID, and an IUMPR group.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemIUMPRDenGroup

- **UUID**: `68dc8bca-d17d-4cdf-b71a-0c257975cee4`
- **参数名**: `DemIUMPRDenGroup`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_NONE`
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_PHYS_API`
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_COLDSTART`
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_EVAP`
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_500MI`
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_INDEPENDENT` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the assigned denominator type which is applied in addition to the General Denominator conditions.

NONE: The denominator is calculated based on the general individual denominator conditions.

PHYS_API: The denominator is calculated based on the general individual denominator conditions. The denominator can be locked via dedicated APIs.

COLDSTART, EVAP, 500MI: The denominator is calculated based on the general individual denominator conditions and additional conditions that must be fulfilled before the denominator is incremented.

INDEPENDENT: Denominator may be incremented independently of the general individual denominator. The denominator may be locked via dedicated APIs.

######## 2. DemIUMPRGroup

- **UUID**: `ca2d446b-771f-41d1-8c64-20374c2e40c9`
- **参数名**: `DemIUMPRGroup`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_IUMPR_BOOSTPRS` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)

Defines a unique ratio Id.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemIUMPRDenGroup

- **UUID**: `5eedbc07-1b95-41c2-8270-ac4fc5d85871`
- **参数名**: `DemIUMPRDenGroup`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_500MILL` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_COLDSTART` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_EVAP` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_NONE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `DEM_IUMPR_DEN_PHYS_API` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: (Not supported yet, please use the AUTOSAR 4.0.3 compliant definition in /Dem/DemGeneral/DemRatioId)
This parameter specifies the assigned denominator type which is applied in addition to the General Denominator conditions.

NONE: The denominator is calculated based on the general denominator conditions.

PHYS_API: The denominator can be locked via dedicated APIs.

COLDSTART, EVAP, 500MILL: Additional conditions must be fulfilled before the denominator is incremented.

##### 63. DemStorageCondition

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `a432a244-2e29-4339-bf31-2c41a34c254f`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemStorageCondition`
- **多重性**: [0..255]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents a single storage condition.

Please note: storage conditions can not be used directly but must be grouped (see DemStorageConditionGroup).

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemStorageConditionId

- **UUID**: `3e651eec-8a41-49b0-8aa1-ec321c0fba8e`
- **参数名**: `DemStorageConditionId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `255`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Internal identifier of the storage condition.

This number is calculated internally and shall not be configured manually. This parameter is only available to publish the result of this calculation.
Applications using Dem APIs must not rely on the value of this parameter. Instead, they must use the symbolic constant (SymbolicNameValue) DemConf_DemStorageCondition_{SHORT-NAME}.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemStorageConditionStatus

- **UUID**: `6fd4afac-83e4-4efe-8b83-382797b8e1c6`
- **参数名**: `DemStorageConditionStatus`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the status of this storage condition after full initialization, i.e. when Dem_MasterInit() or Dem_Init() finished.
After pre-initialization, but before the full intialization (between Dem_(Master)PreInit() and Dem_(Master)Init() ) the status is always 'true'. 
Changes to the storage condition status during runtime are not persisted non-volatile. The status of this storage condition will always be reset to this configuration value during Dem_MasterInit() or Dem_Init().

##### 66. DemMILGroup

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `f39f273a-df0a-41bf-87cd-4e75daad88c5`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemMILGroup`
- **多重性**: [0..65534]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents a group of events that use a common trip counter to determine their MIL status.

Incrementing the common trip counter is triggered by any event of the MIL group.
Healing is performed individually for each event of the MIL group, but the common trip counter is only reset after all events of the group have been healed.

The reference to a MIL group is configured at the DTCClass container.

##### 67. DemPostbuild

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `3ff1ce95-0712-4425-9b33-16133c624ea3`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemPostbuild`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

The content of this container is only relevant for the implementation variant PostBuild (POST-BUILD-LOADABLE).

For implementation variant PreCompile (and therfore PostBuild-Selectable without -Loadable) all contained parameters are ignored.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **8** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemMaxSizeFreezeFrame

- **UUID**: `3ee0662a-6aa2-4f2c-8dd3-29e5b7e94dc4`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxSizeFreezeFrame`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) size of the largest FreezeFrame record.

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents the size of largest defined FreezeFrame record. The minimum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve additional space in the NvRAM blocks. Later in the post-build phase you can choose different DIDs (or a different number of DIDs) for a defined FreezeFrame - as long as the size of the new selected DIDs still fit into the reserved space.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value (when it is smaller than the configured, largest FreezeFrame's size. Changing the FreezeFrame's DIDs afterwards may shorten the largest FreezeFrame's size - but this will NOT decrease this value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum size.

"Size of a freeze frame" is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDidClasses in a DemGeneral/DemFreezeFrameClass. The size of a DidClass is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemNNDataElementClass elements by adding their DemDataElementDataSize values.

######## 2. DemMaxNumberDtr

- **UUID**: `0f0473c7-09c2-4954-8b6b-3f9eb77ab955`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberDtr`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) to store an upper limit of Dtr elements.

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents an upper limit to the maximum value (+1) of the DemDtrId, calculated over all variants. This maximum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve additional number of Dtr elements. Later in the post-build phase you can change Dtr configuration (also create and delete Dtrs) - as long as the maximum value (+1) of the DemDtrId calculated over all variants is smaller than this value.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value (when it is smaller than the configured). Deleting Dtrs afterwards may decrease the lowest possible value - but this will NOT decrease this configured value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum size.

######## 3. DemMaxSizeGlobalFreezeFrame

- **UUID**: `b09925ef-9e62-4381-b7dc-b4fe1e54a75f`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxSizeGlobalFreezeFrame`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) size of the global freeze frame record.

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents the size of the global freeze frame record. The minimum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve additional space in the NvRAM blocks. Later in the post-build phase you can choose different DIDs (or a different number of DIDs) for the global freeze frame - as long as the size of the new selected DIDs still fits into the reserved space.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value when it is smaller than the configured global freeze frame size. Changing the freeze frame's DIDs afterwards may shorten the global freeze frame size - but this will NOT decrease this value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum size.

"Size of the global freeze frame" is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDidClasses in the DemGeneral/DemGlobalFreezeFrame/DemGlobalFreezeFrameClassRef. The size of a DidClass is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDataClass elements by adding their DemDataElementDataSize values.

######## 4. DemMaxSizeTimeSeriesFreezeFrame

- **UUID**: `c0aadee5-9ec7-49c4-baa0-b103fe5f0d72`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxSizeTimeSeriesFreezeFrame`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) size of the time series snapshot records (also known as multi-sample development snapshot records).

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents the size of a time series snapshot record. The minimum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve additional space in the NvRAM blocks. Later in the post-build phase you can choose different DIDs (or a different number of DIDs) for the time series snapshot record - as long as the size of the new selected DIDs still fits into the reserved space.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value when it is smaller than the configured time series snapshot record size. Changing the freeze frame's DIDs afterwards may shorten the time series snapshot record size - but this will NOT decrease this value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum size.

"Size of the time series snapshot record" is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDidClasses in the DemGeneral/DemFreezeFrameClass used in DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemDevFreezeFrameBClassRef. The size of a DidClass is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDataClass elements by adding their DemDataElementDataSize values.

######## 5. DemMaxSizeOBDonUDSFreezeFrame

- **UUID**: `921d9ca5-5933-4f37-b5fa-8238e34654c6`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxSizeOBDonUDSFreezeFrame`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) size of the largest OBD on UDS FreezeFrame record.

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents the size of largest defined OBD on UDS FreezeFrame record. The minimum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve additional space in the NvRAM blocks. Later in the post-build phase you can choose different DIDs (or a different number of DIDs) for a defined FreezeFrame - as long as the size of the new selected DIDs still fit into the reserved space.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value (when it is smaller than the configured, largest FreezeFrame's size. Changing the FreezeFrame's DIDs afterwards may shorten the largest FreezeFrame's size - but this will NOT decrease this value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum size.

"Size of a freeze frame" is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDidClasses in a DemGeneral/DemFreezeFrameClass. The size of a DidClass is calculated as: Summarize the size of all referred DemDataElement Class elements by adding their DemDataElementDataSize values.

######## 6. DemMaxNumberEventsWithMonitorActivityData

- **UUID**: `ea71973e-388d-4ba4-9415-306164a65678`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberEventsWithMonitorActivityData`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is only relevant for OBD on UDS legislation.

Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) to store an upper limit of Monitor Activity Data (see Dem/DemGeneral/DemDataClass/DemDataElementInternalData MONITOR_ACTIVITY_DATA).

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents an upper limit for the number of events for which Monitor Activity Data can be stored. This maximum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve an additional number of events with Monitor Activity Data. Later in the post-build phase you can change the DemExtendedDataClassRef of an event and thereby the DemExtendedDataRecordClasses referenced by an event - as long as the maximum number of events with Monitor Activity Data is smaller or equal to this value.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value (when it is smaller than the configured). Changing DemExtendedDataClassRefs or DemExtendedDataClasses afterwards may decrease the lowest possible value - but this will NOT decrease this configured value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum number.

######## 7. DemMaxNumberEventsPtoTimer

- **UUID**: `ae05d2b6-c2fa-4f4e-b620-05817e6a8c99`
- **参数名**: `DemMaxNumberEventsPtoTimer`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is only relevant if any event is affected by the Dem PTO handling (see /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemConsiderPtoStatus) and the Dem calculates the monitor status since DTCs cleared for PID $01, PID $F501 or J1939 diagnostic readiness 1.
For each event affected by the Dem PTO handling that has an OBD Readiness group (see /DemEventParameter/DemEventClass/DemEventOBDReadinessGroup), an event specific PTO timer is maintained to track the cumulative engine runtime with PTO active.

Enlarge the (RAM/NvRAM) to store an upper limit of event specific PTO timers.

This parameter is mandatory for implementation variant PostBuild-Loadable, it will be ignored for other implementation variants.

It represents an upper limit for the number of events for which a PTO timer can be maintained. This maximum value is auto-calculated when you start "Validate" or "Generate" - set value 0 to force the recalculation of the min value.
For PostBuild-Loadable variants, during the pre-compile configuration phase you can enlarge the value to reserve an additional number of events with PTO timer. Later in the post-build phase you can change the DemEventOBDReadinessGroup of an event as long as the maximum number of events with PTO timer is smaller or equal to this value.

Take care: for PostBuild-Loadable implementation variants the auto-calculation will only increase the value (when it is smaller than the configured). Changing the DemEventOBDReadinessGroup of an event afterwards may decrease the lowest possible value - but this will NOT decrease this configured value again! Set the value to '0' and start "Validate" or "Generate" for the DEM to calculate again the minimum number.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemEventAvailableDefaultSupport

- **UUID**: `a2c3b23c-057e-405f-8366-a150ae474dc0`
- **参数名**: `DemEventAvailableDefaultSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Select whether the default availability value should be initialized with Dem_Init()

TRUE: Use the default values from configuration parameter DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemEventAvailable to set the initial value during Dem_Init()
FALSE: The availability information is not changed during Dem_Init()

If this setting is enabled, API Dem_SetEventAvailable cannot be used before Dem_Init() due to Dem_Init() overwriting the active setting.

##### 68. DemGlobalFreezeFrame

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `67f0449a-4ff9-4447-9248-714bc29dcd4f`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemGlobalFreezeFrame`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

In this container the attributes and content of the global freeze frame can be configured.
If this container is configured, global freeze frame data is stored for each event (In addition to Dem/DemConfigSet/DemEventParameter/DemFreezeFrameClassRef).

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **3** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordNumber

- **UUID**: `4870318e-6a16-4d0c-85f2-da38ed904f12`
- **参数名**: `DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordNumber`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..254]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the record number of the global freeze frame.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordUpdate

- **UUID**: `c90b288a-8f22-4767-be6e-5705146c1c31`
- **参数名**: `DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordUpdate`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_NO`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines, whether the global freeze frame is updated with each trigger.

DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_NO: The global freeze frame is captured only once.

DEM_UPDATE_RECORD_YES: The global freeze frame is captured with each trigger, overwriting the previously captured data (currently not supported).

######## 2. DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordTrigger

- **UUID**: `bb056dea-a439-4c1f-b4bc-9a12e664bf5a`
- **参数名**: `DemGlobalFreezeFrameRecordTrigger`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEM_TRIGGER_ON_CONFIRMED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the trigger to store the global freeze frame. Currently only trigger CONFIRMED is supported.

FDC_THRESHOLD: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by a fault detection counter passing a storage threshold.

CONFIRMED: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by the ConfirmedDTC transition from 0 to 1.

PENDING: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by the PendingDTC transition from 0 to 1.

TEST_FAILED: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by the TestFailed transition from 0 to 1.

TEST_FAILED_FIRST_IN_CYCLE: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by the first TestFailed transition from 0 to 1 in an operation cycle.

FDC_FIRST_IN_CYCLE: The global freeze frame capture is triggered by the first DTC fault detection counter reaching the storage threshold in an operation cycle.

Please note that triggers occuring before an event memory entry is allocated are discarded.

###### 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

####### REFERENCE 类型引用

- **引用类型**: REFERENCE
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **目标**: `DemFreezeFrameClass`
- **完整路径**: `/MICROSAR/Dem/DemGeneral/DemFreezeFrameClass`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter references the list of DIDs to be stored in the global freeze frame.

##### 69. DemTimeSeriesSnapshotClass

if the use of time series snapshot records (also known as multi-sample development snapshot records) is configured for the event.

######## 3. DemTimeSeriesSampleBufferSize

- **UUID**: `2185edf7-0941-4ea1-8752-015681d67103`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSampleBufferSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration value defines the size of the ring buffer in RAM in which the collected DIDs are stored. The value is multiplied with the number of past samples configured in DemGeneral/DemTimeSeriesSnapshotClass/DemTimeSeriesMaxNumberPastSamples.

The ring buffer has to be large enough to store all DIDs relevant for events referencing this DemTimeSeriesSnapshotClass container. You get an error message when the configured value is too small.

######## 4. DemTimeSeriesStartingSequentialDIDIdentifier

- **UUID**: `538b0c2f-13d3-4b0d-9c60-441847384d29`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesStartingSequentialDIDIdentifier`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE

- **描述**: This configuration value defines the starting sequential DID identifier.
For events referencing this DemTimeSeriesSnapshotClass container, the sequential DID identifiers start from this value.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. DemTimeSeriesSamplingTime

- **UUID**: `c77cf548-53f4-4c48-a3e9-06c780738663`
- **参数名**: `DemTimeSeriesSamplingTime`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `1`
- **取值范围**: [0.001..65.535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This configuration value defines the time interval for the periodic collection of the samples.

The value must be a multiple of the DemGeneral/DemTaskTime. Otherwise it is rounded down to the next multiple value of DemTaskTime.

The AUTOSAR configuration standard is to use SI units, so in the EcuC file this parameter is stored as float value in seconds - the DaVinci Configurator 5 GUI will convert the value when using a different unit.

**容器描述**:

Contains configuration options for calibration.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. DemCalibrationAdditionalCounterBasedDebouncingClasses

- **UUID**: `755bc538-eea6-4be0-90f9-332817ebdb65`
- **参数名**: `DemCalibrationAdditionalCounterBasedDebouncingClasses`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter is only relevant if DemGeneral/DemGeneralCalibration/DemCalibrationCounterBasedDebouncingSupport is set to true. 

This parameter specifies the number of DemCounterBasedDebouncingClasses that shall be calibratable in addition the configured and referenced DemConfigSet/DemDebounceCounterBasedClasses.
The values of these additional classes can be calibrated (see DemCalibrationCounterBasedDebouncingSupport) and can be referenced by any event using counter based debouncing via calibration.

####### BOOLEAN 类型参数

######## 1. DemCalibrationCounterBasedDebouncingSupport

- **UUID**: `cb1dbdbc-d03d-47c5-a709-5f7f4a4ac0cd`
- **参数名**: `DemCalibrationCounterBasedDebouncingSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this parameter is set to true, the following parameters of a DemConfigSet/DemDebounceCounterBasedClass can be calibrated: 

- DemDebounceCounterIncrementStepSize
- DemDebounceCounterFailedThreshold
- DemDebounceCounterPassedThreshold
- DemDebounceCounterJumpUp
- DemDebounceCounterJumpUpValue
- DemDebounceCounterJumpDown
- DemDebounceCounterJumpDownValue
- DemCounterBasedFdcThresholdStorageValue
- DemDebounceContinuous
- DemDebounceBehavior
- DemDebounceCounterDecrementStepSize: In contrast to the initially configured value, when calibrating this parameter the modified value needs to be negative, i.e. it is equal to configured value * (-1).

For events with counter based debouncing it is also possible to calibrate which event references which counter based debouncing class. 

Note: To support calibration of counter based debounce classes, optimizations are disabled for the tables containing these classes. This can lead to increased memory consumption.

##### 71. DemDebounceAlgorithmClass

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `6111de60-51d3-4846-9870-b598a5bdcbae`
- **容器类型**: CHOICE-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `DemDebounceAlgorithmClass`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **需要索引**: false

**容器选择**:

- **DemDebounceCounterBased**
  - UUID: `a3cd5d3d-3f7f-4641-9330-1bc37ff8750b`
  - 描述: Counter based debouncing will increment a counter with each Prefailed result, and decrement the counter with each Prepassed result.
A qualified 'Passed' or 'Failed' result will be generated when a given threshold value is reached.

This debouncing type is ideal for cyclic monitors that generate a monitoring result regularly since it involves little overhead.

The debounce value is mapped to a faultdetection count (FDC) as such:
If the Failed threshould is reached, FDC is 127.
If the DebounceCounter is 0, FDC is 0.
If the Passed threshold is reached, FDC is -128.
Values between 0 and the threshold values are linearly mapped to 1..126 respectively -1..-127.
- **DemDebounceMonitorInternal**
  - UUID: `5eb9ae34-0ec8-4eba-a682-38442523bf19`
  - 描述: Monitor internal debouncing is used for monitors that implement the debouncing themselves, and only report qualified results (PASSED/FAILED) to the Dem.

In order to implement the diagnostic services that report the fault detection counter, a callout function is required.
- **DemCallbackGetFDC**
  - UUID: `a19cf877-d405-47de-b578-58268ab4493c`
  - 描述: This container sets up the callout function 'DemCallbackGetFDC' for this Event. The Dem will call this function each time the value of the fault detection counter is needed, e.g. requested by the Dcm.

This function is usually called through a RTE port interface.
This behavior can be changed by creating the sub-parameter 'DemCallbackGetFDCFnc'.
- **DemDebounceTimeBased**
  - UUID: `bc30101f-b22d-4931-9e37-03f45638a955`
  - 描述: Time based debouncing will use a timer started in either passed or failed direction with an unqualified result.
The timer is not restarted in case it is already running, unless a direction change is detected - e.g. after a PrePassed result the next PrePassed result will not restart the timer, a following PreFailed will stop the passed timer and start the failed timer.

Timer supervision incurs a substantial runtime and RAM overhead compared to counter based denouncing and should be used sparingly.

The debounce value is mapped to a faultdetection count as such:
If the Failed time is reached, FDC is 127.
If no timer is running (and no qualified result has been reached), FDC is 0.
If the Passed time is reached, FDC is -128.
If the timer is currently running in either direction, the timer is linearly mapped to 1..126 respectively -1..-127.

**容器描述**:

Select the debouncing algorithm used for the event.
